@q dc1-14 | al-dc#1 | 1 | 計算
合併排序 100 萬筆資料，遞迴切到只剩 1 筆，大約有幾層？
== 20
---
每層對半切，$\lceil\lg10^6\rceil=20$。每層合計 $O(n)$，所以 $O(n\lg n)$。

@q dc1-15 | al-dc#1 | 3 | 計算
同時找 8 個數的最大值和最小值，用「兩兩一組先比、大的跟目前最大比、小的跟目前最小比」，最少要比幾次？
== 10
---
$3n/2-2$：先比 4 組（4 次）；第一組直接當初始最大、最小，其餘 3 組各要 2 次（6 次）。分開找最大、最小要 $2(n-1)=14$ 次。

@q dc1-16 | al-dc#1 | 1 | 計算
在 1000 個排好的數中二分搜尋，最壞要看幾次中間值？
== 10
---
每次剩一半，$\lceil\lg1001\rceil=10$。

@q dc1-17 | al-dc#1 | 2 | 找錯
A student says: "Computing Fibonacci numbers by the recursion $F(n)=F(n-1)+F(n-2)$ is also divide and conquer, so it takes $O(n\lg n)$ time." The student's program is
```
fib(n): if n <= 1: return n
        return fib(n - 1) + fib(n - 2)
```
and it takes about 1 second for $n = 35$ and about 2 minutes for $n = 45$. The teaching assistant asks the student to explain the timings and to compare this recursion with true divide and conquer, such as merge sort, where the two halves share no elements. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
?? 複選
[x] The two subproblems overlap heavily, so the plain recursion takes exponential time.
[ ] The student's $O(n\lg n)$ claim is correct, since each call splits the problem in two.
[ ] Fibonacci numbers cannot be computed by recursion at all.
[ ] The plain recursion takes $O(n)$ time, since each $F(k)$ is computed only once.
[ ] The subproblems $F(n-1)$ and $F(n-2)$ are independent, so nothing is recomputed.
---
$F(n-1)$ 自己又要算 $F(n-2)$，「子問題大量重疊」，呼叫次數和 $F(n)$ 同級，「指數時間」。同一個 $F(k)$ 會被算很多次；遞迴可以算，只是慢；重疊的子問題要記下答案重用（之後學的動態規劃）。

@q dc1-18 | al-dc#1 | 2 | 情境
A search company counts how often each word appears in $10^9$ web pages, using 1000 computers. Each computer counts the words in its own share of the pages, and then the partial counts for each word are added up at the end. An engineer describes the method in a design review and compares it with other approaches from the textbook, such as making one locally best choice per step or storing the answers of repeated subproblems in a table. The method finishes in about 20 minutes, and no computer needs another computer's partial counts until the end. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] It splits, solves independent parts, and combines.
[x] In MapReduce terms, "map" solves the parts and "reduce" combines them.
[ ] This picks the locally best option at each step and never reconsiders it.
[ ] This works only because overlapping subproblem results are stored in a table.
[ ] This tries every possible way of dividing the documents and keeps the best.
---
「切成獨立子問題、各自解、再合併」正是分治；「map 各自解、reduce 合併」。沒有「每步挑最好」的選擇；子問題不重疊，不需要查表；也沒有窮舉所有切法。

@q dc1-19 | al-dc#1 | 3 | 計算
A majority element of an array of $n$ items is one that occurs more than $n/2$ times; for example, in $[3, 1, 3, 3, 2, 3, 3, 1]$ the value 3 occurs 5 times out of 8. A divide-and-conquer algorithm splits the array into two halves, finds the majority candidate of each half recursively, and then counts how often each of the two candidates occurs in the whole array, in linear time, returning the one that occurs more than $n/2$ times, if any. A student writes the recurrence, solves it, and tries to justify why the overall majority must be returned by at least one half. The student also compares the method with a single-pass voting method that keeps one candidate and a counter, and asks whether the majority must also be a majority of both halves. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The recurrence is $T(n)=2T(n/2)+O(n)$, so the time is $O(n\lg n)$.
[x] An overall majority is a majority in some half.
[x] It need not be a majority element of both halves.
[x] A single-pass voting method finds it in $O(n)$ time.
[ ] This divide-and-conquer version runs in $O(n)$ time.
---
合併時線性數一次，「$O(n\lg n)$」，不是線性。若某值在兩半都不過半，加起來也不過半，所以「整體多數一定是某一半的多數」，但「不必兩半都是」：[1,1,1,1,2,2,2] 切成 [1,1,1] 和 [1,2,2,2]。「掃一遍的投票法」（Boyer–Moore）是 $O(n)$。

@q dc1-20 | al-dc#1 | 3 | 比較
A sorting method always splits its data into two parts of sizes $n/10$ and $9n/10$, recurses on both parts, and spends $O(n)$ time on the splitting, so its running time satisfies $T(n) = T(n/10) + T(9n/10) + O(n)$. A student draws the calls as a tree: every level of the tree does at most $O(n)$ total work, the shortest route from the top to a base case has about $\log_{10} n$ levels, and the longest has about $\log_{10/9} n$ levels, which for $n = 10^6$ is about 131. The student wants the running time and the depth of the recursion, and compares this split with one that always produces parts of sizes 1 and $n - 1$, which a careless splitting rule can produce on already sorted input. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
?? 複選
[x] Its running time is $O(n\lg n)$.
[ ] Its running time is $O(n^2)$, since the split is unbalanced.
[ ] Its running time is $O(n)$, since most of the work goes to the small part.
[ ] Its recursion depth is about $n/10$.
[ ] Splitting into sizes $1$ and $n-1$ every time would also give $O(n\lg n)$.
---
最長的路每次乘 $9/10$，深度 $\log_{10/9}n=O(\lg n)$，每層合起來不超過 $n$，「$O(n\lg n)$」。只要切的比例是固定常數，層數就是對數級；切成 $1$ 和 $n-1$ 才有 $n$ 層、變成 $n^2$。

@q dc2-11 | al-dc#2 | 2 | 計算
Karatsuba 算 $1234\times5678$，拆成 $12|34$ 和 $56|78$。中間項 $(a+b)(c+d)-ac-bd$ 是多少？
== 2840
---
$ac=672$、$bd=2652$、$(12+34)(56+78)=46\times134=6164$，$6164-672-2652=2840$。結果 $672\cdot10^4+2840\cdot10^2+2652=7006652$，跟直接乘相同。

@q dc2-12 | al-dc#2 | 3 | 計算
Toom-3 把每個數切成三段，用 5 次乘法完成。遞迴式 $T(n)=5T(n/3)+O(n)$ 的時間指數約是多少？
== 1.465
== 1.46
---
$\log_35\approx1.465$，比 Karatsuba 的 $\log_23\approx1.585$ 小，但常數更大。

@q dc2-13 | al-dc#2 | 2 | 計算
Strassen 的遞迴式 $T(n)=7T(n/2)+\Theta(n^2)$，時間 $n^c$ 的 $c$ 約是？
== 2.81
---
$\log_27\approx2.807$。加法的 $n^2$ 被乘法的部分蓋過。

@q dc2-14 | al-dc#2 | 3 | 計算
Strassen 每一層把 8 次分塊乘法省成 7 次，但要多做幾次 $n/2\times n/2$ 的矩陣加減？
== 18
---
7 個乘積的準備和組合共 18 次加減（一般方法只要 4 次加法）。所以 $n$ 小時 Strassen 反而慢，實作會在小於某個大小時改用三層迴圈。

@q dc2-15 | al-dc#2 | 3 | 陷阱
In Karatsuba multiplication, the sum $a+b$ used in $(a+b)(c+d)$ may have one more digit than $a$ (for example $a=b=99$). Which of the following statements are correct?
[x] This does not break the running-time analysis.
[x] Size $n/2+1$ still gives $\Theta(n^{\lg3})$.
[ ] The running time becomes $\Theta(n^2)$, since the subproblems no longer halve.
[ ] The algorithm becomes incorrect, since the extra digit is lost.
[ ] Four multiplications must then be used instead of three.
---
多一位只讓子問題變成「$n/2+1$」，$3T(n/2+1)+O(n)$ 的解「仍是 $\Theta(n^{\lg3})$」，「不影響分析」。結果照樣正確，也不必改回 4 次乘法；這種細節只要寫程式時處理。

@q dc2-16 | al-dc#2 | 2 | 找錯
A student's "divide-and-conquer multiplication" splits each number into halves and recursively computes the four products $ac$, $ad$, $bc$, $bd$. He claims it is faster than direct multiplication. Which of the following statements are correct?
[x] Its recurrence is $T(n)=4T(n/2)+O(n)$.
[x] It runs in $\Theta(n^2)$ time, no better than direct multiplication.
[x] Reducing the four recursive products to three is what gives an improvement.
[x] Splitting by itself does not make an algorithm faster.
[ ] It is faster than direct multiplication, since each product is only half as long.
---
「$4T(n/2)+O(n)$」，$\log_24=2$，「仍是 $\Theta(n^2)$」：子問題變小了，但數量變 4 倍，抵消了。「光是切開不會變快」，要「省成 3 次」（Karatsuba）才有改善。

同樣遞迴 10 層（$n=2^{10}$），每層 7 個子問題和 8 個子問題，最底層的乘法次數比約是多少倍（8 的比 7 的）？
== 3.8
== 3.76
---
$(8/7)^{10}\approx3.8$。每層省一點，層數多時累積成可觀的差距。

@q dc2-18 | al-dc#2 | 2 | 比較
Which of the following are genuine reasons why Strassen's algorithm is not always used in practice?
?? 複選
[x] Its large constant factors make it slower for small matrices.
[ ] Fewer multiplications guarantee that every result computed with rounding is more accurate than ordinary multiplication.
[ ] Strassen multiplication never needs to store any intermediate sums or products.
[ ] Its result is mathematically wrong.
[ ] It works only when $n$ is a prime number.
---
「常數大使小矩陣較慢」對：額外加減與遞迴管理會讓漸近指數優勢尚未出現。「乘法少就必更準」錯：加減可有消去與誤差放大，操作數少不保證浮點精度。「絕無中間值」錯：七個子乘積及加減組合仍有中間結果；可重用儲存，但不是完全不需保存。「數學上錯誤」錯：精確算術下代數恆等式成立。「只適用質數大小」錯：標準對半遞迴適合二的冪，其他大小可補零。

@q dc2-19 | al-dc#2 | 2 | 情境
A cryptography program multiplies two integers of about 600 decimal digits. Which of the following statements about how big-integer libraries choose the multiplication algorithm are correct?
[x] They pick the method by the length of the numbers.
[x] Asymptotically faster methods have larger constant factors, so they pay off only for large enough inputs.
[ ] They always use schoolbook multiplication, since it has the smallest constant factor.
[ ] They always use the most advanced method available, since it is asymptotically fastest.
[ ] They always use Karatsuba, whatever the size of the numbers.
---
漸近好的方法常數大、額外的加減和記憶體多，「只有數字夠長才划算」，所以「依長度切換」。三個「always」都忽略了大小的影響。

@q dc2-20 | al-dc#2 | 3 | 證明
寫出 Karatsuba 的三個乘積，並說明怎麼從它們得到 $xy$（$x=a\cdot10^m+b$、$y=c\cdot10^m+d$）。
---
$xy=ac\cdot10^{2m}+(ad+bc)\cdot10^m+bd$。只算 $P_1=ac$、$P_2=bd$、$P_3=(a+b)(c+d)=ac+ad+bc+bd$，則 $ad+bc=P_3-P_1-P_2$。三次乘法加上幾次加減和位移就得到結果，所以 $T(n)=3T(n/2)+O(n)$。

== √5
== 2.236
---
(5,1)–(6,3) 和 (6,3)–(7,5) 都是 $\sqrt{1+4}$。程式兩兩比對驗算。

@q dc3-09 | al-dc#3 | 2 | 計算
分治後左半最近距離 3、右半 2.5，分割線 $x=40$。帶狀區域的 $x$ 範圍？
== 37.5,42.5
?? 左界,右界
---
$\delta=\min(3,2.5)=2.5$，帶狀是 $[40-\delta,40+\delta]$。帶狀外的點跟另一半的距離一定 $>\delta$。

@q dc3-10 | al-dc#3 | 1 | 觀念
Which of the following statements about the closest pair among $n$ points on a number line are correct?
[x] Sorting and then comparing only adjacent points solves it in $O(n\lg n)$ time.
[x] On a number line, the two closest points are adjacent after sorting.
[x] Divide and conquer is not required on a line.
[x] After sorting, only $n-1$ adjacent pairs need to be checked.
[x] Points in the plane lack this adjacency property.
---
一維時，兩點間若夾著別的點，那個點離其中一點更近，所以「最近的兩點排序後相鄰」，「排序後看 $n-1$ 對相鄰」，「$O(n\lg n)$」，「不需要分治」；「二維沒有這個性質」，才需要帶狀區域。五句都對。

@q dc3-11 | al-dc#3 | 3 | 陷阱
In the closest-pair algorithm, someone compares **every pair** of points inside the strip around the dividing line. Which of the following statements are correct?
?? 複選
[x] It can degrade to $O(n^2)$.
[ ] The algorithm is still $O(n\lg n)$, since the strip is narrow.
[ ] The algorithm becomes $O(n)$.
[ ] The algorithm becomes $O(n\lg^2n)$.
[ ] The strip never contains more than a constant number of points.
---
點全擠在中線附近時「帶狀區域幾乎包含全部點」，每對都比就是 $\Theta(n^2)$。帶子窄不代表點少；要照 $y$ 排序、每點只比後面常數個，合併才是 $O(n)$。

@q dc3-12 | al-dc#3 | 3 | 計算
邊長 $\delta$ 的正方形裡，任兩點距離都 $\ge\delta$，最多能放幾個點？
== 4
---
放在四個角。這就是「帶狀裡每個點只要比常數個」的關鍵：以 $p$ 為底的 $\delta\times2\delta$ 長方形可以拆成兩個 $\delta\times\delta$ 正方形（左右各一），每邊最多 4 點。

@q dc3-13 | al-dc#3 | 2 | 找錯
In the combine step of the closest-pair algorithm, a student checks only pairs whose two points both lie in the **left** half of the strip. Which of the following statements are correct?
?? 複選
[x] The combine step must consider pairs with one point from each recursive half.
[ ] Pairs entirely in the left half were never examined by the left recursive call.
[ ] A smaller left-side minimum than right-side minimum proves that all crossing pairs may be ignored.
[ ] Examining pairs entirely in the right half is sufficient for the combine step.
[ ] The strip can be omitted even when a crossing pair is closer than either within-half minimum.
---
「需補跨兩側點對」對：兩個遞迴呼叫各自只處理一側。「左邊從未檢查」錯：左遞迴已求該半的最近距離。「左側較小就免跨線」錯：跨線距離仍可能更短，例如分割線兩側各有一個很近的點。「只看右內部就足夠」錯：仍漏掉跨線組合。「可完全省帶狀檢查」錯：題設已容許跨線更近，省掉就會漏答案。

@q dc3-14 | al-dc#3 | 2 | 計算
There are $n = 2^{20}$ (about one million) points in the plane, and we want the closest pair. One method compares every pair of points; another uses the divide-and-conquer closest-pair algorithm, which runs in $O(n\lg n)$ time. Count one basic operation per distance computation or comparison. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] Comparing every pair takes about $5.5\times10^{11}$ distance computations.
[x] The divide-and-conquer closest-pair algorithm takes on the order of $2\times10^7$ basic operations.
[ ] Both approaches take about the same amount of work.
[ ] Halving the number of points would cut the work of comparing every pair to about one half.
[ ] The divide-and-conquer algorithm takes about $10^{12}$ basic operations.
---
「每對都比約 $5.5\times10^{11}$」對：$n(n-1)/2\approx2^{39}\approx5.5\times10^{11}$。

「分治約 $2\times10^7$」對：$n\lg n=2^{20}\times20\approx2\times10^7$。

「差不多」錯：差了兩萬多倍。

「點數減半，兩兩比較減半」錯：工作量是平方，減半後只剩約四分之一。

「分治約 $10^{12}$」錯：那是平方的量級，不是 $n\lg n$。

@q dc3-15 | al-dc#3 | 3 | 陷阱
In the closest-pair algorithm, many points share the same $x$ coordinate, which happens to be the dividing line. Which of the following statements are correct?
[x] The points should be split by position in the sorted order (the first $\lfloor n/2\rfloor$ go left), not by $x$ value.
[x] Splitting by $x$ value could leave one side empty, so the recursion might not shrink.
[x] Points with the same $x$ may end up on both sides, and the strip check handles the distances between them.
[x] Points on the dividing line must remain represented in one of the recursive halves rather than being discarded.
[ ] Splitting only by strict x-coordinate comparison always produces two balanced halves, even if all x values are equal.
---
「依排序位置切」對：每個點有固定身份，取前 floor(n/2) 個到左，其餘到右，平手也能平衡。「只按 x 可能空半」對：全部 x 相同時可全部跑到同側，規模不縮小。「同 x 可跨兩半」對：跨側距離由帶狀檢查補上。「分割線上的點不能丟」對：它仍可能是最近點對的一端。「嚴格座標比較總平衡」錯：全相同 x 已是反例。

@q dc3-17 | al-dc#3 | 2 | 情境
An example air-traffic dataset with positions given by two coordinates has exactly 3000 aircraft, and the two closest positions are recomputed each second. Which of the following statements are correct?
?? 複選
[x] Comparing every pair of a few thousand aircraft takes millions of distance computations each second.
[ ] A fixed spatial grid guarantees linear total work for every placement, even if all aircraft fall into the same grid cell.
[ ] The strip method requires comparing every pair of aircraft, including pairs arbitrarily far apart.
[ ] Comparing every pair is always faster.
[ ] These methods work only because aircraft move along a single straight line.
---
「三千架兩兩比百萬級」對：3000×2999/2＝4,498,500 次。「固定格子每次都線性」錯：若所有點落在同格且仍逐對檢查，工作可達平方，需額外分布或佔用條件才可主張接近線性。「帶狀仍比所有遠點」錯：它利用距離界與排序只查必要的近鄰候選。「暴力永遠快」錯：完整逐對是平方，標準平面分治為 n lg n，不能保證暴力較快。「只適用一直線」錯：本題是平面位置，分治最近點對正是平面演算法。

@q dc3-18 | al-dc#3 | 2 | 觀念
In the divide-and-conquer closest-pair algorithm with presorting, so that the combine step takes $O(n)$ time, which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] Its recurrence is $T(n)=2T(n/2)+O(n)$.
[x] It runs in $O(n\lg n)$ time.
[x] Its recurrence has the same form as merge sort's.
[ ] Its combine step must compare every point in the left half with every point in the right half.
[x] Its tight worst-case running time is $\Theta(n\lg n)$.
---
「兩個半大子問題加線性」對：左右皆遞迴，帶狀合併線性。

「$O(n\lg n)$」對、「緊界 $\Theta(n\lg n)$」對：每層 $\Theta(n)$、共 $\lg n$ 層。

「同合併排序形式」對：兩半遞迴加線性工作。

「合併要比左右每一對」錯：只需看分界線兩側寬度為目前最小距離的帶狀區域，而且每個點只要和帶內依 $y$ 排序後的少數幾個鄰居比較；左右全比就變回平方。

@q dc3-19 | al-dc#3 | 3 | 比較
Which of the following statements describe what the divide-and-conquer algorithms for closest pair and merge sort have in common?
[x] Each splits the input into two halves and recurses on them.
[x] In each, the key step handles the part that crosses the two halves in $O(n)$ time.
[x] Each satisfies $T(n)=2T(n/2)+O(n)$ and runs in $O(n\lg n)$ time.
[ ] Each fills a table of subproblem answers.
[x] Each has an $O(n^2)$ upper bound, even though its standard tight worst-case bound is smaller.
---
「各切兩半」對：兩個子問題各求解。「關鍵步驟線性」對：最近點對查跨線候選，排序合併兩段。「兩半加線性給 n lg n」對：標準分析得 Θ(n lg n)。「填重疊子問題表」錯：標準分治不需要這種重用表。「O(n²) 也是上界」對：n lg n＝O(n²)；O 只給上界，較鬆並不等於錯，若聲稱 Θ(n²) 才不成立。

@q dc3-20 | al-dc#3 | 2 | 計算
帶狀區域有 9 個點，照 $y$ 排好後，每點只跟後面最多 7 個點比。最多比幾次？
== 35
---
第 $i$ 點（從 1 起）比 $\min(7,9-i)$ 次：$7,7,6,5,4,3,2,1,0$，和為 35。不管帶狀多少點，比較次數都不超過 $7n$。

@q dc4-09 | al-dc#4 | 2 | 計算
`[3, 7, 2, 8, 1, 6, 4, 5]` 有幾個逆序對？
== 14
---
程式兩兩比對驗算。可以用合併排序一路數：每次從右半拿數時加上左半剩下的個數。

@q dc4-10 | al-dc#4 | 2 | 追蹤
合併 `[2, 5, 8]` 和 `[1, 3, 9]`，跨兩半的逆序對有幾個？
== 5
---
拿 1（右）：左邊剩 3 個 → +3；拿 2（左）；拿 3（右）：左邊剩 5、8 → +2；拿 5、8（左）；拿 9（右）：左邊剩 0。共 5。

@q dc4-11 | al-dc#4 | 1 | 計算
10 個數最多有幾個逆序對？
== 45
---
完全反序時每一對都是逆序：$\binom{10}2$。

@q dc4-12 | al-dc#4 | 2 | 觀念
An array contains $I$ inversions. Which of the following statements are correct?
[x] Insertion sort performs exactly $I$ shifts of one position.
[x] Insertion sort runs in $O(n+I)$ time.
[x] Bubble sort performs exactly $I$ swaps.
[ ] Insertion sort performs exactly $n$ shifts.
[x] A reverse-sorted array of n distinct elements has $n(n-1)/2$ inversions.
---
「插入移位 I 次」對：每個大於目前元素的先前元素恰被右移一次，各對應一個逆序。「時間 O(n＋I)」對：外層線性管理加各次移位。「氣泡交換 I 次」對：只交換相鄰逆序，每次恰少一個，排序後為零。「恰 n 次移位」錯：已排序可零次，反序可平方次。「反序 n(n−1)/2」對：每個先後位置對都逆序，總組合數如此。

# b-al4.txt

@q dc4-13 | al-dc#4 | 3 | 陷阱
A "significant inversion" is a pair $i<j$ with $a_i>2a_j$. Which of the following statements about finding how many there are by divide and conquer are correct?
[x] The comparisons made while merging cannot be reused directly to find this number.
[x] Before merging, a separate two-pointer scan over the two sorted halves can find the number of pairs with $a_i>2a_j$ across them.
[x] The total time stays $O(n\lg n)$.
[x] The extra cross-half scan takes linear time because both pointers move only forward.
[x] The $O(n\lg n)$ method also has a looser $O(n^2)$ upper bound.
---
「一般合併比較不能直接當答案」對：aᵢ>aⱼ 與 aᵢ>2aⱼ 的門檻不同。「先用兩指標數跨側」對：排序後門檻單調，各左元素累計右半符合者數量。「總 n lg n」對：每層兩個線性掃描仍為線性。「額外掃描線性」對：兩指標各自只向前，移動總數受兩半長度和限制。「平方鬆上界」對：n lg n 屬 O(n²)，此上界有效；若聲稱平方是必要緊界才錯。

# b-al4.txt

@q dc4-14 | al-dc#4 | 2 | 情境
A log file is almost sorted by time; only a few records are out of order. Which of the following statements are correct?
[x] Insertion sort runs in $O(n+I)$ time, where $I$ is the number of inversions, so it is nearly linear here.
[x] The number of inversions measures how unsorted the data is.
[ ] Merge sort is always the fastest choice here.
[ ] Quicksort is guaranteed to be the fastest choice here.
[ ] Selection sort also runs in nearly linear time on almost-sorted input.
---
插入排序的移動次數正好是逆序對數 $I$，資料幾乎排好時 $I$ 很小，接近線性，「Insertion sort runs in…」、「The number of inversions…」對。合併排序不管資料多整齊都是 $\Theta(n\lg n)$；快速排序選第一個當基準時，排好的資料反而最慢；選擇排序永遠 $\Theta(n^2)$，「Merge sort is always…」、「Quicksort is guaranteed to…」、「Selection sort also runs…」錯。

# b-al4.txt

@q dc4-15 | al-dc#4 | 3 | 找錯
合併時遇到左右相等的數，學生先拿右邊的，並把「左邊剩幾個」加進逆序對。`[2]` 和 `[2]` 會算出幾個？正確應該是幾個？
== 1,0
?? 學生,正確
---
相等不是逆序。相等時要先拿左邊的（也讓合併排序穩定），才不會多算。

@q dc4-16 | al-dc#4 | 2 | 計算
1、2、…、10 的所有 $10!$ 種排列，平均每種有幾個逆序對？
== 22.5
---
每一對 $(i,j)$ 在一半的排列中是逆序，$\binom{10}2/2=22.5$。所以插入排序平均也是 $\Theta(n^2)$。

@q dc4-17 | al-dc#4 | 3 | 證明
證明：交換陣列中**相鄰**的兩個數，逆序對數剛好增加或減少 1。
---
設交換位置 $k$、$k+1$ 的兩數 $x$、$y$。任何一對中只要有一個不是這兩個位置，它們的相對順序不變（另一個數對 $x$、$y$ 的前後關係不變）。唯一改變的是 $(x,y)$ 這一對：原本是逆序就變成不是，反之亦然。所以總數剛好 ±1。這也說明「只能交換相鄰」的排序至少要做 $I$ 次交換。

@q dc4-18 | al-dc#4 | 1 | 計算
`[6, 5, 4, 3, 2, 1]` 有幾個逆序對？
== 15
---
完全反序，$\binom62$。

@q dc4-19 | al-dc#4 | 2 | 比較
Inversions in an array of $n=10^5$ numbers are counted either with merge sort or by comparing every pair. An inversion is a pair of positions $i<j$ whose values are out of order. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The merge-sort method uses about $1.7\times10^6$ comparisons.
[x] Comparing every pair uses about $5\times10^9$ comparisons.
[ ] Both methods use about the same number of comparisons.
[ ] The merge-sort method must also compare every pair to find the inversions that cross the middle.
[ ] An array sorted in increasing order has about $5\times10^9$ inversions.
---
「合併排序約 $1.7\times10^6$」對：$n\lg n\approx10^5\times17$。

「每對都比約 $5\times10^9$」對：$n(n-1)/2=4{,}999{,}950{,}000$。

「差不多」錯：差了約 3000 倍。

「跨中間的也要逐對比」錯：合併時右段的元素被輸出，左段還剩幾個就一次加幾個逆序對，不用逐對比較。

「遞增陣列約 $5\times10^9$ 個逆序對」錯：遞增陣列一個逆序對都沒有；遞**減**的才有 $n(n-1)/2$ 個。

@q dc5-09 | al-dc#5 | 2 | 計算
長度 4 的序列 (1,1,1,1) 做 DFT（在 4 個 4 次單位根求值），結果是？
== 4 0 0 0
== 4,0,0,0
---
在 $x=1$ 的值是 4；其他單位根 $\omega$ 有 $1+\omega+\omega^2+\omega^3=0$。程式用 numpy 驗算。

@q dc5-10 | al-dc#5 | 2 | 計算
(1,0,0,0) 的 DFT？
== 1 1 1 1
== 1,1,1,1
---
多項式就是常數 1，在任何點的值都是 1。

@q dc5-11 | al-dc#5 | 3 | 計算
$p(x)=1+x$。在 $1,-1,i,-i$ 求 $p(x)^2$ 的值（點值表示法相乘）？
== 4 0 2i -2i
== 4,0,2i,-2i
---
先求 $p$：$2,0,1+i,1-i$，逐點平方：$4,0,2i,-2i$。內插回去是 $1+2x+x^2$。

@q dc5-12 | al-dc#5 | 2 | 觀念
Use the usual unnormalized forward transform and radix-two FFT, with $n$ a power of two at least four. Which of the following statements about the inverse FFT (converting point values back to coefficients) are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] It uses $\omega^{-1}$ in place of $\omega$.
[ ] Each result must be multiplied by $n$ at the end to recover the original coefficients.
[ ] The radix-two inverse transform has tight running time $\Theta(n^2)$.
[ ] It requires solving an $n\times n$ linear system in $O(n^3)$ time.
[x] It can reuse the same butterfly structure as the forward transform.
---
「改用逆根」對：逆轉換將單位根換為其倒數。

「最後乘 $n$」錯：未正規化的反向轉換會帶出 $n$ 倍，要除以 $n$。

「平方緊界」錯：同樣兩半遞迴加線性合併，為 $\Theta(n\lg n)$。

「必解一般立方聯立」錯：可用根的結構快速計算，不必一般消去。

「可沿用同樣的蝴蝶結構」對：只是把 $\omega$ 換成 $\omega^{-1}$，最後再除以 $n$，程式幾乎一樣。

@q dc5-13 | al-dc#5 | 2 | 計算
長度 8 的 FFT（迭代版），共要做幾次蝴蝶運算（一次乘法＋一加一減）？
== 12
---
$\lg8=3$ 層，每層 $n/2=4$ 個：$12$。一般 $\frac n2\lg n$。

@q dc5-14 | al-dc#5 | 3 | 計算
8 個 8 次單位根各自平方後，得到幾個不同的值？
== 4
---
平方後是 4 次單位根，每個出現兩次（$\omega^k$ 和 $\omega^{k+4}$ 平方相同）。這個「對半」性質讓 FFT 的子問題只要一半的點。

@q dc5-15 | al-dc#5 | 2 | 比較
Use exactly $2n-1$ common distinct evaluation points for the point-value representation. A polynomial of degree less than $n$ can be represented by its coefficients or by its values at enough points. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] Addition takes $O(n)$ time in both representations.
[x] Multiplication takes $O(n^2)$ time when done directly on coefficients.
[x] Multiplication takes $O(n)$ time in point-value form, provided enough points are used.
[x] Addition in point-value form is entrywise and takes time linear in the number of stored evaluation points.
[ ] Converting from coefficients to point values by evaluating each of the $2n-1$ points separately takes $O(n)$ time.
---
「兩表示加法線性」對：係數對係數、相同評估點對相同點值相加。

「直接係數乘平方」對：兩個各 $n$ 項直接做 $n^2$ 個乘積。

「點值乘線性」對：題設 $2n-1$ 個共同相異點，每點乘一次，足以唯一表示次數至多 $2n-2$ 的乘積。

「點值加法逐格線性」對：同一組點逐格加即可。

「逐點求值轉成點值只要 $O(n)$」錯：每個點用 Horner 法要 $O(n)$，$2n-1$ 個點共 $O(n^2)$；要靠 FFT 才能降到 $O(n\lg n)$。

@q dc5-16 | al-dc#5 | 2 | 情境
Pad to $P=2^{20}$, sufficient for the output length. For one transform use the comparison proxy $P\lg P$; it is not a measured total instruction count.
Audio processing convolves a signal of $10^6$ samples with a filter of 1000 samples. Which of the following statements explain why FFT is commonly used?
?? 複選
[x] Convolution is the same computation as polynomial multiplication.
[ ] Direct convolution needs only about one hundred million multiplications for these lengths.
[ ] The specified single-transform proxy at $P=2^{20}$ equals $10P$.
[ ] FFT is used because it gives more accurate results.
[ ] Convolution cannot be computed directly.
---
「摺積等多項式乘法」對：各輸出係數就是對應下標相加之項乘積的總和。「直接一億乘法」錯：10⁶×1000＝10⁹，是十億。「單次代理成本 10P」錯：P＝2²⁰ 的二進位對數為二十，代理值 20P＝20,971,520；完整摺積還含多次轉換與逐點乘法，不能把此代理當總指令數。「FFT 保證更準」錯：浮點仍有捨入誤差。「直接算不了」錯：直接法可正確計算，只是乘法數較多。

# b-al4.txt

@q dc5-17 | al-dc#5 | 2 | 找錯
A student's "FFT" recurses on the even-indexed and odd-indexed coefficients, but in the combine step evaluates each of the $n$ points by direct substitution using exactly n unit-cost arithmetic operations per point. Which of the following statements are correct?
?? 複選
[x] Its recurrence is $T(n)=2T(n/2)+O(n^2)$, giving $O(n^2)$ time.
[ ] The correct combine subtracts the odd contribution: $A(\omega^k)=A_{\text{even}}(\omega^{2k})-\omega^kA_{\text{odd}}(\omega^{2k})$.
[ ] It still runs in $O(n\lg n)$ time.
[ ] It runs in $O(n)$ time.
[ ] Its tight worst-case time is $\Theta(n^3)$.
---
「兩半加平方為平方上界」對：每個合併層由 n 次直接代入各做 n 單位運算，遞迴為 2T(n/2)＋Θ(n²)，合計 Θ(n²)。「合併減奇項」錯：在指定的 ω^k 處應加上 ω^k 乘奇次部分；負號對應另一個評估點，不可原式直接替換。「仍 n lg n」錯：根合併本身已平方。「線性」錯：同一平方下界已否定。「立方緊界」錯：平方是緊界；O(n³) 雖是較鬆上界，Θ(n³) 並不成立。

@q dc5-18 | al-dc#5 | 3 | 計算
$\omega_8=e^{2\pi i/8}$。$\omega_8^3$ 的實部和虛部？
== -0.707,0.707
== -√2/2,√2/2
?? 實部,虛部
---
角度 $3\times45°=135°$：$(\cos135°,\sin135°)$。程式驗算。

@q dc5-19 | al-dc#5 | 3 | 證明
說明為什麼 $n$ 次單位根（$n$ 偶數）滿足 $\omega_n^{k+n/2}=-\omega_n^k$，以及這在 FFT 中的用途。
---
$\omega_n^{n/2}=e^{\pi i}=-1$，所以 $\omega_n^{k+n/2}=\omega_n^k\cdot(-1)$。FFT 合併時 $A(\omega^k)=E+\omega^kO$、$A(\omega^{k+n/2})=E-\omega^kO$（$E$、$O$ 是偶、奇部分在 $\omega^{2k}$ 的值），一次乘法同時得到兩個點的值，這就是蝴蝶運算。

@q dp1-13 | al-dp#1 | 2 | 計算
$4\times4$ 的格子從左上走到右下，每步往右或往下，但第 2 列第 2 行（從 1 數）那格不能走。有幾條路？
== 8
---
DP：每格＝上方＋左方，障礙格填 0。驗算：沒障礙時 $\binom63=20$；經過障礙格的 $2\times6=12$ 條，$20-12=8$。

== 44
---
$f(n)=f(n-1)+f(n-2)+f(n-3)$，$f(0)=1$、$f(1)=1$、$f(2)=2$：4、7、13、24、44。

@q dp1-15 | al-dp#1 | 2 | 計算

== 10

@q dp1-16 | al-dp#1 | 2 | 計算

== 14

@q dp1-17 | al-dp#1 | 3 | 比較
一個 DP 有很多子問題，但對某個特定輸入只有一小部分真的會用到。記憶化（由上而下）和填表（由下而上）哪個比較省？
[x] 記憶化：只算真的被呼叫到的子問題；填表會把所有子問題都算一遍
[ ] 填表一定比較省
[ ] 一樣
[ ] 都不能用
---
反過來，大部分子問題都要用到時，填表沒有遞迴呼叫的負擔，通常比較快。

@q dp1-18 | al-dp#1 | 2 | 找錯
The dictionary keys contain only lengths, the dictionary is retained between runs, and each input price list remains unchanged during its run.
A student memoizes subproblem answers in a global dictionary and then solves two **different** inputs in a row (for example, two different price lists); the second answer is wrong. For example, the first run uses the prices `[1, 5, 8, 9]` for lengths 1 to 4, and the second uses `[2, 4, 9, 10]`. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
?? 複選
[ ] The retained keys already include a distinct immutable identifier for each full input, so cross-input reuse is impossible under the stated setup.
[ ] Clearing the dictionary only after returning the second answer retroactively corrects that returned answer.
[ ] Memoization cannot be done with a dictionary; an array is required.
[x] Including an identifier of the input in every key also fixes the bug.
[ ] The bug happens because the recursion is too deep.
---
「鍵已區分輸入」錯：題設只用長度當鍵，不同價目表會錯用同一答案。「第二次回傳後才清就能補正」錯：錯值已被回傳，清表只能保護後續計算。「不能用字典」錯：問題是鍵缺資訊，容器本身可用。「加唯一不變輸入識別」對：不同輸入狀態不再互撞，亦可每次求解前清表。「必是遞迴太深」錯：本例明確是跨輸入快取污染，與呼叫深度無必然關係。

@q dp2-17 | al-dp#2 | 3 | 陷阱

@q dp2-19 | al-dp#2 | 2 | 計算
用 1、2、5 元硬幣湊 10 元，不計順序有幾種湊法？
== 10
---
外層迴圈跑硬幣、內層跑金額：`ways[s] += ways[s-c]`。若外層跑金額、內層跑硬幣，算的是**有順序**的方法數（會更多）。

@q dp2-20 | al-dp#2 | 3 | 比較
We count the ways to make amount 4 from coins $\{1,2\}$ with an array $w$ where $w[0]=1$. Version 1 loops over coins on the outside: for each coin $c$, for $a=c,\dots,4$, do $w[a]\mathrel{+}=w[a-c]$. Version 2 loops over amounts on the outside: for $a=1,\dots,4$, for each coin $c\le a$, do $w[a]\mathrel{+}=w[a-c]$. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] Version 1 gives $w[4]=3$.
[x] Version 2 gives $w[4]=5$.
[ ] Both versions give $w[4]=3$.
[x] Version 2 counts $1+2+1$ and $2+1+1$ as different ways.
[x] Version one gives two unordered ways to make amount three: three ones, or one one plus one two.
---
「幣外圈四元三種」對：1111、112、22，不計順序。「金額外圈四元五種」對：依最後一枚累加，序列 1、1、2、3、5。「兩版都是三」錯：第二版數的是有順序序列。「121 與 211 分開」對：在金額外圈的遞迴樹是不同選擇路徑。「第一版三元兩組」對：111 與 12；12、21 視為同組，迴圈順序決定了計數語意。

$3+3+5$（重 2+2+3）。一維時容量**由小到大**更新，讓同一件可以重複拿。

@q dp3-14 | al-dp#3 | 3 | 計算
A biology student compares the DNA fragments GATTACA and TAGACCA, both of length 7, by filling the usual LCS table, in which $c[i][j]$ is the LCS length of the first $i$ letters of the first string and the first $j$ letters of the second. The student then backtracks from $c[7][7]$ to report one longest common subsequence, while a classmate, who breaks ties differently during the backtracking, reports a different one. They argue about whose answer is right and about how many letters the two fragments share. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
?? 複選
[ ] Their longest common subsequence has length five.
[ ] Both displayed strings contain exactly two occurrences of $\mathrm C$.
[x] TACA is a longest common subsequence of them.
[ ] GCCA is a longest common subsequence of them.
[ ] Two strings always have exactly one longest common subsequence.
---
「長五」錯：完整 LCS 表末格四。「兩串各兩個該字元」錯：GATTACA 只有一個，TAGACCA 有兩個。「TACA 是最長」對：兩串都能依序取出，長四與表的最佳值一致。「GCCA 是」錯：第一串缺第二個所需字元，不是共同子序列。「LCS 必唯一」錯：此例可取 AACA、GACA、TACA，皆長四。

@q dp3-15 | al-dp#3 | 3 | 觀念
A genome lab must compute both the edit distance and an optimal alignment of two DNA strings of lengths $m$ and $n$, both around $10^6$, where insertions, deletions, and substitutions each cost 1. The usual dynamic program fills a table $d[i][j]$ of size $(m+1)\times(n+1)$, in which each entry depends only on the entries above it, to its left, and diagonally above-left. The lab's server has 64 GB of memory, and each table entry needs $4\ \mathrm{B}$. One engineer proposes keeping only two rows of the table; another proposes Hirschberg's method, which finds where an optimal alignment crosses the middle row and then recurses on the two halves; a third suggests simply choosing the locally cheapest edit at each step. The lab must pick a method that fits in memory and still produces the alignment. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] Keeping only two rows of the DP table gives the edit distance in $O(m+n)$ space.
[ ] With only two rows kept, an optimal alignment can still be recovered by tracing back through the table.
[x] Hirschberg's method recovers an optimal alignment in $O(m+n)$ space and $O(mn)$ time.
[ ] Storing the full table for $m=n=10^6$ needs only about $10^6$ cells.
[ ] Repeatedly choosing the locally cheapest edit at each step gives an optimal alignment.
---
「兩列就能算出距離」對：每一列只依賴上一列。

「兩列也能倒推」錯：倒推要整張表，前面的列已經丟掉了。

「Hirschberg：切中間、遞迴兩半」對：前半正著算、後半反著算，找出最佳切點；每層總工作量約減半，時間仍是 $O(mn)$。這是 DP 加上「切兩半遞迴」的經典組合，生物序列比對常用。

「整張表約 $10^6$ 格」錯：是 $(m+1)(n+1)\approx10^{12}$ 格，存不下。

「每步挑當下最便宜的」錯：貪婪會被前面的選擇卡住，不保證最佳。

@q dp3-16 | al-dp#3 | 2 | 計算
湊硬幣的 DP：面額 1、3、4，`best[6]=2`，而且記錄了 `choice[6]=3`。湊 6 元的硬幣是？
== 3 3
== 3,3
---
`choice[6]=3` 表示拿一枚 3，剩 `best[3]` 的拿法（一枚 3）。重建時沿著 choice 往回走。

@q dp3-17 | al-dp#3 | 2 | 找錯
A knapsack dynamic program is compressed to a one-dimensional array $K[0..L]$, where $L$ is the weight limit, the array starts with all zeros, and every item has a positive integer weight. For each item of weight $w$ and value $v$, it applies the update below to some values of $c$:
```
K[c] = max(K[c], K[c - w] + v)
```
In one version, $c$ runs from $L$ down to $w$; in the other, from $w$ up to $L$. A student tests both versions with a single item of weight 2 and value 3 and limit $L = 4$, and writes down $K[0..4]$ after the update. The student wants to know which version solves the 0/1 knapsack problem, in which each item may be used at most once, and which solves the unbounded version, in which an item may be used any number of times. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] Updating $c$ from large to small computes the 0/1 knapsack answer.
[x] Updating $c$ from small to large lets an item be used many times, which computes the unbounded knapsack answer.
[x] With positive item weights, descending updates read smaller-weight entries before those entries are changed for the current item.
[x] With one item of weight 2 and value 3 and limit 4, small-to-large gives 6 while large-to-small gives 3.
[ ] Both directions always give the same answer.
---
「遞減是 0/1」對：來源格還未在本輪改過，該件不重用。「遞增容許重用」對：來源格可能已含本件。「正重量保證讀到前輪較小格」對：c−w<c，遞減掃描尚未抵達該來源格。「範例六與三」對：遞增二變三後四用它得六，遞減先更新四時二仍零，只得三。「方向永不影響」錯：這個範例直接否定。

@q dp3-18 | al-dp#3 | 2 | 觀念
In the matrix-chain dynamic program for a chain of $n$ matrices, the entry $m[i][j]$ is the minimum number of scalar multiplications needed to multiply matrices $i$ through $j$, and $s[i][j]$ records a split point $k$ that achieves it. The table is filled in order of increasing chain length, and $m[i][j]$ is computed by trying every $k$ from $i$ to $j - 1$. A program that only needs the minimum cost of multiplying a chain of 500 matrices, with sizes up to $1000\times1000$, wants to save memory, and a student considers dropping $s$, or keeping only two rows of $m$ as in the length-only LCS computation. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
?? 複選
[x] If only the minimum number of multiplications is needed, the table $s$ is not needed.
[ ] The split table s alone stores the minimum multiplication count for every interval.
[ ] Without $s$, $m[1][n]$ cannot be computed.
[ ] The unmodified matrix-chain recurrence only needs the two most recently computed rows of m, exactly like length-only LCS.
[ ] The DP runs in $O(n^2)$ time.
---
「只要最小次數不用 s」對：成本遞迴僅讀其他 m 值，可不保存選中的切點。「s 單獨存最小成本」錯：它只存位置，乘法數存 m。「沒 s 算不了成本」錯：每個切點候選算出後取最小即可。「原遞迴直接保留兩列」錯：各區間需讀許多不同起點與長度的子區間，不是只依賴前一列。「平方時間」錯：平方個區間各試線性切點，標準填表立方。

@q dp5-20 | al-dp#5 | 3 | 計算
LIS 用 $O(n^2)$ DP 時多存 `parent[i]`（$L[i]$ 是從哪個 $j$ 來的）。`2 6 3 4 1 2 9 5 8` 的 LIS 是哪一個？
== 2 3 4 5 8
== 2,3,4,5,8
---
長度 5，從 $L$ 最大的 8 沿 parent 往回：8←5←4←3←2。程式驗算這組數只有這一個長度 5 的遞增子序列。

@q dp3-20 | al-dp#3 | 2 | 情境
A diff tool compares two text files of about 2000 lines each and must show which lines were deleted and which were inserted, not just how many changes there are; in a typical pair of versions about 30 lines differ, and the answer is needed in under 1 second. It fills the usual dynamic-programming table, in which the entry $d[i][j]$ is the fewest insertions and deletions needed to turn the first $i$ lines of the old file into the first $j$ lines of the new file. A developer asks how to turn the finished table into a list of changes, and whether a simpler rule, pairing each line with the most similar line of the other file, would also give a shortest list. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] Tracing back from the bottom-right entry recovers which lines were kept, deleted, or inserted.
[ ] The edit distance alone is enough to show the changes.
[ ] The DP must be run a second time to find the changes.
[x] Tracing back needs the whole table, or a linear-space method such as Hirschberg's.
[ ] Pairing each line greedily with the most similar line of the other file always gives a shortest list of changes.
---
「從右下角倒推」對：每一步看是從左（新增）、上（刪除）還是左上（保留或替換）來的。

「距離就夠」錯：距離只是一個數字。

「再做一次 DP」錯：倒推就好。

「要整張表或 Hirschberg」對：只存兩列時拿不到對齊。

「貪婪配對最像的」錯：前面配錯會讓後面全部錯開，不保證最短。

@q dp4-09 | al-dp#4 | 2 | 觀念
A family of knapsack inputs has n positive integers bounded by W and encoded in binary. Compare numeric work terms with encoded length rather than with the number's written decimal appearance. For a concrete trace, take n=10 and W=1073741824, whose binary representation has 31 digits. A second family promises W at most n cubed; that promise must be used when classifying its weight-limit-table algorithm. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] An $O(n^2\lg W)$ bound is polynomial in the encoded input length.
[x] The general $O(nW)$ table bound is pseudo-polynomial.
[ ] An $O(n\sqrt W)$ bound guarantees polynomial time in the encoded length without any value promise.
[x] Under the promise $W\le n^3$, the table bound becomes $O(n^4)$.
[x] For fixed n, replacing W by $W^2$ squares the weight-limit factor in the nW operation model.
---
「n平方乘對數」對：n與寫W所需的位數都受輸入長度控制。「一般nW虛擬多項式」對：W可隨位數呈指數成長。「平方根就保證多項式」錯：W約為2的b次方時，平方根仍約2的b/2次方。「立方數值保證」對：代入得n四次方。「固定n平方容量因子」對：從W變W²；不可把固定的物品維度也誤當一起平方。

@q dp4-11 | al-dp#4 | 2 | 計算
A subset-sum solver scans about nS states and has a budget of 10000000000 basic operations. Compare n=50 with targets 1000000 and two to the power 60. Count one operation per table state for this comparison. A second approach enumerates subsets of two 25-item halves and sorts a list to search for complementary sums. Compare the table counts with enumeration and sorting work, while keeping the numbers binary-encoded. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] For $n=50$ and $S=10^6$, it takes about $5\times10^7$ steps, which is feasible.
[x] For $n=50$ and $S=2^{60}$, it takes about $5.8\times10^{19}$ steps, which is infeasible.
[ ] $O(nS)$ is polynomial in the length of the input.
[x] For $S=2^{60}$, splitting the 50 numbers into two halves, listing the $2^{25}$ sums of each half, and searching one sorted list for each sum of the other is far faster than the table.
[ ] Both cases are feasible with the table.
---

@q dp4-12 | al-dp#4 | 3 | 計算

== 1024

$\sqrt{2^{2048}}=2^{1024}$。$O(\sqrt N)$ 看起來不大，但對輸入位元數 $b$ 是 $2^{b/2}$。RSA 的安全就靠這種落差。

[ ] The promise $W\le n^2$ forces the table to take exponential time.

Multiply every weight and the limit by 2 while retaining the same items and values. For a further trial use a common factor of two to the power k instead. Compare the nW leading operation term, the binary digits of scaled quantities and which subsets fit. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The leading table work roughly doubles.
[x] Each positive scaled weight and the limit gain exactly 1 binary digit.
[ ] Scanning all capacities requires the same work because feasibility is unchanged.

@q dp4-15 | al-dp#4 | 3 | 情境

For a trial with n=4 and vmax=100, K is 5; each value is divided by K and rounded down before value-indexed DP. The selected subset is evaluated using original values. Analyze cumulative rounding loss and whether the scaled table guarantees the exact optimum. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] With this scale the returned value is at least $(1-\varepsilon)$ times the optimum.
[x] The method has time polynomial in n and $1/\varepsilon$.
[ ] Rounding down always preserves the exact optimal subset.
[x] At most nK total original value is lost in rounding any chosen subset.
[x] The value-indexed table uses scaled values to control its width.
---
「至少一減epsilon倍」對：先去除不可行單件，vmax≤最佳值，而nK＝epsilon乘vmax。「時間多項式」對：每個縮放值≤n/epsilon，總值≤n²/epsilon。「一定精確」錯：取整會遺失差異。「至多nK」對：每件誤差嚴格小於K，總誤差也受此寬鬆界控制。「用縮放值控制表寬」對。

@q dp4-17 | al-dp#4 | 3 | 比較
Keep the same weight-limit-table implementation and compare two files describing n=10 and W=1000000. One file stores W using 20 binary digits; the other uses 1000000 tally marks. Parsing costs may differ, but after parsing both runs scan the same nW states. Classify the table's work relative to each file's length, and decide whether enlarging an encoding proves a faster algorithm for ordinary binary input. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] With $W$ written in unary, the input length is at least $W$, so $O(nW)$ is polynomial in that input length.
[x] This does not make the problem any easier: for a large $W$, the computation takes exactly as long as before.
[ ] Writing the numbers in unary shows that knapsack can be solved in time polynomial in the length of the usual binary input.
[x] An $O(nW)$ algorithm is called pseudo-polynomial because it is polynomial in the numeric value $W$ but not in the number of bits of $W$.
[ ] A unary encoding of $W$ is shorter than its binary encoding.
---
「寫成一元，$O(nW)$ 對輸入長度是多項式」對：輸入本身就有 $W$ 個符號。

「並沒有變簡單」對：只是把輸入故意寫得很長，讓時間「相對於輸入」看起來是多項式；要算的步驟一點都沒少。

「證明了對二進位輸入是多項式」錯：二進位輸入長度約 $\lg W$，$W=2^{\lg W}$ 仍是指數。

「虛擬多項式的定義」對。

「一元比二進位短」錯：一元要 $W$ 個符號，二進位只要約 $\lg W$ 個，長得多。

@q dp5-11 | al-dp#5 | 2 | 追蹤

@q dp5-13 | al-dp#5 | 2 | 觀念
For the sequence 9,4,7,2,6,1, compute the longest strictly decreasing subsequence by either negating values or reversing their order and then using strict LIS. A separate sequence 1,2,3 tests whether subtracting LIS length from n is a general formula. Values are ordinary integers with no overflow during negation. Compare index order, inequality direction and the time required for these transformations before judging the listed claims. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] Negating every number and then computing a longest strictly increasing subsequence gives the answer.
[x] Reversing the sequence and then computing a longest strictly increasing subsequence also gives the answer.
[ ] The answer equals $n$ minus the length of a longest increasing subsequence.
[x] For the sequence $9, 4, 7, 2, 6, 1$, the answer is 4.
[ ] Unlike the increasing case, this problem cannot be solved in $O(n \lg n)$ time.
---
「取負號再求 LIS」對：$a_i>a_j$ ⇔ $-a_i<-a_j$。

「反過來讀再求 LIS」對：遞減子序列從後面讀就是遞增的。

「$n$ 減 LIS」錯：這個序列 LIS 是 2（例如 4、7），$6-2=4$ 剛好相同只是巧合；例如 $1,2,3$ 的 LIS 是 3、最長遞減是 1，$3-3=0\ne1$。

「$9,4,7,2,6,1$ 的答案是 4」對：例如 $9,7,6,1$ 或 $9,4,2,1$。

「不能 $O(n\lg n)$」錯：轉換之後就是 LIS，同樣 $O(n\lg n)$。

@q dp5-14 | al-dp#5 | 3 | 計算
`5 2 8 6 3 6 9 7` 最少要分成幾個嚴格遞增的子序列（每個數用一次）才能蓋住整個序列？
== 3
---
答案＝最長**不遞增**子序列的長度（Dilworth 定理），例如 8、6、6。貪婪做法：每個數放到結尾小於它的、結尾最大的那一組，沒有就開新組。

@q dp5-15 | al-dp#5 | 2 | 情境
Cards arrive in order 3,1,4,2. Place each on the leftmost pile whose top is at least its value, or start a new rightmost pile if none qualifies. Track only pile tops when comparing with the tail-array LIS algorithm. Then restart with cards 1,2,3,4 to test whether pile count represents increasing or decreasing subsequence length. Use strict subsequences, so equal values would replace an existing eligible top. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] At the end, the number of piles equals the length of a longest strictly increasing subsequence.
[x] The top cards of the piles play the role of the tail array in the $O(n\lg n)$ algorithm.
[ ] The number of piles equals the number of cards.
[ ] The number of piles equals the length of a longest decreasing subsequence.
[x] For the sequence 3, 1, 4, 2, there are 2 piles at the end.

「堆數＝LIS 長度」對：這就是 $O(n\lg n)$ 算法的由來。

@q dp5-16 | al-dp#5 | 3 | 情境
Envelopes $(w, h)$ can be nested when both the width and the height are strictly smaller. To find the largest number of envelopes that can be nested, we sort by width in increasing order, breaking ties by height in decreasing order, and then compute a longest strictly increasing subsequence of the heights. The input may contain up to 100000 envelopes, each with integer sides between 1 and 1000. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] For the envelopes $(5, 4), (6, 4), (6, 7), (2, 3)$, the answer is 3.
[x] For the envelopes $(1, 1), (1, 2), (1, 3)$, breaking ties by increasing height would give the wrong answer 3 instead of 1.
[ ] Breaking ties by increasing height works just as well.
[x] The whole method runs in $O(n \lg n)$ time.
[ ] For the envelopes $(5, 4), (6, 4), (6, 7), (2, 3)$, the answer is 4.
---
「答案 3」對：例如 $(2,3)\subset(5,4)\subset(6,7)$；$(6,4)$ 和 $(6,7)$ 同寬不能互套，$(5,4)$ 和 $(6,4)$ 同高也不能。

「同寬照高度遞增會得到 3」對：排成 $(1,1),(1,2),(1,3)$，高度 1、2、3 是遞增的，LIS 得 3；但三個同寬，一個都套不進另一個，答案是 1。

「同寬照高度遞增也行」錯：如上。同寬時高度遞減，LIS 最多只會選到其中一個，這就是那個排序規則的目的。

「$O(n\lg n)$」對：排序加上 $O(n\lg n)$ 的 LIS。

「答案 4」錯：四個裡至少有兩個同寬，不可能全部套在一起。

@q dp5-17 | al-dp#5 | 1 | 計算
`1 2 3 4 5` 和 `5 4 3 2 1` 的（嚴格）LIS 長度？
== 5,1
---
本身遞增時 LIS 就是全部；完全遞減時只能挑一個。

@q dp5-18 | al-dp#5 | 3 | 比較
Use the sequence 1,3,2,4 with respective positive weights 5,1,4,1. Compare finding the number of index-distinct longest increasing subsequences with maximizing total weight of an increasing subsequence. A quadratic DP may retain a length and count at each ending position; the ordinary tail array retains only minimum ending values. All weights are positive in the weighted recurrence below, and a subsequence must be nonempty. Identify which information each specified representation preserves. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] By also storing, for each $i$, the number of longest increasing subsequences ending at $i$, the $O(n^2)$ DP can count the longest increasing subsequences; for $1, 3, 2, 4$ there are 2 of them.
[x] The $\mathit{tail}$ method keeps only the smallest last element for each length, so it loses the information needed for such extensions.
[ ] The $\mathit{tail}$ method directly gives the number of longest increasing subsequences.
[x] The $O(n^2)$ DP extends easily to a weighted version: $W[i] = w_i + \max\{W[j] : j < i,\ a_j < a_i\}$ (or $w_i$ if no such $j$ exists).
[ ] Neither method can be extended to count the longest increasing subsequences.
---
「$O(n^2)$ DP 可以順便數個數；$1,3,2,4$ 有 2 個」對：最長是 3，有 $1,3,4$ 和 $1,2,4$。每個位置記「長度」和「有幾種」，轉移時長度相同就把個數加起來。

「tail 法丟掉了需要的資訊」對：同一個長度只留最小的結尾，其他結尾和它們的來源都沒了。

「tail 法直接給個數」錯：如上。

「加權版本好擴充」對：把「長度加 1」換成「加上自己的權重」就好。

「兩者都不能擴充」錯：$O(n^2)$ DP 可以；要兼顧速度，還可以用支援區間最大值的樹狀結構加速到 $O(n\lg n)$。

@q dp5-19 | al-dp#5 | 2 | 證明
說明為什麼 LIS 的 $O(n^2)$ DP 中，答案是 $\max_iL[i]$，而不是 $L[n]$。
---
$L[i]$ 定義為「以第 $i$ 個數**結尾**的最長遞增子序列長度」。最長的那一條不一定以最後一個數結尾（例如最後一個數很小），所以要取所有位置的最大值。

@q gd1-09 | al-greedy#1 | 3 | 計算
4 個工作（工時, 權重）：(3,2)、(1,1)、(4,4)、(2,3)。要讓「權重 × 完成時間」的總和最小，依工時／權重由小到大排。最小總和？
== 57
---
比值 1.5、1、1、0.67 → 先 (2,3)，再 (1,1)、(4,4)（比值相同順序不影響），最後 (3,2)。完成時間 2、3、7、10，總和 $3\cdot2+1\cdot3+4\cdot7+2\cdot10=57$。程式窮舉 24 種順序驗算。

@q gd1-10 | al-greedy#1 | 2 | 計算
工作（工時, 截止）：(3,4)、(2,5)、(4,7)、(1,6)，一次做一個。依截止時間由早到晚做，最大延遲（完成 − 截止，取最大）是多少？
== 3
---
順序 (3,4)、(2,5)、(1,6)、(4,7)，完成 3、5、6、10，延遲 −1、0、0、3。最早截止優先可以讓最大延遲最小（交換論證）。

@q gd1-11 | al-greedy#1 | 2 | 計算
區間 (1,4)、(2,5)、(3,7)、(6,9)、(8,10)、(9,12)。最少放幾個點，讓每個區間都至少包含一點？
== 2
---
依右端點排序，每次把點放在還沒被蓋住的區間的右端：放 4（蓋前三個）、放 9（蓋後三個）。

@q gd1-12 | al-greedy#1 | 2 | 情境
汽車油箱可跑 100 公里，加油站在 30、70、110、150、210、260、290 公里處，終點 320。「每次開到油夠時能到的最遠加油站」最少加幾次油？
== 4
---
停 70、150、210、290。交換論證：任何最佳解的第一站換成「能到的最遠站」不會變差。

@q gd1-13 | al-greedy#1 | 2 | 反例
A delivery driver must visit 20 cities and return home, and wants the shortest round trip; this is the traveling salesman problem. The driver's map has a road between every pair of cities, 190 roads in all, each with a known length, and the route must be computed in about 1 second. A simple rule is to always drive next to the nearest unvisited city and to return home at the end. The company wonders how good this rule is, and whether a smarter but still fast rule could always find the shortest tour. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] It does not always give a shortest tour.
[x] Choosing near cities early can force a very long return trip at the end.
[x] No polynomial-time algorithm is known that always finds a shortest tour.
[ ] It always gives a shortest tour.
[ ] It always gives a longest tour.
---
最近鄰居法一開始一直挑近的，最後可能被迫走一條很長的回程，所以不一定最短，「It does not always…」、「Choosing near cities early…」對、「always … shortest」錯；它也不會總是最長。這個問題目前沒有已知的多項式時間精確解法，最近鄰居法只是啟發式，「No polynomial-time algorithm is…」對。

@q gd1-14 | al-greedy#1 | 2 | 比較
Two teams solve the problem of making change for an amount $A$ with the fewest coins. One team uses a greedy rule, always taking the largest coin that fits; it works for the coins 1, 5, 10, and 50, but for the coins 1, 3, and 4 and $A = 6$ it uses 3 coins instead of 2. The other team uses dynamic programming over all amounts from 0 to $A$. A reviewer asks how the two approaches make their choices, how fast they are, and what each needs for its correctness. Which of the following statements comparing how greedy algorithms and dynamic programming make choices are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] A greedy algorithm makes the choice that looks best at each step and never revisits it.
[x] Dynamic programming solves the subproblems for every possible choice and then compares the results.
[x] When a greedy algorithm is correct, it is usually much faster than dynamic programming, but its correctness needs a proof.
[ ] A greedy algorithm tries every possible choice.
[ ] Dynamic programming makes no choices.
---
貪婪每步只做一個看起來最好的選擇、不回頭；DP 把每種選擇的子問題都解完再比，「A greedy algorithm makes…」、「Dynamic programming solves the…」對。貪婪對的時候比 DP 快很多，但要證明「當下最好」不會害到後面，「When a greedy algorithm…」對。

@q gd1-15 | al-greedy#1 | 3 | 找錯
A student must prove that a greedy algorithm for a scheduling problem is correct, and writes this exchange argument: "Suppose an optimal solution does not start with the greedy choice $g$. Replace its first choice by $g$; since $g$ is the best choice right now, the new solution is better." The teaching assistant marks the argument incomplete and points to a test with 6 activities in which the optimal schedule has 3 of them and the greedy schedule also has 3. In the activity-selection problem, for example, replacing the first activity of an optimal schedule by the earliest-finishing activity works only because the new activity finishes no later, so it cannot overlap the rest of the schedule, and the number of activities stays the same. The student must repair the argument and explain what such an exchange step has to establish in general, not just for this example. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The argument must show that the solution after the replacement is still feasible.
[x] The argument must show that the total value does not get worse.
[ ] "$g$ is the best choice right now" by itself proves that the new solution is no worse.
[ ] The argument must show that the new solution is worse.
[ ] The argument must replace every step of the optimal solution at once.
---
換完之後要說明解仍然合法（不違反限制）、總值不會變差，「The argument must show that the solution…」、「The argument must show that the total…」對；「當下最好」不自動推出整體不變差，「"$g$ is the best…」錯。例如活動選擇中，換成最早結束的活動後，要說明它不會跟後面的活動衝突。交換論證只要不變差就夠，不必（也不能）證明變差；一次換一步就好。

@q gd1-16 | al-greedy#1 | 3 | 計算
3 個工作（工時, 權重）＝(1,1)、(2,10)、(3,1)。只看工時由短到長排，和依工時／權重排，加權完成時間總和各是？
== 37,29
?? 只看工時,看比值
---
只看工時：(1,1) 完成 1、(2,10) 完成 3、(3,1) 完成 6，總和 $1+30+6=37$。看比值：(2,10) 0.2 最先、(1,1) 1、(3,1) 3，完成 2、3、6，總和 $20+3+6=29$。權重大的工作要早點做完。

活動 (2,4)、(1,6)、(5,8)、(7,9)、(3,10)、(9,12)、(11,14)、(10,15)、(13,16)。最早結束貪婪選出幾個？哪幾個？
== 4,(2,4) (5,8) (9,12) (13,16)
?? 個數,活動
---
依結束時間：(2,4) → (5,8)（5≥4）→ (9,12)（9≥8）→ (13,16)。程式驗算。

同一組活動全部都要辦，最少要幾間會議室？
== 3

@q gd2-11 | al-greedy#2 | 3 | 計算
加權活動：(0,3,權重 2)、(2,5,權重 10)、(4,7,權重 2)。最早結束貪婪拿到的權重和？最佳是多少？
== 4,10
?? 貪婪,最佳
---
貪婪選 (0,3)、(4,7) 共 4；只選中間那個 10 更好。加權版本要用 DP：依結束時間排序，$d[i]=\max(d[i-1],w_i+d[p(i)])$。

@q gd2-12 | al-greedy#2 | 3 | 反例
For activity selection, consider the rule "always choose the activity with the fewest conflicts with the others." Which of the following statements are correct?
[x] This rule does not always find a maximum set of non-conflicting activities.
[x] The rule "earliest finish first" is guaranteed optimal by an exchange argument.
[ ] This rule always finds a maximum set.
[ ] This rule works whenever the number of activities is even.
[ ] This rule always finds a minimum set.
---
可以構造衝突數最少的活動恰好卡住一個更好的選法，所以「fewest conflicts」不保證最佳，「This rule does not…」對、「always finds a maximum」錯；活動數的奇偶無關，它也不會總是最少。只有「最早結束」（或對稱的「最晚開始」）有交換論證保證最佳，「The rule "earliest finish…」對。

@q gd2-13 | al-greedy#2 | 3 | 觀念
For activity selection, consider the rule "repeatedly choose the compatible activity with the **latest start** time, filling the schedule from the end." Which of the following statements are correct?
[x] This rule also yields a maximum number of activities.
[x] Reversing the time axis turns it into the earliest-finish rule.
[ ] This rule yields the minimum number of activities.
[ ] This rule works only when all activities have the same length.
[ ] Choosing the shortest activity first also always yields a maximum number of activities.
---
對稱性：時間軸反過來，最晚開始就是最早結束，所以一樣最佳，「This rule also yields…」、「Reversing the time axis…」對，也不需要等長、不會變最少。「最短的先選」不一定最佳：(1,4)、(5,8) 和中間短的 (3,6)，先選 (3,6) 只能選 1 個，最佳是 2 個。

@q gd2-14 | al-greedy#2 | 2 | 情境
A sports hall wants to hold as many games as possible in one day, each game having a fixed time slot. The staff always schedule the game that **starts earliest** among the compatible ones. Which of the following statements are correct?
[x] One long game that starts very early can block many short games.
[x] With games (0, 10), (1, 2), (3, 4), this rule holds 1 game, but 2 are possible.
[ ] This rule is always optimal.
[ ] This rule can schedule overlapping games.
[ ] This rule is optimal whenever all games have different start times.
---
反例：(0,10)、(1,2)、(3,4)，最早開始的 (0,10) 佔掉整天，只排到 1 場，最佳排 (1,2)、(3,4) 兩場，「One long game that…」、「With games (0, 10),…」對、「always optimal」錯；這個例子的開始時間都不同，「This rule is optimal…」錯。它只挑相容的比賽，不會重疊。

@q gd2-15 | al-greedy#2 | 2 | 找錯
A student tests whether activity $j$ can follow activity $i$ with `s_j > f_i` (strictly greater). In this problem, activities $(1,3)$ and $(3,5)$ are allowed to run back to back. Which of the following statements are correct?
?? 複選
[ ] The strict test accepts both activities (1,3) and (3,5) as consecutive compatible choices.
[x] If back-to-back activities are permitted, compatibility uses start at least the previous finish.
[ ] Rejecting an equality case can only remove pairs that overlap for a positive duration.
[ ] Changing the test to non-strict makes (1,4) and (3,5) compatible.
[ ] The strict test also accepts an activity whose start is less than the previous finish.
---
「允許接續用不小於結束」對：端點相接而無正長度重疊可選。「嚴格判斷接受兩活動」錯：三大於三為假，所以少選一場。「等號只對正長重疊」錯：一至三與三至五重疊長度零。「改不嚴格就接受一至四與三至五」錯：三仍小於四，照樣不相容。「開始較小也接受」錯：大於條件不會接受小於的值。

@q gd2-16 | al-greedy#2 | 3 | 證明
證明：最少會議室數等於「同一時間最多有幾個活動在進行」。
---
下界：同一時刻有 $k$ 個活動在進行，它們兩兩重疊，至少要 $k$ 間。上界：依開始時間處理，每個活動到來時若有空出來的房間就用，否則開新房間。開第 $k$ 間時，前 $k-1$ 間都被跟它重疊的活動佔著，加上它自己有 $k$ 個同時進行，所以開的房間數不超過最大重疊數。

@q gd3-11 | al-greedy#3 | 2 | 計算
字元頻率 a:12、b:5、c:9、d:20、e:4、f:30。Huffman 編碼後總共幾個位元？
== 187
---
合併 4+5=9、9+9=18、12+18=30、20+30=50、30+50=80。總位元數＝所有合併節點的和 $9+18+30+50+80=187$。程式驗算。

@q gd3-12 | al-greedy#3 | 2 | 計算
同一組（a:12、b:5、c:9、d:20、e:4、f:30）用固定長度編碼，每個字元幾位元、總共幾位元？Huffman 省了幾成？
== 3,240,22%
?? 每字,總數,省
---
6 個字元要 3 位元，$80\times3=240$；$(240-187)/240\approx22\%$。

@q gd3-13 | al-greedy#3 | 3 | 計算
頻率 1、1、2、3、5、8（費氏數）的 6 個字元，Huffman 樹最深的葉子深度？總位元數？
== 5,45
?? 最深,總位元
---
每次合併的新節點剛好跟下一個頻率配對，樹長成一條鍊：碼長 5、5、4、3、2、1。總和 $2+4+7+12+20=45$。費氏頻率讓 Huffman 樹最不平衡。

@q gd3-14 | al-greedy#3 | 2 | 陷阱
8 個字元頻率全部相同。Huffman 編碼後每個字元幾位元？
== 3
---
兩兩合併成平衡的完整二元樹，每個都 3 位元，跟固定長度一樣。Huffman 只在頻率不平均時有好處。

@q gd3-15 | al-greedy#3 | 2 | 追蹤
碼表 a=0、b=10、c=110、d=111。解碼位元串 `1101001110`。
== cabda
== c a b d a
---
110→c、0→a、10→b、111→d、0→a。前綴碼不需要分隔符號：讀到一個合法的碼就確定是它。

@q gd3-16 | al-greedy#3 | 2 | 找錯
A student builds a Huffman tree by repeatedly merging the two characters (or subtrees) with the **largest** frequencies. Which of the following statements are correct?
[x] High-frequency characters end up deepest, with the longest codewords.
[x] The total encoded length can become larger than optimal.
[x] The correct algorithm repeatedly merges the two smallest frequencies.
[ ] The result is still optimal.
[ ] No tree can be built this way.
---
每次合併最大的兩個，它們就被放到最深處，碼最長，總長度反而變大，「High-frequency characters end up…」、「The total encoded length…」對、「still optimal」錯（例如 a:1、b:1、c:10、d:10，這樣做會讓 c、d 用 3 位元）。正確做法是用優先佇列每次合併最小的兩個，「The correct algorithm repeatedly…」對。樹還是建得出來，只是不是最佳。

@q gd3-17 | al-greedy#3 | 2 | 觀念
Consider the code table a = 0, b = 01, c = 11. Which of the following statements are correct?
[x] It is not a prefix code, because the codeword of a is a prefix of the codeword of b.
[x] After reading `0`, a decoder cannot yet tell whether it has seen a or the start of b; it must look ahead.
[x] Every code produced by Huffman's algorithm is a prefix code, because characters appear only at leaves.
[ ] It is a prefix code.
[ ] The string `011` has two different valid decodings.
---
0 是 01 的開頭，不是前綴碼，「It is not a…」、「After reading `0`, a…」對、「is a prefix code」錯：讀到 0 時要往後看才知道。`011` 只能拆成 0 + 11（a、c），拆成 01 + 1 時剩下的 1 不是任何碼，所以只有一種解讀，「The string `011` has…」錯；這組碼其實能唯一解碼，只是不能「讀到就確定」。Huffman 的字元都在葉子上，任一個碼不會是另一個的開頭，「Every code produced by…」對。

@q gd3-18 | al-greedy#3 | 3 | 計算
三元 Huffman（每個內部點 3 個孩子，碼用 0、1、2）。有 6 個字元時，要補幾個頻率 0 的假字元，才能每次都剛好合併 3 個？
== 1
---
每次合併少 2 個節點，要從 $n$ 個合到 1 個，需要 $n\equiv1\pmod2$。6 是偶數，補 1 個變 7。

@q gd3-19 | al-greedy#3 | 2 | 情境
Huffman coding is used in formats such as JPEG and ZIP. Which of the following statements about why the code table is usually stored in the file are correct?
[x] Symbol frequencies differ from file to file, so the code tables differ.
[x] The decoder needs the same table to recover the data.
[x] Some formats use a fixed default table so that the table need not be stored.
[ ] The table is stored to encrypt the data.
[ ] The table never needs to be stored, since every file uses the same codes.
---
每個檔案的符號頻率不同、碼表不同，解碼端要有同一份碼表才能還原，「Symbol frequencies differ from…」、「The decoder needs the…」對。有些格式用固定的預設碼表省掉這部分，「Some formats use a…」對。碼表和加密無關。

@q gd4-09 | al-greedy#4 | 2 | 情境
A truck can carry 10 tons. The cargo is either loose gravel (any fraction may be loaded) or crated machines (each crate is loaded whole or not at all). Which of the following statements are correct?
[x] For the gravel, loading the highest value per ton first is optimal; this is fractional knapsack.
[x] For the crates, the problem is 0/1 knapsack, which needs dynamic programming.

@q gd4-10 | al-greedy#4 | 2 | 反例
Fractional knapsack with weight limit 10 and items given as (weight, value): (10, 60), (5, 40), (5, 40). Consider the rule "take the item with the highest total value first." Which of the following statements are correct?
[x] The highest-value-first rule takes the (10, 60) item, for a total value of 60.
[x] Taking items by value per unit weight gives both (5, 40) items, for a total value of 80.
[x] This instance shows that the highest-value-first rule is not optimal.
[ ] Both rules give a total value of 60.
[ ] The highest-value-first rule is optimal for this instance.
---
價值最高的是 (10,60)，拿了就滿，得 60；單位價值 (5,40) 是 8、(10,60) 是 6，先拿兩個 (5,40) 得 80，「The highest-value-first rule takes…」、「Taking items by value…」、「This instance shows that…」對、「Both rules give a…」、「The highest-value-first rule is…」錯。分數背包一定要看「每單位重量的價值」。

@q gd4-11 | al-greedy#4 | 3 | 觀念
Fractional knapsack is to be solved without sorting. Which of the following statements are correct?
[x] It can be solved in $O(n)$ time: find the middle value-per-weight ratio in linear time, check whether the items above it fit, and recurse into only one half.
[x] The resulting recurrence $T(n)=T(n/2)+O(n)$ solves to $O(n)$.
[x] A complete sort is unnecessary; only enough ratio comparisons to choose the next half are required.
[x] The division into groups is based on value per unit weight, rather than on item weight alone.
[ ] It can be done in $O(n)$ time only when all items have equal weight.
---
「中位比值加單半遞迴」對：高比值側太重只在該側找，裝得下則全取後處理低比值側，平手比值可直接分配剩額。「T(n/2) 加線性為線性」對：每輪至少捨去一半候選，幾何和 O(n)。「不必完整排序」對：選擇中位門檻已能決定丟棄哪一半。「按價值重量比分組」對：只分輕重無法比較每單位容量的收益。「只有等重能線性」錯：選擇法也適用不同正重量；等比值的一組可直接按容量取部分。

@q gd4-12 | al-greedy#4 | 2 | 找錯
A student solves 0/1 knapsack with the fractional greedy rule, skipping any item that does not fit, and claims this is optimal. Consider weight limit 10 and items (weight, value) = (6, 36), (5, 25), (5, 25). Which of the following statements are correct?
[x] Greedy by value per unit weight takes (6, 36) first and ends with total value 36.
[x] The optimum takes both (5, 25) items for total value 50.
[x] So the greedy rule is not optimal for 0/1 knapsack.
[ ] The greedy rule also finds total value 50 here.
[ ] No counterexample to the student's claim exists.
---
單位價值 6 > 5，貪婪先拿 (6,36)，剩 4 放不下別的，得 36；拿兩個 (5,25) 剛好 10，得 50，「Greedy by value per…」、「The optimum takes both…」、「So the greedy rule…」對、「The greedy rule also…」、「No counterexample to the…」錯。不能切時，貪婪可能留下用不到的空間，要考慮組合。

@q gd4-13 | al-greedy#4 | 1 | 計算
分數背包容量 5，只有一件物品 (8, 64)。最大價值？
== 40
---
只拿 5/8，價值 $64\times5/8$。

@q gd4-14 | al-greedy#4 | 3 | 比較
For 0/1 knapsack, consider the greedy rule "take items in decreasing order of value per unit weight whenever they fit". Which of the following statements are correct?
[x] Its answer can be worse than the optimum by an arbitrarily large factor.
[x] With weight limit $W\ge2$ and the items (weight 1, value 2) and (weight $W$, value $W$), it gets 2 while the optimum is $W$.
[x] Returning the larger of the greedy answer and the single most valuable item guarantees at least half of the optimum.
[ ] Its answer is always within 1 of the optimum.
[ ] It always finds the optimum.
---
(1,2) 的單位價值 2 比 $(W,W)$ 的 1 高，貪婪先拿它，剩下的容量放不下 $(W,W)$，只得 2；$W$ 越大差越多，「Its answer can be…」、「With weight limit $W\ge2$…」對。取「貪婪結果」和「單一最有價值的物品」兩者中較大的，保證至少有最佳的一半，「Returning the larger of…」對。

@q gd5-07 | al-greedy#5 | 2 | 觀念
Which of the following set systems are matroids?
[x] All subsets of size at most $k$ of a given set
[x] All edge sets of a graph that contain no cycle
[ ] All sets of pairwise non-adjacent vertices of a graph
[ ] All sets of edges of a graph with no shared endpoint
[ ] All sets of pairwise non-overlapping intervals
---
大小 ≤ $k$ 滿足遺傳性和交換性（小的可以從大的借一個進來，大小還是 ≤ $k$），是均勻擬陣；沒有環的邊集合是圖擬陣，這兩個對。另外三個交換性都會失敗：例如路 a–b–c 中 $\{b\}$ 和 $\{a,c\}$ 互不相鄰，但 $a$、$c$ 都不能加進 $\{b\}$；不共用端點的邊：路 a–b–c–d 的 $\{bc\}$ 和 $\{ab,cd\}$；區間：$\{(0,10)\}$ 和 $\{(0,4),(5,10)\}$，小的都借不到。

@q gd5-08 | al-greedy#5 | 3 | 計算
單位時間工作（截止, 罰金）：a(2,30)、b(1,25)、c(2,20)、d(3,15)、e(3,10)、f(1,5)。依罰金由大到小、能排進去就排，最小罰金總和？
== 35
---
依罰金：a 可；b 可（截止 1、2）；c 加入後截止是 1、2、2，第 3 個工作來不及，捨棄；d 可（1、2、3）；e、f 也都放不進去。錯過 c、e、f：$20+10+5$。程式窮舉驗算。

@q gd5-09 | al-greedy#5 | 3 | 反例
Consider the sets of pairwise non-overlapping intervals from a collection of intervals. Which of the following statements are correct?
[x] The exchange property fails: for $\{(0,10)\}$ and $\{(0,4),(5,10)\}$, the smaller set cannot take any interval from the larger one.
[x] The hereditary property holds.
[x] Hence "greedy by decreasing weight" is not guaranteed optimal for weighted activity selection.
[ ] The hereditary property fails.
[ ] This system is a matroid.
---
互不重疊的區間拿掉幾個還是互不重疊，遺傳性成立，「The hereditary property holds.」對、「hereditary property fails」錯。$(0,4)$、$(5,10)$ 都和 $(0,10)$ 重疊，大的那組借不出任何一個，交換性失敗，不是擬陣，「The exchange property fails:…」對、「is a matroid」錯。所以加權活動選擇不能直接用擬陣貪婪，要用 DP，「Hence "greedy by decreasing…」對。

@q gd5-10 | al-greedy#5 | 2 | 觀念
In the graphic matroid of a connected graph, the edges are considered in increasing order of weight, and an edge is added whenever it does not form a cycle. Which of the following statements are correct?
[x] The result is an acyclic edge set connecting all vertices with minimum total weight.
[x] This is exactly Kruskal's algorithm.
[x] Because the graphic matroid is a matroid, this greedy rule is optimal for any weights.
[ ] The result is a minimum-weight route between two given vertices.
[ ] The result is a largest set of edges with no shared endpoint.
---
不形成環就加入、權重由小到大，得到連通全部點、沒有環、總權重最小的邊集合，就是 Kruskal，「The result is an…」、「This is exactly Kruskal's…」對；擬陣保證這種貪婪對任何權重都最佳，「Because the graphic matroid…」對。兩點間最短路線、互不共用端點的邊集合是別的問題。

@q gd5-11 | al-greedy#5 | 2 | 計算
集合 {1,…,6} 分成 A={1,2,3}、B={4,5,6}，「每組最多選 1 個」的子集合都算獨立（分割擬陣）。最大獨立集有幾個元素？
== 2
---
每組各選一個。它滿足遺傳性和交換性，所以「挑權重最大的、能加就加」就會在每組挑最重的。

@q gd5-12 | al-greedy#5 | 3 | 證明
說明為什麼擬陣中所有「極大」獨立集（不能再加任何元素的）大小都一樣。
---
設 $A$、$B$ 都是極大獨立集，若 $|A|<|B|$，由交換性存在 $x\in B\setminus A$ 使 $A\cup\{x\}$ 仍獨立，跟 $A$ 極大矛盾。所以大小相等。這就是為什麼貪婪一路加到不能再加，一定得到大小正確的基底。

@q gd5-13 | al-greedy#5 | 2 | 找錯
A student says: "If the independent sets are closed under taking subsets (the hereditary property), greedy by weight is always optimal." Consider sets of edges with no shared endpoint on the path a–b–c–d, with edge weights ab = 2, bc = 3, cd = 2. Which of the following statements are correct?
[x] The hereditary property alone does not guarantee optimal weighted greedy selection.
[x] Greedy selects bc of weight three, whereas ab and cd together have weight four.
[x] These matching edge sets are hereditary but fail augmentation.
[x] The matching containing only bc is maximal by inclusion but is not maximum-weight here.
[ ] Having no common endpoints guarantees the augmentation property for every pair of matching edge sets in every graph.
---
「僅遺傳不夠」對：仍需擬陣交換性才能保證一般權重貪婪。「三對四反例」對：bc 擋住兩端，ab 與 cd 可同取。「遺傳而不交換」對：匹配的子集仍匹配，但小集 bc 不能從較大集 ab、cd 添邊。「極大非最大權重」對：bc 加不進任何邊，卻輸給總權重四。「所有圖都交換」錯：本路徑已提供一個失敗配對。

@q gd5-14 | al-greedy#5 | 1 | 觀念
Which of the following statements about the hereditary property of a matroid are correct?
[x] Every subset of an independent set is also independent.
[x] It implies that the empty set is independent (given that some independent set exists).
[x] The other condition in the definition of a matroid is the exchange property.
[ ] It says that every set is independent.
[ ] It says that independent sets must be large.
---
遺傳性：獨立集的任何子集合也是獨立，空集合是任何集合的子集合，所以也獨立，「Every subset of an…」、「It implies that the…」對；另一個條件是交換性，「The other condition in…」對。遺傳性讓「一路加元素」的過程中每一步都還是獨立集。它沒有說所有集合都獨立，也跟大小無關。

@q gd6-09 | al-greedy#6 | 2 | 計算
面額 1、6、9，湊 12 元。貪婪用幾枚？最少幾枚？
== 4,2
?? 貪婪,最少
---
貪婪 9+1+1+1；最佳 6+6。程式驗算 12 也是這組面額讓貪婪出錯的最小金額。

@q gd6-10 | al-greedy#6 | 3 | 計算
面額 1、5、10、20、25，哪個最小的金額讓貪婪找零出錯？
== 40
---
貪婪 25+10+5（3 枚），最佳 20+20（2 枚）。加了 20 元面額就破壞了原本 1、5、10、25 的貪婪性質。程式掃描 1～99 驗算。

@q gd6-11 | al-greedy#6 | 2 | 計算
書桌放 3 本，接下來依序要用 A C B D A C E B A D。「放回下次用到最晚的」規則（Belady）共跑書架幾次（第一次拿也算）？用「放回最久沒用的」呢？
== 6,10
?? Belady,最久沒用
---
程式模擬兩種。Belady 需要知道未來，現實中做不到，但它是最佳的，用來衡量其他方法。

@q gd6-12 | al-greedy#6 | 2 | 比較
Use a positive integer target and unit-cost division, returning coin counts rather than listing every coin.
Coin change with $k$ denominations and target amount $A$. Which of the following statements are correct?
[x] With the denominations sorted, the greedy method takes $O(k)$ time (one division per denomination).
[x] The DP method takes $O(kA)$ time.
[x] For positive integer $A$, both stated methods satisfy an $O(kA)$ upper bound, although it is loose for greedy.
[x] With denominations one, four, six and target eight, greedy uses three coins while the optimum uses two.
[ ] Greedy gives the minimum number of coins for every set of denominations.
---
「排序後商數貪婪 O(k)」對：每面額做固定次除法與更新，不逐枚列出。「DP O(kA)」對：逐金額試面額。「兩法 O(kA)」對：$A$≥1 時 O(k) 也屬 O(kA)，只是對貪婪不緊。「八元三枚對兩枚」對：貪婪六加一加一，最佳四加四。「所有面額都最佳」錯：此例正是反例。

@q gd6-13 | al-greedy#6 | 3 | 情境
某國想發行面額 1、4、5 的硬幣。設計前用程式檢查「貪婪找零是否永遠最佳」，最小的反例金額是？
== 8
---
貪婪 5+1+1+1（4 枚），最佳 4+4（2 枚）。實務上會避免這種面額，讓收銀員直覺的找法就是最好的。

@q gd6-14 | al-greedy#6 | 2 | 觀念
Which of the following statements about Belady's algorithm, which evicts the page whose next use is farthest in the future, are correct?
[x] It is a greedy algorithm: each step decides only which page to evict now.
[x] An exchange argument proves it optimal.
[x] It needs knowledge of the future, so it serves as a theoretical yardstick for choosing which page to evict.
[ ] It is a dynamic programming algorithm.
[ ] It makes random choices.
---
每次只看眼前要替換誰，是貪婪；用交換論證可以證明它缺頁最少，「It is a greedy…」、「An exchange argument proves…」對。它是「知道未來的貪婪」，實際做不到，所以是快取替換的理論下界，「It needs knowledge of…」對。

@q gd6-15 | al-greedy#6 | 2 | 找錯
A student says: "Using as many of the largest coin as possible is always best, so greedy change-making works for every set of denominations." Which of the following statements are correct?
[x] Denominations one, three, four with amount six give a greedy counterexample.
[x] Greedy uses four plus one plus one, while two threes use only two coins.
[x] Greedy is optimal for all nonnegative integer amounts with denominations one, five, ten.
[ ] With denominations one and two, amount three requires three coins under the largest-first greedy rule.
[x] Having denomination one ensures representability, not greedy minimum-coin optimality for arbitrary larger denominations.
---
「一三四六反例」對：四加一加一是三枚，三加三只兩枚。「三對二」對：逐步算即可。「一五十為標準可行組」對：每五枚一可換五、兩枚五可換十，不增加枚數，故可整理成貪婪形式。「一二湊三需三枚」錯：二加一只兩枚。「一只保證可湊」對：不能把表示存在誤當局部選大幣必最少。

@q gd6-16 | al-greedy#6 | 3 | 證明
說明為什麼面額 1、5、10、25 用貪婪找零一定最佳（可以只論證「最佳解裡 1 元最多 4 枚、5 元最多 1 枚、10 元最多 2 枚」）。
---
最佳解中：1 元若有 5 枚可換成一枚 5 元（枚數變少），所以 ≤4；5 元若有 2 枚可換一枚 10 元，所以 ≤1；10 元若有 3 枚可換 25+5（3 枚變 2 枚），所以 ≤2，而且 10 元 2 枚加 5 元 1 枚＝25 可換一枚 25。所以 1、5、10 的部分總和最多 24，不到 25；最佳解的 25 元枚數就是 $\lfloor A/25\rfloor$，跟貪婪一樣，剩下的部分同理遞推。

@q sp1-11 | al-sp#1 | 2 | 計算
$s.d=0$，其他 $\infty$。依序鬆弛 $(s,a)$ 權重 4、$(s,b)$ 權重 1、$(b,a)$ 權重 2、$(a,c)$ 權重 1。之後 $a.d$、$c.d$？
== 3,4
?? a.d,c.d
---
$(s,a)$：$a.d=4$；$(s,b)$：$b.d=1$；$(b,a)$：$1+2=3<4$，$a.d=3$；$(a,c)$：$c.d=4$。鬆弛順序剛好沿最短路徑，一遍就對。

@q sp1-12 | al-sp#1 | 3 | 陷阱
同一張圖（s→a 4、s→b 1、b→a 2、a→c 1），鬆弛順序改成 $(a,c)$、$(s,a)$、$(s,b)$、$(b,a)$。一遍之後 $c.d$？
== ∞
== inf
---
第一個鬆弛 $(a,c)$ 時 $a.d$ 還是 ∞，$c.d$ 不變；之後 $a.d$ 變 3，但 $(a,c)$ 不會再被鬆弛。鬆弛順序沒照路徑時要多跑幾輪（這就是 Bellman-Ford 跑 $V-1$ 輪的原因）。

@q sp1-13 | al-sp#1 | 2 | 觀念
Every single-source shortest-path algorithm in the textbook starts with $s.d=0$ and $v.d=\infty$ for $v\ne s$, and then changes estimates only by relaxation: for an edge $(u,v)$ of weight $w$, if $u.d + w < v.d$, it sets $v.d = u.d + w$. A student watches such an algorithm on a graph with 6 vertices and 9 edges whose integer weights lie between $-2$ and 8, no negative-weight cycle, and one vertex $z$ that cannot be reached from $s$, recording all estimates after every relaxation and comparing them with the true distances computed afterwards. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] At every moment, $v.d\ge\delta(s,v)$ for every vertex $v$.
[x] Once $v.d=\delta(s,v)$, the value $v.d$ never changes again.
[x] At every moment, each finite $v.d$ is the weight of some path from $s$ to $v$.
[x] If $v$ is not reachable from $s$, then $v.d$ stays $\infty$.
[ ] Right after the edge $(u,v)$ is relaxed, $v.d=\delta(s,v)$.
---
「$v.d\ge\delta$」對：這是上界性質。

「每個有限的 $v.d$ 都是某條路徑的權重」對：鬆弛把 $v.d$ 設成 $u.d+w(u,v)$，就是「走到 $u$ 的那條路再接上這條邊」。也因此 $v.d$ 不會小於真正的最短距離。

「相等後就不再變」對：鬆弛只會讓它變小，但它已經不能更小了。

「走不到就一直是 $\infty$」對：沒有任何路徑能給它有限的值。

「鬆弛 $(u,v)$ 後立刻是最短」錯：只有在 $u.d$ 已經正確、而且 $(u,v)$ 在某條最短路徑上時才成立。

@q sp1-14 | al-sp#1 | 2 | 計算
圖中 a→b 3、b→c −2、c→a −2，s→a 2、s→d 1。$s$ 到 $d$ 的最短距離有定義嗎？到 $b$ 呢？
== 有,沒有
?? d,b
---
環 a→b→c→a 權重 $3-2-2=-1$，從 $s$ 走得到，環上的點和從環走得到的點距離都是 $-\infty$。$d$ 不經過環，距離 1。

@q sp1-15 | al-sp#1 | 2 | 情境
匯率換算：把匯率 $r$ 取 $-\log r$ 當邊權重，找「換一圈回到原貨幣錢變多」的套利機會，等於找什麼？
[x] 負環：一圈的匯率相乘 $>1$ ⇔ 一圈的 $-\log r$ 相加 $<0$
[ ] 最短路徑
[ ] 正環
[ ] 權重最大的那條邊
---
乘法變加法、大於 1 變小於 0。用 Bellman-Ford 的負環偵測就能找套利。

@q sp1-16 | al-sp#1 | 2 | 找錯
學生說：「最短路徑的子路徑也是最短路徑，所以最長路徑的子路徑也是最長路徑。」錯在哪？
[x] 最長**簡單**路徑沒有這個性質：子路徑的最長版本可能會跟其他部分共用點，接起來不再是簡單路徑
[ ] 沒錯
[ ] 最短路徑也沒有這個性質
[ ] 兩者都要負權重才成立
---
這就是最長簡單路徑不能直接用 DP 或 Bellman-Ford 的原因。

@q sp1-17 | al-sp#1 | 1 | 觀念
無向圖的邊權重有負的，能直接當成兩個方向的有向邊來算最短路徑嗎？
[x] 不行：一條負的邊來回走就是負環
[ ] 可以
[ ] 只要只有一條是負的就可以
[ ] 可以，只要點的編號照順序
---
無向圖有負邊時要用別的方法（例如轉成匹配問題）。

@q sp1-18 | al-sp#1 | 3 | 證明
說明為什麼沒有負環時，最短路徑一定可以是簡單路徑（不重複點）。
---
若最短路徑重複經過某點 $v$，中間那段是從 $v$ 回到 $v$ 的環。沒有負環，這個環權重 ≥ 0，把它拿掉得到的路徑權重不會更大，還是最短。重複拿掉直到沒有重複點。所以最多 $V-1$ 條邊。

@q sp3-19 | al-sp#3 | 2 | 計算
6 個點的圖沒有負環。最短路徑最多經過幾條邊？Bellman-Ford 至少要跑幾輪才保證正確？
== 5,5
?? 邊數,輪數
---
簡單路徑最多 $V-1$ 條邊；每輪至少把路徑上再多一條邊鬆弛對。

@q sp2-12 | al-sp#2 | 2 | 追蹤
有向圖 s→a 4、s→b 1、b→a 2、a→c 1、b→c 5、c→d 3、b→d 8、d→e 2、c→e 6。Dijkstra 從 s 出發，點被取出的順序？
== s b a c d e
== s,b,a,c,d,e
---
d 值 s 0、b 1、a 3（經 b）、c 4、d 7、e 9。程式驗算。

@q sp2-13 | al-sp#2 | 2 | 計算
同一張圖，s 到 e 的最短路徑經過哪些點？
== s b a c d e
== s,b,a,c,d,e
---
沿 parent 往回：e←d←c←a←b←s，長度 $1+2+1+3+2=9$。直接 s→a 是 4，比繞 b 的 3 長。

@q sp2-14 | al-sp#2 | 3 | 計算
Dijkstra's algorithm uses a binary heap on a graph with $V$ vertices and $E$ edges. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] Decreasing some vertex's $d$ value and adjusting the heap happens at most $E$ times.
[x] Each such adjustment takes $O(\lg V)$ time.
[x] Together with the $V$ removals of the smallest $d$ value, the total time is $O((V+E)\lg V)$.
[ ] Decreasing a $d$ value happens only $V$ times, each in $O(1)$ time.
[ ] Each edge can decrease its endpoint's $d$ value many times.
---
「最多 $E$ 次」對：每條邊只在它的起點被取出時鬆弛一次。

「每次 $O(\lg V)$」對：往上浮。

「總共 $O((V+E)\lg V)$」對。

「只有 $V$ 次、每次 $O(1)$」錯。

「每條邊可以讓 $d$ 變小很多次」錯：每條邊只在它的起點被取出時鬆弛一次，最多讓 $d$ 變小一次，所以全部最多 $E$ 次。

@q sp2-15 | al-sp#2 | 2 | 情境
導航 App 算從家裡到公司的最短開車時間（每段路的時間都是正的），地圖有一百萬個路口。用哪個演算法？

[ ] 窮舉所有路線

@q sp2-16 | al-sp#2 | 3 | 陷阱
In a graph, only the edges leaving the source $s$ may have negative weights; all other edges are nonnegative, and no edge enters $s$. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] Dijkstra's algorithm still gives correct distances.
[x] $s$ is extracted first, and afterwards no path can return to $s$ and use those negative edges again to shorten an extracted vertex.
[ ] Dijkstra's algorithm is wrong whenever any edge is negative.
[x] If instead a negative edge entered a vertex other than $s$ that was already extracted, the result could be wrong.
[ ] The result is correct only when the negative weights are small in absolute value.
---
「仍然正確」對：問題在「已經取出的點，之後被更短的路徑更新」，負邊只在起點時不會發生。這是少見的例外。

「起點最先取出、之後用不到那些負邊」對：其他點取出後，後面的路徑都只經過非負邊。

「有負邊一定錯」錯：見上。

「負邊指向已取出的點就可能錯」對：那個點不會再處理一次。

「只有負得少才對」錯：跟大小無關。

@q sp2-17 | al-sp#2 | 2 | 比較
Which of the following statements about Dijkstra's algorithm and BFS are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] If every edge has weight 1, Dijkstra's algorithm extracts the vertices in nondecreasing order of BFS distance.
[x] In that case, BFS with an ordinary queue suffices and runs in $O(V+E)$ time.
[ ] BFS correctly handles arbitrary nonnegative weights.
[x] If every weight is 0 or 1, a double-ended queue that puts vertices reached by 0-edges at the front and by 1-edges at the back finds shortest paths in $O(V+E)$ time.
[ ] On graphs where every edge has weight 1, Dijkstra's algorithm is faster than BFS.
---
「權重都是 1 時順序和 BFS 一樣」對：距離就是邊數。

「BFS 就夠」對：佇列本身就照距離排好了。

「BFS 處理任意權重」錯：它只數邊。

「0-1 權重用雙端佇列」對：0 邊不增加距離，放前面；1 邊放後面，佇列仍照距離排序。

「Dijkstra 比較快」錯：多了堆積的 $\lg V$。

@q sp2-18 | al-sp#2 | 2 | 找錯
A student's Dijkstra's algorithm, after extracting a vertex $u$, relaxes only the edges to neighbors that have not been extracted, but finds the minimum by scanning all vertices, including those already extracted. All edge weights are nonnegative. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The minimum must be taken only among vertices that have not yet been extracted.
[x] Because extracted vertices are included in the scan, the same vertex can be picked again and again.
[ ] Edges into already-extracted neighbors must also be relaxed, or their distances may stay wrong.
[x] With nonnegative weights, relaxing an edge into an already-extracted vertex never changes that vertex's distance.
[ ] The algorithm should pick the vertex with the largest distance estimate.
---
「只能從還沒取出的點挑」對：這是 Dijkstra 的定義。

「會一直重複挑同一個」對：已取出的點 $d$ 通常最小，一直被選中，其他點永遠輪不到。

「鬆弛到已取出的點不會改變它」對：已取出的點 $d$ 已經是最終答案。

「挑最大的」錯。

[x] The cycle $a\to b\to c\to a$ has total weight $-1$.
[x] After 4 rounds, checking every edge once more finds an edge that can still be relaxed.
[ ] After 4 rounds, no edge can be relaxed.
[x] The distance $\delta(s,d)=1$ is still well defined.

@q sp3-12 | al-sp#3 | 3 | 陷阱
We want to know whether a directed graph contains a negative cycle anywhere, even one that is not reachable from a given vertex $s$. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] We can add a new vertex $q$ with an edge of weight 0 to every vertex and run Bellman-Ford from $q$.
[ ] Running Bellman-Ford once from an arbitrary vertex $s$ always detects every negative cycle in the graph.
[x] After the usual $|V'| - 1$ rounds from $q$, where $V'$ includes $q$, the graph has a negative cycle if and only if some edge can still be relaxed.
[ ] Running $|V|$ rounds from $s$ instead of $|V| - 1$ rounds detects negative cycles that are not reachable from $s$.
[x] If the graph has no negative cycle, every distance computed from $q$ is at most 0.
---
「加新點 $q$ 連到每點」對：$q$ 走得到每個點，所以任何負環都變成可達的。

「從任意 $s$ 跑一次就能偵測所有負環」錯：從 $s$ 走不到的負環，它的點距離一直是無限大，邊永遠鬆弛不了，看不出來。

「跑完還能鬆弛 ⇔ 有負環」對：沒有負環時最短路徑最多 $|V'|-1$ 條邊，跑完一定收斂；還能鬆弛就表示有可達的負環，而從 $q$ 出發每個負環都可達。

「多跑一輪就能看到走不到的負環」錯：問題是「走不到」，不是輪數不夠。

「沒有負環時距離都 $\le0$」對：$q$ 直接走 0 權重的邊就到每個點，最短距離不會超過 0。

@q sp3-13 | al-sp#3 | 2 | 計算
A system of difference constraints is $x_1 - x_2 \le 0$, $x_1 - x_5 \le -1$, and $x_2 - x_5 \le 1$. It is solved with a constraint graph and Bellman-Ford from a new vertex $v_0$ that has an edge of weight 0 to every variable's vertex. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] A constraint $x_i - x_j \le c$ becomes an edge from $j$ to $i$ with weight $c$.
[x] Taking $x_i = \delta(v_0, i)$ gives the solution $x_1 = -1$, $x_2 = 0$, $x_5 = 0$.
[ ] Every variable gets the value 0, since $v_0$ has an edge of weight 0 to it.
[x] If the constraint graph contains a negative cycle, the system has no solution.
[ ] Adding the same constant to every $x_i$ in a solution may make it violate some constraint.
---
「$x_i-x_j\le c$ 對應 $j\to i$、權重 $c$」對：最短距離滿足 $d_i\le d_j+w(j,i)$，也就是 $d_i-d_j\le w(j,i)$，形式剛好一樣。

「解是 $(-1,0,0)$」對：邊有 $2\to1$(0)、$5\to1$($-1$)、$5\to2$(1)，再加 $v_0$ 到每點 0。$x_5=0$；$x_2=\min(0,\ 0+1)=0$；$x_1=\min(0,\ 0+0,\ 0-1)=-1$。檢查：$-1-0\le0$、$-1-0\le-1$、$0-0\le1$ 都成立。

「全部都是 0」錯：$v_0$ 的 0 邊只是上界；別的路徑可能更短，例如 $v_0\to5\to1$ 讓 $x_1=-1$。

「有負環就無解」對：沿著負環把約束加起來，左邊抵消成 0，右邊是負數，得到 $0\le$ 負數，矛盾。

「全部加同一個常數可能違反」錯：每個約束只看差，同加一個常數差不變，一定還是解。

@q sp3-14 | al-sp#3 | 2 | 情境

For difference constraints, a constraint $x_i - x_j \le c$ becomes an edge from $j$ to $i$ with weight $c$. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The first requirement is $x_P - x_Q \le -3$.
[x] The second requirement is $x_Q - x_P \le 5$.
[x] The constraint graph has edges $Q \to P$ with weight $-3$ and $P \to Q$ with weight 5, forming a cycle of weight 2, so the system is feasible.
[ ] Starting $P$ on day 0 and $Q$ on day 6 satisfies both requirements.
[ ] If the second requirement were changed to "at most 2 days after $P$ starts", the system would still be feasible.
---
「$x_P-x_Q\le-3$」對：$x_Q\ge x_P+3$，移項就是這樣。

「$x_Q-x_P\le5$」對：直接寫。

「環的權重 2、有解」對：沒有負環就有解，例如 $x_P=0$、$x_Q=3$。

@q sp3-15 | al-sp#3 | 3 | 比較
A queue-based variant of Bellman-Ford (often called SPFA) relaxes only the outgoing edges of vertices whose distance estimate changed since they were last processed. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] Its worst-case running time is still $O(VE)$.
[x] It skips vertices whose distance estimate did not change, which often makes it much faster than plain Bellman-Ford in practice.
[ ] It works correctly only when all edge weights are nonnegative.
[x] If a negative cycle is reachable from the source, the plain queue-based version may never stop unless it also counts how many times each vertex enters the queue.
[ ] In the worst case it is always asymptotically faster than plain Bellman-Ford.
---
「最壞仍 $O(VE)$」對：能構造出讓同一個點反覆進出佇列的圖。

「跳過沒變的點，實務上常快很多」對：沒變的點再鬆弛一次也不會改善任何鄰居。

「只能用在非負權重」錯：它和 Bellman-Ford 一樣可以處理負權重邊；只能用在非負權重的是 Dijkstra。

@q sp3-16 | al-sp#3 | 2 | 找錯

[x] 每一輪都要鬆弛**所有**邊，共 $V-1$ 輪
[ ] 沒錯
[ ] 應該只鬆弛負邊
[ ] 應該跑 $E$ 輪

@q sp3-17 | al-sp#3 | 3 | 證明
說明為什麼 Bellman-Ford 跑完 $V-1$ 輪後，若還有邊 $(u,v)$ 滿足 $u.d+w<v.d$，就一定有可達的負環。
---
沒有可達負環時，每個點的最短路徑最多 $V-1$ 條邊，路徑鬆弛性質保證 $V-1$ 輪後 $v.d=\delta(s,v)$，而真正的最短距離滿足三角不等式 $\delta(s,v)\le\delta(s,u)+w$，不可能再被鬆弛。所以還能鬆弛就表示假設不成立，存在可達負環。

== 100000
---
$(V-1)\times E+E=V\times E$。

@q sp4-09 | al-sp#4 | 3 | 計算
Johnson：a→b 3、b→c −4、c→d 2、a→d 4、d→b 3。加新點 q（到每點權重 0）跑 Bellman-Ford 得 $h$。$h(a),h(b),h(c),h(d)$？
== 0 0 -4 -2

---
q→c 經 b：$0-4=-4$；q→d 經 c：$-4+2=-2$；a、b 直接 0。程式驗算（另檢查沒有負環：b→c→d→b 總和 1）。

== 0,6,1
