@q sp4-13 | al-sp#4 | 3 | 陷阱
Johnson's algorithm adds a vertex $q$ with a weight-0 edge to every vertex, lets $h(v)$ be the shortest distance from $q$ to $v$, and reweights each edge as $\hat w(u,v)=w(u,v)+h(u)-h(v)$. Assume there is no negative-weight cycle. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] For every path $p$ from $x$ to $y$, $\hat w(p)=w(p)+h(x)-h(y)$.
[x] $\hat w(u,v)\ge0$ for every edge, because $h(v)\le h(u)+w(u,v)$.
[ ] Adding $|w_{\min}|$ to every edge, where $w_{\min}$ is the smallest edge weight, would also keep every shortest path a shortest path.
[x] Between any two vertices, a path is shortest under $\hat w$ if and only if it is shortest under $w$.
[ ] If the graph had a negative-weight cycle, this reweighting would still make every weight nonnegative.
---
「$\hat w(p)=w(p)+h(x)-h(y)$」對：沿路加起來中間項相消（望遠鏡和），只剩起點和終點。

「每條邊 $\ge0$」對：$h$ 是最短距離，滿足三角不等式 $h(v)\le h(u)+w(u,v)$。

「每條邊加上 $|w_{\min}|$」錯：邊數不同的路徑被加的量不同。例：$x\to y$ 權重 0，$x\to a\to y$ 權重 1、$-2$（共 $-1$，較短）。每條加 2 後直達變 2、繞路變 $3+0=3$，最短路徑換人了。

「最短路徑不變」對：同起訖點的每條路徑都被加上同一個 $h(x)-h(y)$。

「有負環也能變非負」錯：有負環時 $h$ 沒有定義（可以無限小）；Johnson 先用 Bellman–Ford 算 $h$，正是為了順便偵測負環。

@q sp4-14 | al-sp#4 | 2 | 找錯
After reweighting with $\hat w(u,v)=w(u,v)+h(u)-h(v)$, Johnson's algorithm runs Dijkstra's algorithm from every vertex and obtains the distances $\hat\delta(u,v)$. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The original distance is $\delta(u,v)=\hat\delta(u,v)-h(u)+h(v)$.
[ ] $\hat\delta(u,v)$ can be output directly as the original distance $\delta(u,v)$.
[x] A path found to be shortest under $\hat w$ is also a shortest path under the original weights.
[x] Bellman–Ford is run only once, on the graph with the added vertex $q$, to compute $h$.
[ ] The original distance is obtained as $-\hat\delta(u,v)$.
---
「$\delta=\hat\delta-h(u)+h(v)$」對：每條 $u$ 到 $v$ 的路徑都被加了 $h(u)-h(v)$，要減回來。

「直接輸出」錯：路徑本身對，但長度被平移了。

「$\hat w$ 下的最短路徑也是原本的最短路徑」對：所有同起訖點的路徑被平移同樣的量，大小順序不變。

「Bellman–Ford 只跑一次」對：從 $q$ 跑一次得到 $h$（順便偵測負環），之後都是 Dijkstra。

「取負號」錯：那是把最長路徑轉成最短路徑的技巧，跟這裡無關。

@q sp4-15 | al-sp#4 | 2 | 觀念
Johnson 演算法的第一步跑 Bellman-Ford，除了算出 $h$，還順便做什麼？
[x] 偵測負環；有負環就停止，因為最短路徑沒有定義
[ ] 找出所有點的一種排序
[ ] 排序所有邊
[ ] 什麼都沒有
---
有負環時重新加權後一定會出現負邊。

@q sp4-16 | al-sp#4 | 3 | 證明
說明為什麼 $h(v)=\delta(q,v)$（q 是新加的點）能保證 $\hat w(u,v)=w(u,v)+h(u)-h(v)\ge0$。
---
三角不等式：$\delta(q,v)\le\delta(q,u)+w(u,v)$，也就是 $h(v)\le h(u)+w(u,v)$，移項得 $w(u,v)+h(u)-h(v)\ge0$。

@q sp5-19 | al-sp#5 | 2 | 計算
A graph has $V=1000$ vertices and $E=5000$ edges, with some negative edge weights but no negative-weight cycle. Johnson's algorithm runs Bellman–Ford once and then Dijkstra's algorithm with a binary heap from every vertex. Take $\lg V\approx10$. Which of the following estimates are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The Bellman–Ford step takes about $VE=5\times10^6$ steps.
[x] The $V$ runs of Dijkstra's algorithm take about $6\times10^7$ steps in total.
[x] Floyd–Warshall takes about $10^9$ steps.
[ ] If instead $E\approx V^2=10^6$, Johnson's algorithm would still be about 15 times faster than Floyd–Warshall.
[ ] Floyd–Warshall cannot be used on this graph because some edge weights are negative.
---
「Bellman–Ford 約 $5\times10^6$」對：$1000\times5000$。

「$V$ 次 Dijkstra 約 $6\times10^7$」對：每次 $(V+E)\lg V\approx6000\times10$，乘 1000。

「Floyd–Warshall 約 $10^9$」對：$V^3$。稀疏圖上 Johnson 明顯比較快。

「$E\approx V^2$ 時 Johnson 仍快 15 倍」錯：Bellman–Ford 就要 $VE=10^9$，Dijkstra 部分約 $1000\times10^6\times10=10^{10}$，反而比 Floyd–Warshall 慢。稠密圖用 Floyd–Warshall。

「Floyd–Warshall 不能有負邊」錯：它允許負邊，只要沒有負環。

@q sp5-10 | al-sp#5 | 2 | 追蹤
Floyd–Warshall：1→2 5、2→3 −2、3→1 4、1→4 9、4→3 1、3→4 6。做完 $k=1$（只准經過點 1）之後，$d_{32}$ 是多少？
== 9
---
原本 3 到 2 沒有邊（∞）；經過 1：$d_{31}+d_{12}=4+5=9$。程式驗算。

@q sp5-11 | al-sp#5 | 2 | 計算
同一張圖全部做完，$d_{21}$、$d_{41}$？
== 2,5
---
2→3→1：$-2+4=2$；4→3→1：$1+4=5$。程式驗算。

@q sp5-12 | al-sp#5 | 3 | 陷阱
同一張圖，$d_{14}$ 是 9。直接邊 1→4 和 1→2→3→4 哪條？
[x] 一樣長，都是 9（$5-2+6=9$）
[ ] 只有直接邊
[ ] 只有繞路
[ ] 繞路是 8
---
最短路徑可能不唯一；要記錄路徑時，比較用 `<` 還是 `<=` 會決定記哪一條。

@q sp5-13 | al-sp#5 | 2 | 觀念
Floyd–Warshall is run on a graph with $V$ vertices and no negative-weight cycle, updating a single two-dimensional array in place. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] During round $k$, the entries $d_{ik}$ and $d_{kj}$ do not change, so updating one array in place is safe.
[x] The algorithm uses $O(V^2)$ space.
[x] Updating one matrix remains safe with negative edges when there is no negative-weight cycle.
[x] The algorithm runs in $\Theta(V^3)$ time.
[ ] The loop over $k$ can be moved inside the loops over $i$ and $j$ without affecting correctness.
---
「該輪到 k 與由 k 的值不變」對：其候選只是原值加 d[k][k]，沒有負環時對角零，因此不會降低。「平方空間」對：保留一張距離矩陣。「負邊仍可原地」對：需要的是無負環，不是每條邊非負。「立方時間」對：完整三重迴圈。「k 放最內仍必正確」錯：會混用尚未完成的可用中繼點範圍，單輪端點順序不能保證所有路徑已傳播。

@q sp5-14 | al-sp#5 | 2 | 計算
Floyd–Warshall 跑完後，$d_{33}=-2$。這代表什麼？
== 負環
---
點 3 到自己距離為負，表示經過 3 的某個環總權重是負的。檢查對角線就能偵測負環。

@q sp5-15 | al-sp#5 | 3 | 情境
To recover the shortest paths themselves, Floyd–Warshall also keeps a table $\text{next}[i][j]$, the vertex that follows $i$ on the current best path from $i$ to $j$. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] When $d_{ij}$ is updated to $d_{ik}+d_{kj}$, $\text{next}[i][j]$ should be set to $\text{next}[i][k]$.
[ ] The table next needs $\Theta(V^3)$ space, one entry for each triple $(i,j,k)$.
[x] When no path from $i$ to $j$ exists, $\text{next}[i][j]$ can be left undefined, since printing first checks that $d_{ij}$ is finite.
[x] Initially, $\text{next}[i][j]=j$ for every edge $(i,j)$.
[x] The path from $i$ to $j$ is printed by starting at $i$ and repeatedly moving from the current vertex $u$ to $\text{next}[u][j]$ until reaching $j$.
---
「設成 $\text{next}[i][k]$」對：走 $i$ 到 $j$ 要先走 $i$ 到 $k$ 的那條路，第一步跟 $i$ 到 $k$ 的第一步一樣。常見錯誤是設成 $k$（$k$ 不一定和 $i$ 相鄰），或設成 $\text{next}[k][j]$（那是從 $k$ 出發的下一步）。

「需要 $\Theta(V^3)$ 空間」錯：每個 $(i,j)$ 只存一格，隨 $k$ 覆寫，是 $\Theta(V^2)$。

「沒有路徑時可以不定義」對：$d_{ij}=\infty$ 時根本不會沿著 next 走。

「一開始有邊就是 $j$」對：直接走那條邊。

「一路跟著 $\text{next}[u][j]$ 走」對：每次往前一步，直到 $j$。

@q sp5-16 | al-sp#5 | 2 | 找錯
A student writes Floyd–Warshall with the loops nested as for $i$: for $j$: for $k$:, so $k$ is innermost. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The result can be wrong: when $d_{ij}$ is computed, $d_{ik}$ and $d_{kj}$ may not yet account for other intermediate vertices.
[x] The loop over $k$ must be outermost, since $k$ is the stage of the DP.
[ ] The result is always the same as with $k$ outermost.
[ ] The loops never terminate.
[x] Repeating the whole wrongly ordered triple loop three times is known to give correct results.
---
「可能錯」對：有些最短路徑會漏掉。

「$k$ 要在最外層」對：第 $k$ 輪的意思是「只用 $1..k$ 當中繼點」。

「結果一樣」錯。

「不會停」錯：迴圈次數固定。

「整個跑三遍會對」對：這是 2019 年證明的結果，但那是另一回事，正確寫法仍是 $k$ 在外。

@q sp5-17 | al-sp#5 | 1 | 計算
$V=200$ 的 Floyd–Warshall，三層迴圈共執行幾次最內層？
== 8000000
---
$200^3$：每個 $k$ 都要把所有 $(i,j)$ 看一遍。

@q sp6-09 | al-sp#6 | 2 | 計算
工作的相依關係（邊上的數字是前一個工作的時長）：A→B 3、A→C 2、B→D 4、C→D 1、C→E 5、D→F 2、E→F 3。從 A 開始到 F 可以開始，最少要多久？
== 10
---
最長路徑（關鍵路徑）：A→C→E→F $2+5+3=10$；A→B→D→F 只有 9。把權重取負在 DAG 上求最短路徑即可。程式驗算。

@q sp6-10 | al-sp#6 | 2 | 計算
接上題，關鍵路徑經過哪些工作？
== A C E F
== A,C,E,F
---
這些工作只要延誤一天，整個專案就延誤一天；不在關鍵路徑上的（例如 B）有一點寬裕時間。

@q sp6-11 | al-sp#6 | 3 | 計算

== 4
---
F 最晚 10 開始；D 要在 $10-2=8$ 前完成開始，D 最晚 8；B→D 4，B 最晚 $8-4=4$。最早是 3，所以 B 有 1 天寬裕。

@q sp6-14 | al-sp#6 | 2 | 找錯
To find shortest paths in a DAG, a student relaxes the outgoing edges of the vertices in increasing order of their vertex numbers instead of in topological order. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] If some edge goes from a higher-numbered vertex to a lower-numbered one, a vertex may be processed before its $d$ value is final.
[x] If the numbering happens to be a topological order, the result is correct.
[ ] The algorithm then loops forever.
[ ] The algorithm then reports a negative cycle.

@q sp6-15 | al-sp#6 | 3 | 情境

[ ] 因為權重都是正的

@q sp6-16 | al-sp#6 | 2 | 計算

@q mf1-11 | al-mst-flow#1 | 2 | 計算
無向圖 A–B 6、A–C 3、B–C 4、B–D 2、C–D 5、C–E 7、D–E 1、D–F 8、E–F 9。MST 的總權重？
== 18
---
Kruskal 依序加 D–E 1、B–D 2、A–C 3、B–C 4，C–D 5 會成環跳過、C–E 7 也成環，最後 D–F 8。$1+2+3+4+8=18$。程式驗算。

@q mf1-12 | al-mst-flow#1 | 2 | 追蹤
同一張圖，從 A 開始跑 Prim，加入的邊依序是？
== AC BC BD DE DF
== AC,BC,BD,DE,DF
== A–C,C–B,B–D,D–E,D–F
---
每次加「已選點集合往外最便宜的邊」：A–C 3 → C–B 4 → B–D 2 → D–E 1 → D–F 8。順序跟 Kruskal 不同，但總和一樣。

@q mf1-13 | al-mst-flow#1 | 3 | 陷阱
同一張圖，把邊 D–F 的權重從 8 改成 10，MST 會變嗎？
[x] 會換掉 D–F：E–F 9 變得比它便宜，MST 改用 E–F，總和 19
[ ] 不會變
[ ] 總和變 20
[ ] 會整個重算成另一棵完全不同的樹
---
F 只靠 D–F 或 E–F 連進來，挑便宜的那條。總和 $18-8+9=19$。

@q mf1-14 | al-mst-flow#1 | 2 | 計算
完整圖 $K_4$ 的 6 條邊權重是 1～6 各一條（怎麼分配都可以）。MST 總權重最少可能是？最多可能是？
== 6,7
?? 最少,最多
---
MST 有 3 條邊。最少：1、2、3 不成環時全拿，6。最多：1、2 一定會被選（兩條邊不可能成環）；若 1、2、3 剛好圍成三角形，3 被跳過，第三條要用 4（4 不可能再跟 1、2 成環，因為三角形已經用掉了），所以最多 $1+2+4=7$。程式窮舉 720 種分配驗算。

@q mf1-15 | al-mst-flow#1 | 2 | 觀念
A student implements Kruskal's algorithm for a graph with $V$ vertices and $E$ edges as follows:
```
sort edges by weight
for each edge (u, v) in sorted order:
    if find(u) != find(v):
        add (u, v) to the tree
        union(u, v)
```
Here `find` and `union` belong to a Union-Find structure that uses path compression and union by rank. On a test graph with $V = 6$ and $E = 9$, the loop accepts 5 edges and rejects the other 4, and the student wants to explain each step of the code in a written report. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] It uses a Union-Find structure: an edge whose endpoints are already in the same set would form a cycle.
[x] With path compression and union by rank, each Union-Find operation takes nearly constant time on average over any long sequence of operations.
[x] Its running time is dominated by sorting the edges, $O(E\lg E)$.
[ ] It detects cycles with a stack in $O(1)$ time per edge.
[ ] It needs a priority queue keyed by each vertex's distance from a start vertex.
---
「用 Union-Find 判斷成環」對：不在同一集合就加入並合併。

「幾乎常數」對：攤銷 $O(\alpha(n))$。

「瓶頸是排序」對。

「用堆疊 $O(1)$」錯：堆疊沒辦法快速判斷兩點是否已連通。

「以起點距離當 key 的優先佇列」錯：那是 Prim（以「到樹的距離」為 key）或 Dijkstra 的作法。

@q mf1-16 | al-mst-flow#1 | 2 | 比較
稠密圖（$E\approx V^2$）上，Kruskal 和用陣列實作的 Prim 哪個比較好？
[x] Prim 陣列版 $O(V^2)$；Kruskal 要排序 $O(E\lg E)=O(V^2\lg V)$，比較慢
[ ] Kruskal 比較好
[ ] 一樣
[ ] 都不能用
---
稀疏圖時 Kruskal 或堆積版 Prim 比較好。

@q mf1-17 | al-mst-flow#1 | 3 | 找錯
學生說：「從某點出發、讓每個點到它的距離都最短的那棵樹，就是 MST。」反例？
[x] 三角形 s–a 2、s–b 2、a–b 1：讓距離最短的樹是 s–a、s–b（總 4）；MST 是 a–b 加一條 2（總 3）
[ ] 沒有反例
[ ] 只有一條邊的圖
[ ] 所有權重相同的圖
---
最短路徑樹讓「每點到 s」最近；MST 讓「整棵樹」最便宜。

@q mf1-18 | al-mst-flow#1 | 3 | 證明
用 cycle property 說明：圖中權重**唯一最大**的那條邊，若在某個環上，就一定不在 MST 裡。
---
設邊 $e$ 在環 $C$ 上且權重嚴格最大。若 MST $T$ 含 $e$，把 $e$ 拿掉 $T$ 分成兩塊；環 $C$ 上除了 $e$ 還有別條邊跨過這兩塊，換成那條邊得到的樹權重更小，跟 $T$ 是 MST 矛盾。

@q mf1-19 | al-mst-flow#1 | 2 | 情境
村莊之間要拉電線，有些村莊之間已經有現成的線（成本 0）。最小成本讓全部連通，怎麼處理？
[x] 把現成的線當權重 0 的邊（或先把它們連起來的村莊合成一個點），再跑 MST
[ ] 不能用 MST
[ ] 把現成的線刪掉再跑
[ ] 只連最近的兩個村莊
---
Kruskal 會先把所有 0 的邊加進去（只要不成環），剛好就是「先用現成的」。

@q mf2-11 | al-mst-flow#2 | 2 | 計算
流量網路 s→a 10、s→b 10、a→b 3、a→c 6、b→c 4、b→t 4、c→t 9。最大流？
== 13
---
程式跑 Edmonds–Karp 得 13。驗證用割：$S=\{s,a,b,c\}$ 的割容量＝b→t 4＋c→t 9＝13。

@q mf2-12 | al-mst-flow#2 | 3 | 計算
同一張網路，下列割的容量各是多少：$S=\{s\}$、$S=\{s,a,b\}$、$S=\{s,a,b,c\}$？
== 20,14,13
---
$\{s\}$：10+10；$\{s,a,b\}$：a→c 6＋b→c 4＋b→t 4；$\{s,a,b,c\}$：4＋9。最小的 13 等於最大流（最大流最小割）。

@q mf2-13 | al-mst-flow#2 | 2 | 觀念
擴增路徑上的瓶頸是什麼？
[x] 路徑上殘餘容量最小的那條邊，決定這次能推多少流
[ ] 容量最大的邊
[ ] 第一條邊
[ ] 最後一條邊

推完之後瓶頸邊的殘餘容量變 0。

@q mf2-14 | al-mst-flow#2 | 3 | 陷阱
邊 a→b 容量 5，現在流 3。殘餘網路裡 a→b 和 b→a 的容量各是？
== 2,3
---
還能多推 2；可以「退回」3（反向邊）。反向邊讓演算法能修正之前不好的選擇。

@q mf2-15 | al-mst-flow#2 | 2 | 情境
A power company models its grid as a directed network from the plant $s$ to the city $t$. Each line is listed with the most power it can carry, and this number is also the cost of cutting it: $s \to a$: 3, $s \to b$: 2, $a \to b$: 1, $a \to t$: 2, $b \to t$: 3. The company wants the cheapest set of lines whose removal leaves no directed path from $s$ to $t$. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The maximum flow value from $s$ to $t$ is 5.
[x] The minimum total cost of lines to cut is 5.
[x] Cutting the lines $s \to b$, $a \to b$, and $a \to t$ disconnects $t$ from $s$ at the minimum cost.
[ ] This network has exactly one minimum cut.
[ ] Cutting the two cheapest lines, $a \to b$ and $s \to b$, already leaves no path from $s$ to $t$.
---
「最大流 5」對：$s\to a$ 送 3（其中 2 走 $a\to t$、1 走 $a\to b$），$s\to b$ 送 2，$b\to t$ 收 3，總共 5；而 $s$ 送出去的容量只有 $3+2=5$，不可能更多。

「最便宜要 5」對：最大流最小割定理，最小割的容量＝最大流。

「切 $s\to b$、$a\to b$、$a\to t$ 是最小割」對：這是 $S=\{s,a\}$ 的割，$2+1+2=5$。

「最小割唯一」錯：$S=\{s\}$（切 $s\to a$、$s\to b$）、$S=\{s,a\}$、$S=\{s,a,b\}$（切 $a\to t$、$b\to t$）三個都是 5。

「切最便宜的兩條就夠」錯：只花 3，但 $s\to a\to t$ 還在。挑最便宜的邊不等於最小割，要看割是否真的把 $s$、$t$ 分開。

@q mf2-16 | al-mst-flow#2 | 2 | 計算
容量都是整數時，Ford–Fulkerson 每次至少增加多少流？最大流是 $f^*$ 時最多擴增幾次？
== 1,f*
?? 每次至少,最多次數
---
所以時間 $O(E\cdot f^*)$，跟流量數值有關（偽多項式）。Edmonds–Karp 用 BFS 改成 $O(VE^2)$，跟容量大小無關。

@q mf2-17 | al-mst-flow#2 | 2 | 找錯
A network has the edges $s \to a$, $a \to t$, $s \to b$, $b \to t$, and $a \to b$, each able to carry 1 unit of flow. A student implements Ford–Fulkerson but searches for augmenting paths using only the original edges, never the reverse (residual) edges. The network has 4 vertices and 5 edges, and the student's code is tested on it first. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The maximum flow value of this network is 2.
[x] If the student's first augmenting path is $s \to a \to b \to t$, no further augmenting path exists among the original edges, so the student stops with a flow of 1.
[x] With the reverse edge $b \to a$ in the residual network, the path $s \to b \to a \to t$ can be found, which raises the flow to 2.
[ ] Ignoring reverse edges always gives the maximum flow; it only makes the algorithm slower.
[ ] Using reverse edges may produce a flow that sends more than 1 unit through some edge.
---
「最大流 2」對：$s\to a\to t$ 和 $s\to b\to t$ 各送 1；$s$ 只有兩條出邊，不可能更多。

「先走 $s\to a\to b\to t$ 就卡在 1」對：之後 $s\to a$、$b\to t$ 都滿了，只看原本的邊時，$s\to b$ 之後只能走 $b\to t$（滿了），找不到路。

「有反向邊就能找到 $s\to b\to a\to t$」對：走 $b\to a$ 等於把原本 $a\to b$ 那 1 單位「退回」，改走 $a\to t$；結果是兩條獨立路徑，流量 2。

「不考慮反向邊也一定最大」錯：上面就是反例。反向邊讓演算法可以修正一開始選錯的路。

「反向邊會超過容量」錯：反向邊的可用量正好等於目前已送的量，退回的不會超過送出的，每條邊仍在限制內。

@q mf2-18 | al-mst-flow#2 | 3 | 證明
說明最大流演算法結束時（殘餘網路沒有 $s$ 到 $t$ 的路），流值等於某個割的容量。
---
令 $S$＝殘餘網路中 $s$ 走得到的點，$t\notin S$。跨 $S\to T$ 的原邊都滿載（否則殘餘還有容量，可以走過去）；跨 $T\to S$ 的原邊流量都是 0（否則有反向殘餘邊）。所以淨流＝$S\to T$ 容量總和＝割容量。又任何流 ≤ 任何割，所以它是最大流、這個割是最小割。

@q mf2-19 | al-mst-flow#2 | 1 | 觀念
流量網路中，「流量守恆」這條限制是指？
[x] 除了 $s$ 和 $t$，每個點流進的總量等於流出的總量
[ ] 每條邊的流量都相同
[ ] $s$ 流出的等於每條邊的容量
[ ] 流量不能超過 1
---
另一條限制是每條邊的流量不超過容量。

@q mf3-09 | al-mst-flow#3 | 2 | 計算
工人 1、2、3、4，工作 A、B、C、D。能做的：1–A、1–B、2–A、3–B、3–C、4–C、4–D。最大匹配？
== 4
---
1–B、2–A、3–C、4–D 剛好完美匹配。程式窮舉驗算。

@q mf3-10 | al-mst-flow#3 | 3 | 計算
工人 1、2、3、4，能做的：1–A、2–A、3–A、3–B、4–B、4–C。最大匹配？用 Hall 條件說明為什麼不能 4 個都配到。
== 3
---
工人 {1,2} 只認識 {A}，鄰居數 1 < 2，違反 Hall 條件，1 和 2 最多一個配到。最大匹配例如 1–A、3–B、4–C。

@q mf3-11 | al-mst-flow#3 | 2 | 觀念
二分圖轉最大流做匹配後，為什麼最大流的值就是最大匹配數？
[x] 容量都 1、流是整數，每個左點最多流出 1、右點最多流入 1，流過的中間邊就是一組匹配
[ ] 因為圖是二分的
[ ] 流量可以是分數
[ ] 不一定相等
---
整數容量保證存在整數最大流（演算法每次推整數）。

@q mf3-12 | al-mst-flow#3 | 3 | 陷阱
König's theorem says that in a bipartite graph, the size of a maximum matching equals the size of a minimum vertex cover. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[ ] The equality also holds for the triangle $K_3$.
[x] In $K_3$, a maximum matching has size 1 and a minimum vertex cover has size 2.
[x] In the complete bipartite graph $K_{2,3}$, both sizes are 2.
[x] In the cycle of 5 vertices, a maximum matching has size 2 and a minimum vertex cover has size 3.
[ ] In every graph, a minimum vertex cover is no larger than a maximum matching.
---
「$K_3$ 也成立」錯：$K_3$ 的最大匹配是 1、最小點覆蓋是 2，不相等；這個等式只在二分圖成立。

「$K_3$：1 和 2」對。

「$K_{2,3}$：都是 2」對：左邊 2 個點就覆蓋所有邊，也能配出 2 對。

「5 點環：2 和 3」對：奇數環，最多配 2 對；2 個點最多蓋 4 條邊，要 3 個點。

「點覆蓋 $\le$ 匹配」錯：方向反了；一般圖只有「最大匹配 $\le$ 最小點覆蓋」。

@q mf3-13 | al-mst-flow#3 | 2 | 情境
選課系統：每門課有名額上限，每個學生最多選 3 門、只能選自己有興趣的課。要讓選上的總人次最大。怎麼建流量網路？
[x] s→每個學生容量 3、學生→有興趣的課容量 1、每門課→t 容量＝名額
[ ] 每條邊容量都是 1
[ ] s→課、課→學生
[ ] 用最小生成樹
---
這是二分匹配加上容量的推廣（$b$-matching）。

@q mf3-14 | al-mst-flow#3 | 2 | 計算
$s$ 到 $t$ 之間，要切斷幾條邊才能讓 $s$ 到不了 $t$？若 $s$ 到 $t$ 最多有 3 條邊不相交路徑，答案是？
== 3
---
Menger 定理：最少要切的邊數＝最多邊不相交路徑數（每條邊容量 1 的最大流＝最小割）。

@q mf3-15 | al-mst-flow#3 | 3 | 找錯
In a flow network, each vertex $v$ other than $s$ and $t$ may pass at most $c(v)$ units of flow. A student just writes these limits next to the vertices and runs an ordinary maximum-flow program. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The standard fix splits each such vertex $v$ into $v_{\text{in}}$ and $v_{\text{out}}$ joined by an edge $v_{\text{in}} \to v_{\text{out}}$ with limit $c(v)$.
[x] After the split, every edge that entered $v$ enters $v_{\text{in}}$, and every edge that left $v$ leaves $v_{\text{out}}$.
[ ] An ordinary maximum-flow program enforces vertex limits automatically.
[x] The split doubles the number of such vertices and adds one edge per split vertex.
[ ] Lowering the limit of every edge entering $v$ to $c(v)$ enforces the vertex limit.
---
「拆成進點和出點，中間一條上限 $c(v)$ 的邊」對：所有經過 $v$ 的流都要走這條邊。

「進來的接 $v_{\text{in}}$、出去的接 $v_{\text{out}}$」對。

「一般程式自動處理」錯：一般最大流只看邊的上限。

「點數加倍、多一條邊」對：規模只多一點點。

「把每條進來的邊都降到 $c(v)$」錯：好幾條進來的邊加起來仍可能超過 $c(v)$。

@q mf3-16 | al-mst-flow#3 | 2 | 計算
二分圖左邊 5 個點、右邊 4 個點，最大匹配最多可能是多少？
== 4
---
每個匹配用掉一個右點，最多 $\min(5,4)$。

@q mf3-17 | al-mst-flow#3 | 3 | 證明
用 Hall 條件說明：每個點度數都是 $d\ge1$ 的二分圖（左右各 $n$ 點），一定有完美匹配。
---
任取左邊的集合 $S$，從 $S$ 出去的邊有 $d|S|$ 條。這些邊都落在 $N(S)$，而 $N(S)$ 每個點最多接 $d$ 條，所以 $d|N(S)|\ge d|S|$，即 $|N(S)|\ge|S|$。Hall 條件成立，有蓋住左邊所有點的匹配，左右點數相同所以是完美匹配。

@q pb1-11 | al-prob#1 | 2 | 計算
雇用問題 6 個應徵者隨機順序來，每次遇到比目前最好的還好就雇用。期望雇用幾次？
== 2.45
== 49/20
---
第 $i$ 個是前 $i$ 個中最好的機會是 $1/i$，加總 $1+\frac12+\cdots+\frac16=\frac{49}{20}$。

@q pb1-12 | al-prob#1 | 2 | 計算

== 2.14
== 2.137
---
每一對同天生日的機會 $\frac1{365}$，共 $\binom{40}2=780$ 對：$780/365$。

@q pb1-13 | al-prob#1 | 2 | 情境

== 1.9

$\binom{20}2\cdot\frac1{100}=190/100$。就算格子遠多於資料，碰撞也很常見（生日問題）。

@q pb1-14 | al-prob#1 | 3 | 計算

== 14.7
---
已經有 $k$ 種時，拿到新的機率 $\frac{6-k}6$，期望要 $\frac6{6-k}$ 次。加總 $6(1+\frac12+\cdots+\frac16)=14.7$。

@q pb1-16 | al-prob#1 | 3 | 陷阱
A board game moves a token by the sum $X$ of two fair six-sided dice, and its designer wants both the average move and how much the moves vary. The designer computes $E[X]$ by linearity, and then tries to compute $E[X^2]$ by squaring $E[X]$, arguing that expectation is linear and that the two dice are independent. A playtest of 3600 rolls gives an average move of 7.02 and an average squared move of 54.7, and the designer wants to know which of the two calculations matches the playtest. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] $E[X]=7$.
[x] $E[X^2]=49+\frac{35}{6}$, which is larger than $(E[X])^2$.
[x] $E[X^2]-(E[X])^2$ is the variance of $X$.
[ ] $E[X^2]=(E[X])^2$, because expectation is linear.
[ ] $E[X^2]=(E[X])^2$, because the two dice are independent.
---
「$E[X]=7$」。線性性只適用於加法和乘常數，$X^2$ 不是線性的；「$E[X^2]=7^2+\frac{35}6$」，多出來的正是「變異數」。兩顆骰子獨立也不會讓 $X$ 和自己獨立，所以兩個「相等」的理由都不成立。

@q pb1-17 | al-prob#1 | 2 | 找錯
A card magician shuffles a deck of $n$ cards uniformly at random and counts the fixed points, the cards that end in their original positions. To find the expected number of fixed points, a student writes $X = \sum_{i=1}^{n} X_i$, where $X_i$ is the indicator that card $i$ stays in position $i$, and then says: "The events 'card $i$ stays in position $i$' are not independent, because if cards 1 to $n-1$ all stay, card $n$ must stay too, so linearity of expectation cannot be used." For $n = 3$, the 6 possible orders have 3, 1, 1, 1, 0, and 0 fixed points. The student also wonders whether the answer depends on the size of the deck, and whether the dependence between the events matters for anything else, such as the spread of $X$. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] Linearity of expectation does not require independence.
[x] Each card stays in place with probability $\frac1n$.
[x] The expected number of fixed points is $n\cdot\frac1n=1$.
[x] The dependence would matter for computing the variance, not the expectation.
[x] The expected number is the same for every $n\ge1$.
---
「線性不需要獨立」，每張牌「留在原位的機率 $\frac1n$」，加起來「期望 1」，「和 $n$ 無關」。相關性要等算變異數時才需要處理（要用到兩兩的聯合機率）。五句都對。

@q pb1-18 | al-prob#1 | 3 | 計算
某個每次隨機挑一個元素來切分的排序法中，排好後位置 $i$ 和位置 $j$（$i<j$）的兩個元素會被比較的機率是 $\frac2{j-i+1}$。$n=4$ 時期望比較次數？
== 4.83
== 29/6
---
所有配對：差 1 的 3 對各 $1$、差 2 的 2 對各 $\frac23$、差 3 的 1 對 $\frac12$：$3+\frac43+\frac12=\frac{29}6$。一般是 $O(n\lg n)$。

@q pb1-19 | al-prob#1 | 2 | 觀念
指示隨機變數 $I_A$ 的定義和期望值？
[x] $A$ 發生時是 1、否則 0；$E[I_A]=\Pr[A]$
[ ] $A$ 發生的次數；$E=1$
[ ] $A$ 的機率；$E=0$
[ ] 永遠是 1
---
把複雜的計數寫成很多 $I$ 的和，再用線性性。

@q pb2-09 | al-prob#2 | 2 | 計算

== 1/81
== 0.0123
---
$(1/3)^4$。每多跑一次，錯誤率乘上 $\frac13$。

@q pb2-10 | al-prob#2 | 2 | 計算
Las Vegas 演算法每次嘗試成功的機率 $\frac12$，失敗就重來。10 次之內成功的機率？
== 0.999
== 1023/1024
---
$1-(1/2)^{10}$。時間雖然隨機，但很快就幾乎一定結束。

@q pb2-11 | al-prob#2 | 2 | 比較
Which of the following statements about randomized quicksort are correct?
[x] It is a Las Vegas algorithm.
[x] Its expected running time is $O(n\log n)$ for every input.
[ ] Its worst-case running time is $O(n\log n)$, because the pivot is chosen at random.
[ ] It is a Monte Carlo algorithm, since it occasionally outputs an unsorted array.
[ ] Its expected time is $O(n\log n)$ only for inputs given in random order.
---
結果一定排好、只有時間隨機，是「Las Vegas」；「期望 $O(n\log n)$ 對每個輸入都成立」。隨機選 pivot 運氣很差時仍是 $\Theta(n^2)$，所以最壞不是 $n\log n$；它不會輸出沒排好的結果；期望來自自己的隨機選擇，不需要輸入是隨機的。

@q pb2-12 | al-prob#2 | 3 | 計算
A faulty shuffle of 3 cards swaps each position $i$ (for $i=1,2,3$) with a position chosen uniformly at random from all three. Which of the following statements are correct?
[x] There are $3^3=27$ equally likely executions.
[x] Since 27 is not a multiple of $3!=6$, the 6 orderings cannot all have the same probability.
[x] Fisher–Yates has $3\cdot2\cdot1=6$ equally likely executions, each producing a different ordering.
[ ] The shuffle is fair, because every card can end up in every position.
[ ] Since 27 is odd, at least one ordering must have probability 0.
---
每個位置 3 種選擇，「27 種等可能的過程」；「27 不是 6 的倍數」，不可能均勻（實際三種各 5 次、三種各 4 次）。「Fisher–Yates」第 $i$ 步只從還沒定的位置選，6 種過程各對一種排法。每張牌能到每個位置不代表均勻；每種排法都出現 4 或 5 次，沒有機率 0 的。

@q pb2-13 | al-prob#2 | 2 | 觀念
A Las Vegas algorithm is modified so that, if it has not finished within a fixed time limit, it stops and outputs a guess. Which of the following statements are correct?
[x] The modified algorithm is a Monte Carlo algorithm.
[x] Its running time is now bounded, but its answer may be wrong.
[x] Conversely, a Monte Carlo algorithm whose answers can be checked can be rerun until correct.
[x] A longer time limit cannot increase the chance of returning a guess.
[ ] The modified algorithm is still a Las Vegas algorithm, since it was built from one.
---
「時間有上限、答案可能錯」就是「Monte Carlo」；反過來「能檢查答案的 Monte Carlo 重跑到對」就是 Las Vegas。時間上限越長，跑不完而只好猜的機率只會變小或不變。改完以後已經可能答錯，不再是 Las Vegas。

@q pb2-14 | al-prob#2 | 3 | 情境
Two remote machines want to check whether their large files are identical without sending a whole file. One machine picks a random prime $p$ and sends it, and both compare their files' values modulo $p$ (fingerprints). Which of the following statements are correct?
[x] If the fingerprints differ, the files are certainly different.
[x] If the fingerprints match, the files are identical with high probability.
[x] Repeating with several independently chosen primes lowers the error probability.
[x] Only the prime and a fingerprint need to be sent, not the file.
[x] This is a Monte Carlo method with one-sided error.
---
相同的檔案取模後一定相同，所以「指紋不同就確定不同」；「指紋相同只是很可能相同」，錯誤只出在這一邊，是「單邊錯誤的 Monte Carlo」。「多試幾個質數」能壓低錯誤率；傳的只有「質數和指紋」。五句都對。

@q pb2-15 | al-prob#2 | 2 | 找錯
學生的洗牌：「對每個位置 $i$，從**整副牌**隨機挑一張跟它交換」，說這等於 Fisher–Yates。錯在哪？
[x] Fisher–Yates 第 $i$ 步只能從還沒處理的位置挑；從整副挑會讓某些排列出現得比較多
[ ] 沒錯
[ ] 應該從已處理的位置挑
[ ] 應該挑兩次
---
程式模擬 3 張牌可以看出有些排列機率是 5/27、有些是 4/27。

@q pb2-16 | al-prob#2 | 3 | 計算
Monte Carlo 判斷「是」的時候一定對、判斷「否」時有 $\frac12$ 機率錯（單邊錯誤）。要讓錯誤機率 ≤ $10^{-6}$，至少要獨立跑幾次（只要有一次說「是」就回答是）？
== 20
---
$(1/2)^k\le10^{-6}$ ⇒ $k\ge\lg10^6\approx19.9$。

@q pb2-17 | al-prob#2 | 2 | 觀念
Which of the following statements about the expected running time of a randomized algorithm are correct?
?? 複選
[x] The expectation is over the algorithm's own random choices, and the bound holds for every input.
[ ] The expectation is over a random input, so particular inputs may be much slower on average.
[ ] The bound holds only when the input is drawn uniformly at random.
[ ] The bound describes the worst case over all of the algorithm's random choices.
[ ] The expectation is taken over all the machines the algorithm might run on.
---
期望是「對演算法自己的隨機選擇取平均，對每個輸入都成立」，沒有特別壞的輸入。對輸入取平均是平均情況分析；最壞的隨機選擇是另一件事（例如隨機快排運氣差仍是 $n^2$）；和機器無關。

== 0,1,0,1,2,2,3
---
逐位：a 0；aa 1；aab 0；aaba 1；aabaa 2；aabaaa 2（aa）；aabaaab 3（aab）。程式驗算。

@q pb3-11 | al-prob#3 | 2 | 計算

@q pb3-12 | al-prob#3 | 3 | 追蹤
KMP 用 $P=$ abcab（$\pi=0,0,0,1,2$）比對，已經比對成功 5 個字元（整個 abcab），接著找下一次出現時 $q$ 要退到多少？
== 2
---
$q=\pi[5]=2$：已比對的 abcab 的後綴 ab 就是 $P$ 的前綴，不用重比。

@q pb3-13 | al-prob#3 | 2 | 觀念
KMP searches a text of length $n$, and $q$ denotes the number of pattern characters currently matched. Which of the following statements are correct?
[x] The position in the text never moves backward.
[x] $q$ increases by at most 1 per text character, so by at most $n$ in total.
[x] Each mismatch with $q>0$ decreases $q$, so there are at most $n$ such mismatches.
[x] The search phase makes at most about $2n$ comparisons.
[ ] The values of the failure function are always small, which is why the search is linear.
---
「文字指標只往前」，每前進一格「$q$ 最多加 1」，總共最多加 $n$；「比對失敗讓 $q$ 變小」，減少的總量不可能超過增加的總量，所以失敗最多 $n$ 次，「總比較次數約 $2n$」。這是攤銷分析；失敗函數的值可以很大，不是原因。

@q pb3-14 | al-prob#3 | 2 | 計算
Rabin–Karp 把視窗當十進位數再取模 13。視窗「31」的值是 5。往右移一格，新視窗是「14」，新的值？
== 1
---
$(5-3\cdot10)\cdot10+4=-246$，$-246\bmod13=1$。驗算：$14\bmod13=1$。

@q pb3-15 | al-prob#3 | 3 | 陷阱
In Rabin–Karp with modulus $q$, a window's value modulo $q$ equals the pattern's value modulo $q$. With text length $n$ and pattern length $m$, which of the following statements are correct?
[x] The window is not necessarily a match; the characters must still be compared.
[x] In the worst case every window is a spurious hit, giving $O(nm)$ time.
[ ] With a large prime $q$, the worst-case time becomes $O(n+m)$.
[ ] Once $q$ exceeds the alphabet size, equal values modulo $q$ always mean a match.
[ ] The window is always a match whenever the values are equal and $q$ is prime.
---
取模後相同可能只是碰撞，「要逐字確認」；最壞「每個視窗都是假警報」，$O(nm)$。大質數只讓期望時間變成 $O(n+m)$，最壞情況不變；模數再大、是不是質數，碰撞都不可能是零。

@q pb3-16 | al-prob#3 | 2 | 情境
We must find, in one long article, every occurrence of any of 1000 different keywords, all of the same length $m$. Which of the following statements are correct?
[x] Rabin–Karp extends easily: store the 1000 keyword values modulo $q$ in a lookup table.
[x] Aho–Corasick, a multi-pattern version of KMP, also handles all keywords in one pass.
[ ] Running KMP once per keyword still scans the article only once in total.
[ ] Rabin–Karp needs the keywords to have different lengths to share a single pass.
[ ] Naive search is the most efficient choice when there are many keywords.
---
長度都一樣時，每個視窗的值「查一次表」就知道有沒有對到任何關鍵字，Rabin–Karp 很好擴充；「Aho–Corasick」也是一遍掃完。KMP 一次只處理一個模式，要掃 1000 遍；長度相同正是 Rabin–Karp 能共用一遍的條件；樸素比對最慢。

@q pb3-17 | al-prob#3 | 2 | 找錯
While computing the KMP failure function, a student lets the whole string count as a usable prefix, so that $\pi[q]$ may equal $q$. Which of the following statements are correct?
[x] On a mismatch, $q$ falls back to $\pi[q]=q$ and does not decrease, so the search can get stuck.
[x] The definition requires the longest proper prefix that is also a suffix.
[x] With the correct definition, $\pi[q]<q$ for every $q\ge1$.
[x] So $q$ strictly decreases at each fallback.
[ ] Allowing the whole string only adds 1 to every value, which the search later subtracts again.
---
整個字串一定既是前綴又是後綴，若允許它，「$\pi[q]=q$，$q$ 退不下去」，KMP 卡住。定義是「真前綴」，所以「$\pi[q]<q$」，「每次退回都嚴格變小」。四句都對。允許整串時值會直接變成 $q$，不是每個值加 1。

@q pb3-18 | al-prob#3 | 1 | 計算
樸素比對，文字長 1000、樣式長 10，最多要比較幾個字元？
== 9910
---
$(n-m+1)\cdot m=991\times10$。最壞情況例如文字全 a、樣式 aaaaaaaaab。

@q np1-11 | al-np#1 | 2 | 陷阱
「NP」是哪幾個字的縮寫？
[x] Nondeterministic Polynomial（非確定性多項式時間）
[ ] Not Polynomial
[ ] New Problem
[ ] Non-Parallel
---
「不是多項式」是最常見的誤解；NP 包含 P。

@q np1-12 | al-np#1 | 3 | 陷阱
Recall that EVEN-SPLIT asks whether $n$ given weights can be divided into two parts of equal total, that a proposed division can be checked with at most $n$ additions, and that EVEN-SPLIT is NP-complete. Suppose a paper proves that EVEN-SPLIT cannot be solved in $O(n^k)$ steps for any constant $k$; that is, no algorithm runs in time $n^2$, $n^{10}$, $n^{100}$, or any other polynomial. Assume the proof is correct. Which of the following statements follow? Select all that apply.
[x] P ≠ NP.
[x] No NP-complete problem is in P.
[x] No NP-hard problem is in P.
[x] Every NP-complete problem then requires more than polynomial time.
[x] The proof would settle the P versus NP question.
---
「P ≠ NP」對、「P 對 NP 有了答案」對：EVEN-SPLIT 在 NP 卻不在 P。

「沒有 NP-complete 在 P」對、「每個 NP-complete 都需要超過多項式時間」對：NP-complete 問題彼此可以在多項式時間互相轉換，任一個在 P，EVEN-SPLIT 也會在 P，矛盾。

「沒有 NP-hard 在 P」對：NP-hard 問題若在 P，所有 NP 問題（包括 EVEN-SPLIT）都能先轉換再解，也矛盾。五句都對。

@q np1-13 | al-np#1 | 2 | 觀念
Consider three problems. SORTED: given a list of $n$ integers, is it in increasing order? HALT: given the source code of a program of, say, 200 lines and an input of 10 numbers, does the program eventually stop? EVEN-SPLIT: can $n$ given weights be divided into two parts of equal total? A list of 1000 integers can be checked for SORTED with 999 comparisons. Every problem in NP can be transformed in polynomial time into HALT. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
?? 複選
[x] HALT is NP-hard but not in NP.
[ ] SORTED is NP-hard but not in NP.
[ ] Every NP-hard problem is in NP, by the definition of NP-hardness.
[ ] HALT is in NP, since a halting run of the program serves as a certificate.
[ ] HALT is not NP-hard, because it is undecidable.
---
「HALT 是 NP-hard 但不在 NP」對：任何 NP 問題都能轉成它，所以 NP-hard；它不可判定，所以不在 NP。

「SORTED 是 NP-hard」錯：它在 P（$n-1$ 次比較），當然在 NP。

「NP-hard 都在 NP」錯：定義不要求自己在 NP，HALT 就是反例。

「停機的執行當證據」錯：程式可能跑很久才停，執行過程不一定是多項式長度，不能在多項式時間驗證。

「不可判定所以不是 NP-hard」錯：不可判定反而更難，不影響它是 NP-hard。

@q np1-14 | al-np#1 | 2 | 計算
驗證一個 $n$ 個變數、$m$ 個子句的 CNF 公式在某個賦值下是否為真，要多少時間？
[x] $O(n+m\cdot k)$（$k$ 是子句長度）：逐個子句檢查有沒有一個文字為真
[ ] $O(2^n)$
[ ] $O(n!)$
[ ] 不能驗證
---
「容易驗證」正是 NP 的特徵；困難的是**找到**那個賦值。

@q np1-15 | al-np#1 | 2 | 找錯
學生說：「我能把排序問題在多項式時間轉成布林公式可滿足問題，所以排序是 NP-hard。」錯在哪？
[x] 方向反了：把簡單的問題轉成難的問題什麼都沒證明；要證 $X$ 難，要把已知難的問題轉成 $X$
[ ] 沒錯
[ ] 排序真的是 NP-hard
[ ] 布林公式可滿足問題在 P 裡
---
$A\le_pB$ 表示 $B$ 至少跟 $A$ 一樣難。

@q np1-16 | al-np#1 | 3 | 比較
A delivery tour must visit 20 towns, and every road length is an integer from 1 to 1000, so every tour has total length between 20 and 20000. The decision version ROUTE($k$) asks whether some tour has length at most $k$; the optimization version asks for the shortest tour length. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] ROUTE($k$) is in NP, since a proposed tour can be checked by adding 20 lengths and comparing the sum with $k$.
[x] If ROUTE($k$) is solvable in polynomial time, 15 calls with halving ranges of $k$ find the shortest length.
[ ] The theory of NP is defined for problems that output a shortest tour rather than a yes or no answer.
[ ] The optimization version can always be solved with a single call to ROUTE($k$).
[ ] The optimization version is always easier than the decision version, since it asks for less.
---
「加 20 個長度再比較，所以在 NP」對：證據就是那條路線。

「15 次縮半找到最短長度」對：可能的答案有 $20000-20+1=19981$ 個，$2^{14}<19981\le2^{15}$，每次問一次 ROUTE($k$) 就能把範圍縮一半，15 次就夠。

「NP 是為輸出最短路線的問題定義的」錯：NP 只針對是／否問題，所以理論先研究判斷版本。

「問一次就夠」錯：一次只得到「有／沒有」，不知道最短是多少。

「最佳化版本比較容易」錯：它要給出具體答案，會解最佳化就能直接回答判斷版本，所以不會比較容易。

@q np1-17 | al-np#1 | 2 | 情境
你寫的程式要解一個 NP-complete 問題的大型實例。實務上常見的做法，下列哪個**不**合理？
[x] 繼續找一個保證最佳又保證多項式時間的演算法
[ ] 用能證明誤差上限的方法，接受不是最佳
[ ] 用啟發式或現成的最佳化求解器，接受不保證的時間
[ ] 利用實例的特殊結構（例如圖很稀疏、數字很小）
---
找到前者等於證明 P = NP。

@q np1-18 | al-np#1 | 2 | 觀念
Problem $A$ asks whether a list of $n$ integers contains the number 0. Problem $B$ asks whether the product of a list of integers equals 0. A student shows $A\le_pB$ with the transformation $f$ that outputs its input list unchanged; for example, $f([3, -1, 0, 7]) = [3, -1, 0, 7]$, a yes-instance of both problems. Which of the following conditions must a transformation used to show $A\le_pB$ satisfy? Select all that apply.
[x] It can be computed in polynomial time.
[x] If $x$ is a yes-instance of $A$, then $f(x)$ is a yes-instance of $B$.
[x] If $f(x)$ is a yes-instance of $B$, then $x$ is a yes-instance of $A$.
[x] It is allowed to map several different instances of $A$ to the same instance of $B$.
[ ] It is enough that yes-instances of $A$ map to yes-instances of $B$; no-instances may map anywhere.
---
「多項式時間可算」對：學生的 $f$ 只是複製一次，線性時間。

「是 ⇒ 是」對、「轉過去是 ⇒ 原本是」對：清單含 0 若且唯若乘積為 0，兩個方向都成立。

「可以多對一」對：轉換不需要一對一或映成，只要答案相同。

「只要是對應到是」錯：no-instance 可能被轉成 yes-instance，用 $B$ 的答案就判斷錯了；兩個方向都要。

@q np2-09 | al-np#2 | 2 | 計算

== 3

@q np2-11 | al-np#2 | 3 | 計算
3-CNF 公式有 5 個子句，歸約到 CLIQUE 時圖有幾個點？要找多大的團？
== 15,5
?? 點數,團大小
---
每個子句 3 個文字各一個點；不同子句、而且不互相矛盾的文字之間連邊。每個子句挑一個為真的文字，正好是大小 5 的團。

@q np2-12 | al-np#2 | 2 | 觀念
In the standard reduction from 3-CNF-SAT to CLIQUE, consider
$\varphi = (x_1 \lor \neg x_2 \lor x_3) \land (\neg x_1 \lor x_2 \lor x_3) \land (x_1 \lor x_2 \lor \neg x_3)$.
Each literal occurrence becomes one vertex, and two vertices are joined by an edge exactly when they come from different clauses and are not complementary (such as $x_1$ and $\neg x_1$). Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The graph has 9 vertices, and no edge joins two vertices that come from the same clause.
[x] The graph has exactly 21 edges.
[ ] The vertex $x_1$ of the first clause is adjacent to the vertex $\neg x_1$ of the second clause.
[x] $\varphi$ is satisfiable if and only if the graph has a clique of size 3.
[ ] The graph has a clique of size 4.
---
「9 個點、同子句不連」對：3 個子句各 3 個文字；同子句不連是為了讓團在每個子句裡最多挑一個。

「剛好 21 條邊」對：不同子句的點對有 $3\times(3\times3)=27$ 對，扣掉互補的 6 對：第一和第二子句 $x_1/\neg x_1$、$\neg x_2/x_2$；第一和第三 $\neg x_2/x_2$、$x_3/\neg x_3$；第二和第三 $\neg x_1/x_1$、$x_3/\neg x_3$。$27-6=21$。

「$x_1$ 和 $\neg x_1$ 相連」錯：互補的文字不連，保證團裡挑出的文字可以同時為真。

「可滿足若且唯若有大小 3 的團」對：$k$＝子句數 3；例如 $x_1=x_2=x_3=$ 真時，三個子句各挑一個真的文字，兩兩不互補，就是一個 3-團。

「有大小 4 的團」錯：同子句的點互不相連，所以團裡每個子句最多一個點，最大就是 3。

@q np2-13 | al-np#2 | 3 | 證明
證明 CLIQUE $\le_p$ 獨立集。
---
給 $(G,k)$，造補圖 $\bar G$（多項式時間，最多 $n^2$ 條邊）。$G$ 有大小 $k$ 的團 ⇔ 這 $k$ 點在 $G$ 兩兩相鄰 ⇔ 在 $\bar G$ 兩兩不相鄰 ⇔ $\bar G$ 有大小 $k$ 的獨立集。所以用獨立集的演算法解 $(\bar G,k)$ 就回答了 CLIQUE。

@q np2-14 | al-np#2 | 2 | 找錯
學生說：「獨立集的點是彼此不相鄰的，點覆蓋是要碰到每條邊，兩者沒關係。」錯在哪？
[x] 兩者互補：拿掉一個獨立集剩下的點，一定碰到每條邊（每條邊至少有一端不在獨立集）
[ ] 沒錯
[ ] 兩者相同
[ ] 點覆蓋是獨立集的子集合
---
所以最大獨立集和最小點覆蓋是同一個問題的兩面。

@q np2-15 | al-np#2 | 2 | 情境
A party host with 60 guests wants to seat the largest possible set of guests who all know one another at one table. The host models guests as vertices and acquaintances as edges; the guest list has 412 acquaintance pairs. A friend who studied algorithms suggests that the problem might be the same as finding the fewest guests who touch every acquaintance, or the largest set of mutual strangers, and that checking every pair of guests would quickly decide whether a set of 10 such guests exists. The host only has a laptop and 1 evening. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
?? 複選
[x] This is the maximum clique problem.
[ ] This is the minimum vertex cover problem, since every acquaintance must be covered.
[ ] Its decision version can be solved in polynomial time by checking every pair of guests.
[ ] Finding the largest set of guests no two of whom know each other is the same problem on the same graph.
[ ] This is the problem of finding the fewest introductions needed to connect two given guests.
---
「互相都認識」就是兩兩有邊，是「最大團」，判定版是 NP-complete，只看每一對不夠。點覆蓋是要碰到每條邊；「互不認識」的最大一群要在補圖上找團，在原圖上是另一個問題；最少介紹幾次是最短路徑。

@q np2-16 | al-np#2 | 3 | 陷阱
CLIQUE is known to be NP-complete. A student studies a new scheduling problem $X$ and constructs a polynomial-time reduction $X\le_p\text{CLIQUE}$: every instance of $X$ with $n$ jobs, $n$ up to 500, is turned, in $O(n^3)$ time and in about 2 seconds, into a graph and a number $k$, such that the instance of $X$ is a yes-instance exactly when the graph has a clique of size $k$. The student's report concludes that $X$ is therefore NP-complete. The advisor points out that the direction of a reduction matters, and asks the student what the reduction actually shows, what would be needed to prove that $X$ is NP-hard, and what would follow for $X$ if someone found a polynomial-time algorithm for CLIQUE. The report is due before the research meeting on Friday, and the advisor wants each claim justified. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] This shows that $X$ is in NP.
[x] This does not show that $X$ is NP-hard.
[x] To show that $X$ is NP-hard, one needs a reduction $\text{CLIQUE}\le_p X$.
[x] If CLIQUE turned out to be in P, then $X$ would also be in P.
[ ] This shows that $X$ is NP-complete.
---
$X\le_p$ CLIQUE 表示 $X$ 不比 CLIQUE 難：CLIQUE 在 NP，所以「$X$ 在 NP」；CLIQUE 若有快速解法，「$X$ 也有」。但這「不能說明 $X$ 難」，要「反方向 CLIQUE $\le_p X$」，所以還不能說 $X$ 是 NP-complete。

@q np2-17 | al-np#2 | 2 | 觀念
CLRS proves NP-completeness for a whole family of problems by a chain of polynomial-time reductions: once one problem is known to be NP-complete, others are shown NP-complete by reducing a known one to them. A student drawing the chain of 7 reductions for a review sheet of 8 problems must decide which problem stands at the very beginning, which 2 problems come right after it, and how that first problem is shown to be NP-complete when no earlier problem is available to reduce from. Classmates suggest SUBSET-SUM or graph connectivity as simpler starting points, since numbers and connected graphs feel more basic than circuits. Which of the following statements about where the CLRS chain of NP-completeness proofs begins are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] It starts from circuit satisfiability (CIRCUIT-SAT).
[x] CIRCUIT-SAT is shown NP-complete directly, rather than by a reduction from another NP-complete problem.
[ ] It starts from SUBSET-SUM, since numbers are the simplest objects to reason about.
[ ] CIRCUIT-SAT is shown NP-complete by a reduction from 3-CNF-SAT.
[ ] It starts from graph connectivity, which is the simplest graph problem.
---
第一個 NP-complete 問題沒有別的問題可以歸約過來，只能「直接證明」：任何 NP 問題的驗證程序都能寫成電路，所以「從 Circuit-SAT 開始」，之後才 SAT → 3-CNF-SAT → …。3-CNF-SAT 在它後面；SUBSET-SUM 在鏈的後段；連通性在 P。

@q np3-08 | al-np#3 | 2 | 比較
歐拉迴路和漢米頓迴路，各是？
[x] 歐拉迴路在 P（看度數就知道）；漢米頓迴路是 NP-complete
[ ] 都在 P
[ ] 都是 NP-complete
[ ] 歐拉迴路是 NP-complete
---
「每條邊走一次」很簡單；「每個點走一次」就很難。

@q np3-09 | al-np#3 | 2 | 比較
Assume P ≠ NP. A telecom company must connect cities with cables at minimum total cost, where the possible cables and their costs form a weighted graph with 300 vertices. In problem (i), all 300 cities must be connected, so the company wants a minimum-weight tree that connects all vertices. In problem (ii), only 40 of the cities are customers and must be connected, but the tree may also pass through other cities as relay points if that lowers the cost; this is the Steiner tree problem. An engineer believes that both problems should be equally easy, since both ask for a tree of minimum weight, and another believes that problem (ii) is easier because it connects fewer vertices and therefore has fewer choices to make. The company needs an exact answer, not an estimate, for both problems this year. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] Problem (i) is in P.
[x] Problem (ii) is NP-hard.
[x] The freedom to choose which extra vertices to use is what makes (ii) hard.
[ ] Both problems are in P, since both ask for a tree of minimum weight.
[ ] Problem (i) is NP-hard, while problem (ii) is in P because it connects fewer vertices.
---
連通所有點的就是最小生成樹，「(i) 在 P」；只連指定的點、可以借用其他點的 Steiner 樹「(ii) 是 NP-hard」，難在「要不要借用哪些點」的選擇。都是找最小樹不代表一樣難；點比較少也不會比較容易。

@q np3-10 | al-np#3 | 2 | 比較
Assume P ≠ NP. A university must schedule 500 exams into time slots so that no two exams with a common student are at the same time; exams are vertices, conflicts are edges, and colors are time slots. With only 2 slots available, a student proposes running BFS from each uncolored vertex and alternating colors layer by layer, reporting failure if some edge joins two vertices of the same color. With 3 slots, the same student hopes that the extra color makes the problem easier, and asks which graphs can be 2-colored at all. Which of the following statements about graph coloring are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] Deciding whether a graph can be properly colored with 2 colors is in P.
[x] A graph can be properly 2-colored exactly when it has no odd cycle.
[x] Deciding whether a graph can be properly colored with 3 colors is NP-complete.
[x] BFS layering checks 2-colorability in $O(V+E)$ time.
[ ] Deciding 3-colorability is in P, since 3 colors give more freedom than 2.
---
2-著色就是判斷二分圖：「沒有奇環」⇔ 可以 2-著色，用「BFS 分層」線性時間檢查，「在 P」。「3-著色是 NP-complete」：顏色多了選擇多，反而更難判斷。跟 2-SAT／3-SAT 一樣，從 2 到 3 跨過了難度的分界。

@q np3-11 | al-np#3 | 3 | 觀念
A strongly NP-complete problem stays NP-complete even when all the numbers in its input are bounded by a polynomial in the input size, or, equivalently, when the numbers are written in unary; a weakly NP-complete problem has a pseudo-polynomial algorithm, whose running time is polynomial in the input size and the magnitude of the numbers. For example, knapsack with $n = 100$ and weight limit $W = 10^5$ is solved in about $10^7$ steps by its $O(nW)$ algorithm, while 3-PARTITION has no such algorithm unless P = NP. A student compares the two classes and asks which of them a better algorithm for small numbers could help, and whether either class is easier in general, or has no algorithm at all for them. Which of the following statements comparing strongly NP-complete problems (such as 3-PARTITION) with weakly NP-complete problems (such as knapsack) are correct? Select all that apply.
?? 複選
[x] A strongly NP-complete problem remains hard even when its numbers are written in unary.
[ ] Strongly NP-complete problems have pseudo-polynomial algorithms, just like weakly NP-complete ones.
[ ] Knapsack is strongly NP-complete, since its $O(nW)$ algorithm is exponential in the bit length.
[ ] Strongly NP-complete problems are easier than weakly NP-complete problems.
[ ] Weakly NP-complete problems have no algorithm at all.
---
強 NP-complete「用 unary 寫數字還是難」，所以除非 P = NP 否則沒有偽多項式演算法。背包有 $O(nW)$ 的偽多項式演算法，正是弱 NP-complete 的標誌；強的比較難；弱的有演算法，只是一般不是多項式。

@q np3-12 | al-np#3 | 2 | 比較
線性規劃和整數線性規劃？
[x] 線性規劃在 P（橢球法、內點法）；整數線性規劃是 NP-hard
[ ] 都在 P
[ ] 都是 NP-hard
[ ] 線性規劃是 NP-hard
---
限制變數是整數就讓問題變難；很多 NP-hard 問題都能寫成整數規劃。

@q np3-13 | al-np#3 | 2 | 計算
2-SAT 怎麼在多項式時間解？
[x] 每個子句 $(a\lor b)$ 變成兩條蘊含邊 $\neg a\to b$、$\neg b\to a$，找出「互相走得到」的分群；某變數和它的否定在同一群就無解
[ ] 窮舉所有賦值
[ ] 用 DP
[ ] 不能
---
$O(n+m)$。3-SAT 的子句沒辦法這樣拆成兩兩的蘊含。

@q np3-14 | al-np#3 | 3 | 陷阱
「最短簡單路徑在 P，所以把權重取負，最長簡單路徑也在 P」對嗎？
[ ] 對
[x] 不對
---
取負後會出現負環，最短**簡單**路徑在有負環的圖上本身就是 NP-hard（等於找最長路徑）。只有 DAG 上兩者都簡單。

@q np3-15 | al-np#3 | 2 | 情境
Model four courses as vertices 1 through 4. Courses 1,2,3 interference pairwise, and course 4 interferences only with course 3. An edge means its endpoints cannot share a time slot. Minimize the number of slots, with no extra room or instructor constraints. Use this instance to test the claim that pairwise interferences always allow two slots, then consider unrestricted interference graphs versus special graph families. Assume P differs from NP. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] Finding the minimum number of time slots is NP-hard in general.
[x] On special structures such as interval graphs, the minimum number of colors can be found quickly.
[ ] Two time slots are always enough, since interferences always come in pairs.
[ ] Some fast algorithm finds the minimum number of time slots for any timetable.
[ ] Choosing the locally best slot for each course always gives the minimum number of slots.
---
一般情況下「求最少時段數是 NP-hard」，實務上用啟發式；「區間圖等特殊結構」可以快速求出。衝突雖然是兩兩定義，有奇環時兩個時段就不夠；每次挑當下最好的方法只是啟發式，不保證最少。

@q np4-09 | al-np#4 | 2 | 計算
點覆蓋的「挑邊加兩端」近似：圖是星形（中心連 5 個葉子）。演算法可能得到多大的覆蓋？最佳是多少？
== 2,1
?? 演算法,最佳
---
挑任一條邊加入中心和一片葉子，之後所有邊都被蓋住：2；最佳只要中心 1 個。比值剛好 2。

@q np4-10 | al-np#4 | 2 | 觀念
Use the approximation-ratio convention rho at least 1, comparing optimum divided by returned value for maximization. An instance has optimum 100 and a feasible returned value of 60, while another feasible solution has value 40. Test the ratio-2 guarantee on each return separately. For minimization, use returned cost divided by optimum instead. A parameter close to 1 controls relative error but need not force equality of distinct objective values. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The value it returns is at least $\frac1\rho$ times the optimal value.
[x] If $\rho=2$, it always returns at least half of the optimal value.
[x] For a minimization problem, ratio $\rho$ would instead mean the returned value is at most $\rho$ times the optimum.
[ ] The value it returns is at most $\frac1\rho$ times the optimal value.
[ ] The value it returns always equals the optimal value whenever $\rho$ is close to 1.
---
慣例讓 $\rho\ge1$：最大化「至少是最佳值的 $\frac1\rho$」，$\rho=2$ 就是「至少一半」；最小化則是「不超過 $\rho$ 倍」。「至多 $\frac1\rho$」把方向弄反了；$\rho$ 接近 1 只是很接近最佳，不保證相等。

@q np4-11 | al-np#4 | 3 | 計算
metric TSP 的 2-近似：MST 總長 30。這個方法得到的路線最多多長？最佳路線至少多長？
== 60,30
?? 演算法最多,最佳至少
---
最佳路線去掉一條邊就是生成樹，所以最佳 ≥ MST＝30；沿 MST 繞一圈（每邊兩次）是 60，抄捷徑（三角不等式）不會變長。

@q np4-12 | al-np#4 | 2 | 計算
set cover 的貪婪近似比約 $\ln n$。全集有 1000 個元素，最佳解用 10 個集合，貪婪最多大約用幾個？
== 69
== 70
---
$10\times\ln1000\approx10\times6.9$。近似比會隨規模慢慢變大。

@q np4-13 | al-np#4 | 3 | 證明
說明為什麼一般 TSP（不保證三角不等式）沒有常數近似比的多項式演算法（除非 P = NP）。
---
若有近似比 $\rho$ 的演算法，可以用它判斷漢米頓迴路：原圖的邊權重設 1，非邊設 $\rho n+1$。有漢米頓迴路時最佳路線是 $n$，演算法給出 $\le\rho n$，只能全用原圖的邊；沒有時任何路線都至少用一條非邊，$>\rho n$。看演算法的結果就知道答案，等於在多項式時間解了 NP-complete 問題。

@q np4-14 | al-np#4 | 2 | 找錯
For vertex cover, a student proposes the rule "repeatedly take a vertex of maximum degree and delete its edges", and claims that its approximation ratio is always better than 2. Which of the following statements are correct?
[x] The claim is false.
[x] On some graphs, this rule returns a cover $\Theta(\lg n)$ times larger than the optimum.
[ ] The rule always returns an optimal cover, since high-degree vertices cover the most edges.
[ ] The rule's approximation ratio is at most 2 on every graph.
[ ] The simple rule "take both endpoints of an uncovered edge" has no approximation guarantee.
---
挑度數最大的點聽起來聰明，但存在讓它「差到 $\Theta(\lg n)$ 倍」的圖，所以「說法錯」，連 2 都保證不了，更不是最佳。「挑一條沒被蓋住的邊、兩端都選」雖然笨，卻有 2 的保證。

倉庫要從 50 個候選地點挑最少的幾個蓋物流中心，讓每個客戶都在某個中心的服務範圍內。這是哪個問題？可以怎麼近似？
[x] set cover：每個候選點蓋住一群客戶，用「每次挑蓋住最多未服務客戶的」貪婪
[ ] 最短路線
[ ] 網路流量
[ ] 排序
---
貪婪保證不超過最佳的約 $\ln n$ 倍。

[x] 近似演算法有可以證明的誤差上限；啟發式通常表現不錯，但沒有保證
[ ] 兩者一樣
[ ] 啟發式一定比較準
[ ] 近似演算法沒有保證

例如 TSP 的最近鄰居法是啟發式；用 MST 的是 2-近似演算法。

@q gd4-15 | al-greedy#4 | 2 | 計算
分數背包容量 15，物品（重, 價）＝(4,20)、(6,42)、(5,15)、(8,48)。最大總價值？
== 95
---
單位價值 5、7、3、6。先拿 (6,42)、再拿 (8,48)，剩容量 1，拿 (4,20) 的 1/4 得 5。$42+48+5=95$。

== 90

(6,42)＋(8,48) 重 14、價 90，剩 1 放不下別的；其他組合例如 (4,20)＋(6,42)＋(5,15) 只有 77。分數版本多出的 5 來自切開的那一塊。

@q gd4-17 | al-greedy#4 | 3 | 陷阱
0/1 背包容量 10，物品 (1,2) 和 (10,10)。照單位價值貪婪得多少？最佳是多少？
== 2,10
?? 貪婪,最佳
---
(1,2) 比值 2 先拿，剩 9 放不下 (10,10)。只看比值的貪婪可以差到任意倍；改成「比值貪婪」和「最貴的單一物品」取較大者，就保證至少有最佳解的一半。

@q gd4-18 | al-greedy#4 | 2 | 陷阱
In fractional knapsack, two items have the same value per unit weight. Which of the following statements are correct?
[x] Which of the two is taken first does not change the final total value.
[x] Only items with different value-to-weight ratios need to be taken in a particular order.
[ ] The heavier of the two must be taken first.
[ ] The lighter of the two must be taken first.
[ ] The one with the higher total value must be taken first.
---
比值相同代表每拿一單位重量換到的價值一樣，把那段容量分給哪一件總價值都相同，「Which of the two…」、「Only items with different…」對；只有比值不同時順序才重要。先拿重的、輕的或總價值高的都一樣，「The heavier of the…」、「The lighter of the…」、「The one with the…」錯。

@q gd4-19 | al-greedy#4 | 2 | 計算

== 20
---
兩件 (3,9) 比值 3 全拿，剩 1 拿 (4,8) 的 1/4 得 2。

@q gd4-20 | al-greedy#4 | 2 | 比較
Which of the following statements about common algorithms for the fractional and 0/1 knapsack problems are correct?
[x] Fractional knapsack: sort by value per unit weight and take greedily, in $O(n\lg n)$ time.
[x] 0/1 knapsack: dynamic programming over the weight limits $0,\dots,W$, in $O(nW)$ time.
[x] $O(nW)$ is exponential in the number of bits needed to write $W$.
[ ] Both problems are solved in $O(n\lg n)$ time.
[x] The stated weight-table DP depends on the numeric W, while the stated fractional ratio-sorting method does not enumerate all capacities up to W.
---
「分數比值排序 n lg n」對：排序加一趟分配。「0/1 重量表 nW」對：狀態是物品前綴與可用容量。「隨 W 位數可指數展開」對：W 的位數約 lg W，但表逐數值容量建立。「兩個指定方法都 n lg n」錯：沒有對 W 的限制時，重量表不能一概如此界定。「一個枚舉容量一個不枚舉」對：這正是虛擬多項式成本的差異；0/1 的最佳化問題稱 NP-hard，相應的門檻決策版本才稱 NP-complete。

@q gd4-21 | al-greedy#4 | 3 | 證明
證明：分數背包存在一個最佳解，最多只有一件物品被切開（只拿一部分）。
---
若最佳解中有兩件物品 $i$、$j$ 都只拿一部分，設 $v_i/w_i\ge v_j/w_j$。把 $j$ 的一小段重量 $\epsilon$ 換成 $i$ 的 $\epsilon$（兩件都還沒拿滿，所以做得到），總重不變，價值變化 $\epsilon(v_i/w_i-v_j/w_j)\ge0$，仍是最佳。一直換到其中一件拿滿或拿光為止，被切開的物品數就減少。重複下去，最後最多剩一件被切開。

@q gd5-15 | al-greedy#5 | 3 | 計算
單位時間工作（截止, 罰金）：a(1,10)、b(2,40)、c(2,30)、d(3,20)、e(3,50)、f(4,15)。罰金由大到小、「還排得進去就加入」。準時完成哪些？總罰金？
== bcef,30
?? 準時的工作,罰金
---
依序 e、b、c 可以；d 加入後截止 ≤3 的有 4 個，放不下；f 可以；a 加入後截止 ≤2 的有 3 個，放不下。遲交 d、a，罰 $20+10=30$。

@q gd5-16 | al-greedy#5 | 2 | 追蹤
接上題，準時的 b、c、e、f 照截止時間排成時程（同截止先排 b）。第 3 個時段做誰？
== e
---
截止 2、2、3、4 依序排：b、c、e、f。照截止排序後，只要「截止 ≤ $t$ 的工作數 ≤ $t$」對每個 $t$ 都成立，每個工作都能準時。

@q gd5-17 | al-greedy#5 | 3 | 陷阱
On the path a–b–c–d, a set of edges counts as independent if no two of its edges share an endpoint. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] This system is not a matroid.
[x] With $A=\{ab,cd\}$ and $B=\{bc\}$, $|A|>|B|$, but no edge of $A$ can be added to $B$.
[ ] Every maximal independent set in this system has exactly 2 edges.
[ ] This system is a matroid only because the graph is a tree.
[ ] Whether it is a matroid depends on the edge weights.
---
「不是擬陣」對、「$A$ 的邊都加不進 $B$」對：$ab$ 和 $bc$ 共用 $b$，$cd$ 和 $bc$ 共用 $c$，交換性不成立。

「每個極大獨立集都恰有 2 條邊」錯：$\{bc\}$ 已經加不進任何邊，是極大的，卻只有 1 條邊。擬陣裡所有極大獨立集一樣大，這裡不一樣大，也說明它不是擬陣。

「因為是樹才是擬陣」錯：它不是擬陣；這個圖本身就是樹。

「看權重」錯：擬陣是集合系統本身的性質，跟權重無關。所以最大權重的「不共用端點」邊集合不能直接用擬陣貪婪，貪婪在這裡可能先挑 $bc$ 就卡住。

@q gd5-18 | al-greedy#5 | 2 | 計算
圖有 7 個點、分成 2 個連通塊。它的圖擬陣中，極大獨立集有幾條邊？
== 5
---
極大獨立集就是生成森林，每個連通塊 $k$ 個點用 $k-1$ 條邊，合計 $7-2$。

@q gd5-19 | al-greedy#5 | 1 | 計算
集合 {1,…,6}，元素 $i$ 的權重是 $i$。「大小 ≤ 3 的子集合都算獨立」。最大權重的獨立集總權重？
== 15
---
這是均勻擬陣，貪婪由大到小拿 6、5、4，拿到 3 個就滿了。

@q gd5-20 | al-greedy#5 | 2 | 觀念
Jobs each take one time unit and have deadlines. Which of the following statements about when a set of such jobs can all finish on time are correct?
[x] It is possible exactly when, for every $t$, the number of jobs in the set with deadline at most $t$ is at most $t$.
[x] When it is possible, running the set in increasing order of deadline meets every deadline.
[ ] It is enough that the number of jobs is at most the latest deadline.
[ ] It requires all deadlines to be distinct.
[ ] It requires the total penalty to be at most $t$.
---
截止時間 $\le t$ 的工作都要在前 $t$ 個時段做完，數量不能超過 $t$；條件成立時照截止時間排就都準時，「It is possible exactly…」、「When it is possible,…」對。只看總數不夠：截止時間 1、1、3 的三個工作，總數 3 不超過 3，但兩個都要在時段 1 完成；截止時間可以重複（1、2、2 就排得完）；罰金跟能不能準時無關。這個條件讓「可準時的工作集合」形成擬陣。

@q gd5-21 | al-greedy#5 | 2 | 比較
On a graphic matroid, a greedy algorithm considers the edges in decreasing order of weight and adds an edge whenever it creates no cycle. Which of the following statements are correct?
[x] It returns a spanning forest of maximum total weight.
[x] In a connected graph, the result is a spanning tree.
[x] Negating all weights turns it into an algorithm for a minimum-weight spanning tree.
[ ] It returns a spanning forest of minimum total weight.
[ ] It returns a longest path.
---
擬陣貪婪由大到小找到權重最大的極大獨立集，也就是每個連通塊一棵樹的生成森林，連通圖時就是生成樹，「It returns a spanning forest of maximum…」、「In a connected graph,…」對。把權重取負就變成找最小的（Kruskal），「Negating all weights turns…」對。

@q sp4-17 | al-sp#4 | 3 | 計算
Johnson：a→b 4、b→c −3、c→a 2、a→c 5。加新點 $q$（到每點權重 0）跑 Bellman-Ford 得 $h$。$h(a),h(b),h(c)$ 和重新加權後的 $\hat w(a,c)$？
== -1,0,-3,7
?? h(a),h(b),h(c),ŵ(a,c)
---
$h(c)=-3$（q→b→c）、$h(a)=h(c)+2=-1$、$h(b)=0$。$\hat w(a,c)=5+h(a)-h(c)=5-1+3=7$。

@q sp4-18 | al-sp#4 | 2 | 情境
圖有 $10^4$ 個點、$3\times10^4$ 條邊，有負邊但沒有負環，要所有點對之間的距離。用 Johnson，還是從每個點各跑一次 Bellman-Ford？
[x] Johnson：$O(VE\lg V)$ 約 $4\times10^9$；每點各跑 Bellman-Ford 是 $O(V^2E)=3\times10^{12}$
[ ] 每點各跑 Bellman-Ford，因為有負邊
[ ] 兩者一樣
[ ] 都不能用
---
Floyd–Warshall 的 $O(V^3)=10^{12}$ 也慢很多。稀疏圖用 Johnson，稠密圖（$E\approx V^2$）兩者差不多，Floyd–Warshall 較好寫。

@q sp4-19 | al-sp#4 | 3 | 陷阱

[x] 不會：沿著環走一圈，每個 $h(v)$ 一加一減剛好抵消

[ ] 會變成負的
---

@q sp4-20 | al-sp#4 | 2 | 計算
一條從 $s$ 到 $t$ 的路徑原權重 10，$h(s)=-3$、$h(t)=-7$。重新加權後這條路徑的權重？
== 14
---
$\hat w(p)=w(p)+h(s)-h(t)=10-3+7$。中間點的 $h$ 都抵消了，所以同一對點的所有路徑都差同一個常數。

@q sp4-21 | al-sp#4 | 2 | 找錯
A student implements Johnson's algorithm but skips Bellman–Ford, simply setting $h(v)=0$ for every vertex. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] With $h\equiv0$, reweighting changes no weight, so the negative edges remain.
[x] Dijkstra's algorithm may then give wrong distances.
[ ] The results are the same as the correct version; only the running time changes.
[x] $h$ must be the shortest distances from the added vertex $q$ so that every reweighted edge is nonnegative.
[ ] The real problem is that positive-weight cycles are no longer detected.
---
「權重完全不變」對：$w+0-0=w$。

「Dijkstra 可能算錯」對：負邊還在。

「結果一樣」錯。

「$h$ 要是從 $q$ 的最短距離」對：才能保證 $w(u,v)+h(u)-h(v)\ge0$（三角不等式）。

「偵測不到正環」錯：正環本來就不用偵測。

@q np3-16 | al-np#3 | 2 | 比較
「最小切割」和「最大切割」（把點分兩邊，使跨兩邊的邊數最少／最多），各是？
[x] 最小切割在 P；最大切割是 NP-complete
[ ] 兩者都在 P
[ ] 兩者都 NP-complete
[ ] 最小切割 NP-complete、最大切割在 P
---
最小切割可以用最大流在多項式時間求出。又一組「最小容易、最大困難」的例子。

@q np3-17 | al-np#3 | 3 | 陷阱
「找一張圖裡有沒有 3 個點兩兩相鄰（三角形）是 NP-complete，因為團（clique）問題是 NP-complete。」對嗎？
[ ] 對
[x] 不對
---
大小固定為 3 時，檢查所有 $\binom n3$ 組只要 $O(n^3)$，在 P。團問題困難是因為大小 $k$ 是輸入的一部分，$\binom nk$ 會變成指數。

@q np3-18 | al-np#3 | 3 | 比較
Consider an undirected path with vertices 1,2,3,4 and edges joining consecutive numbers. Compare choosing edges that touch every vertex with choosing vertices that touch every edge. Find a largest collection of edges sharing no endpoint, and use it to check the minimum edge-cover formula. In a separate trial add vertex 5 without any incident edge. General complexity claims concern unrestricted input graphs, and assume P differs from NP. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] Finding a minimum edge cover is in P.
[x] Finding a minimum vertex cover is NP-hard.
[x] In a graph with no isolated vertices, the minimum edge cover size equals $|V|$ minus the largest number of edges no two of which share a vertex.
[x] A graph with an isolated vertex has no edge cover at all.
[ ] Both problems are NP-hard, since their definitions are symmetric.
---
邊覆蓋可由最大匹配推出：取最大匹配，剩下沒碰到的點各補一條邊，大小是「$|V|$ 減最大匹配」，「在 P」；「點覆蓋是 NP-hard」。孤立點沒有任何邊能碰到它，「根本沒有邊覆蓋」。定義看起來對稱，難度完全不同。

@q np3-19 | al-np#3 | 2 | 情境
An organization has supervisor edges (1,2), (1,3), (2,4), (2,5), forming a tree rooted at employee 1. Every invitation has equal value, and a person cannot attend with their direct supervisor. For each subtree compare the best invitation count with its root included and excluded; restrictions concern direct edges, not every ancestor. Use the five-person example to inspect the recurrence before comparing tree inputs with unrestricted organization graphs. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] This asks for a largest set of tree nodes with no two of them adjacent.
[x] It can be solved in $O(n)$ time by dynamic programming on the tree.
[x] For each node, the DP keeps the best value with that node invited and with it not invited.
[x] Restricting the input to trees can make a problem that is NP-hard on general graphs easy.
[x] The same problem on general graphs is NP-hard.
---
「不能和直屬主管同時出席」就是「樹上相鄰的兩點不能同選」；對每個點記「選它／不選它」的最佳值，由下往上合併，「$O(n)$」。這問題在「一般圖上是 NP-hard」，但「限制成樹」就容易。五句都對。

@q np3-20 | al-np#3 | 3 | 觀念

[x] 4 色：答案永遠是「能」（四色定理），判定是常數時間；3 色：即使限制在平面圖也是 NP-complete
[ ] 兩者都 NP-complete
[ ] 兩者都在 P
[ ] 4 色比 3 色難

「判定」容易不代表「找出著色」一樣簡單，但平面圖 4 著色也有多項式演算法。

@q np3-21 | al-np#3 | 2 | 計算

@q rc3-15 | al-recurrence#3 | 3 | 計算
For powers of two, let $T(n)=T(n/4)+T(n/2)+n^2$ with constant positive base cost at sizes at most one. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The first fully expanded child depth contributes $5n^2/16$ nonrecursive work.
[x] The tight total solution is $\Theta(n^2)$.
[ ] The first child depth contributes $5n^2/8$ nonrecursive work.
[ ] Every internal depth contributes n squared until the longest path ends.
[ ] Two recursive calls force a further logarithmic factor in the tight solution.
---
「五 n²/16」對：(n/4)²＋(n/2)²＝n²/16＋n²/4。「平方」對：每層內部總成本至多乘 5/16，和受根固定倍數控制，基底數 O(n)，根本身給 n²。「五 n²/8」錯：平方後分母應十六與四。「每層同根」錯：第一層已大幅下降。「兩孩子必多對數」錯：只有適當等量層成本才出現那種因子，不能只看呼叫數。

@q rc3-16 | al-recurrence#3 | 3 | 計算
For powers of two, let $T(n)=2T(n/4)+T(n/2)+n$ with constant positive base cost at sizes at most one. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The child sizes sum to n.
[x] The tight solution is $\Theta(n\lg n)$.
[ ] The child sizes sum to $3n/4$ because there are only two distinct size expressions.
[ ] Repeatedly choosing a quarter-size child gives the longest path.
[ ] Every late internal depth must remain full even after quarter-size branches reach the base.
---
「合計 n」對：兩個 n/4 再加 n/2，不能漏掉係數二。「n lg n」對：短路徑約 log₄n、長約 log₂n，完整前段給對數層的 n 下界，每層至多 n 給上界，基底成本 O(n)。「三 n/4」錯：漏算第二個四分之一。「四分之一最長」錯：它縮得較快，最長一直選二分之一。「後段永遠完整」錯：短分支先停，不能把每層都當滿樹。

@q rc3-17 | al-recurrence#3 | 3 | 陷阱
Consider $T(n)=2T(2n/3)+n$ on sizes $n=(3/2)^k$, with $T(1)=1$. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The immediate children's total size is $4n/3$.
[x] The tight solution is $\Theta(n^{\log_{3/2}2})$.
[ ] The internal work remains n at every depth.
[ ] The growth power is $\log_2(3/2)$.
[ ] The tight solution is $\Theta(n\lg n)$ solely because there are two equal-sized children.
---
「四 n/3」對：兩個各 2n/3。「log₍₃/₂₎2 次方」對：規模每次除 3/2，深度 log₍₃/₂₎n，基底數為二的該次方，約 n¹·⁷¹。「每層 n」錯：每層線性成本乘 4/3，會增加。「log₂(3/2)」錯：把換底角色顛倒，這是正確次方的倒數。「兩孩子就 n lg n」錯：還得看每個孩子的縮小比例，這裡比二分之一更大。

@q rc3-18 | al-recurrence#3 | 2 | 計算
$T(n)=T(n/4)+T(3n/4)+n$，$n=4^{10}$。遞迴樹最短的那條路徑有幾層？
== 10
---
最短路徑一直走 $n/4$，$\log_4 n=10$ 層；這之前每層總和都是完整的 $n$。最長路徑走 $3n/4$，有 $\log_{4/3}n$ 層。

@q rc3-19 | al-recurrence#3 | 3 | 證明
用代入法證明 $T(n)=T(n/4)+T(n/2)+n$ 是 $O(n)$。
---
假設對較小的 $m$ 有 $T(m)\le cm$。則 $T(n)\le c\frac n4+c\frac n2+n=(\frac{3c}4+1)n$。只要 $\frac{3c}4+1\le c$，即 $c\ge4$，就有 $T(n)\le cn$。再取 $c$ 夠大讓小的初始值也成立即可。

@q rc3-20 | al-recurrence#3 | 2 | 情境
某排序演算法每次都把資料切成 1:9 兩份再各自遞迴（切割花 $\Theta(n)$），時間是？
[x] $\Theta(n\lg n)$：$T(n)=T(n/10)+T(9n/10)+n$，每層總和 $\le n$，約 $\log_{10/9}n$ 層
[ ] $\Theta(n^2)$
[ ] $\Theta(n)$
[ ] $\Theta(n\lg^2n)$
---
只要每次都切成固定比例（再不平均），深度仍是 $O(\lg n)$；比例越偏，常數越大。

@q rc4-16 | al-recurrence#4 | 2 | 計算
$T(n)=2T(n-1)+n$ 的解是？
[x] $\Theta(2^n)$
[ ] $\Theta(n2^n)$
[ ] $\Theta(n^2)$
[ ] $\Theta(n\lg n)$
---
展開 $\sum_{i}2^i(n-i)$，主導項是 $2^n\sum_j j/2^j$，後者收斂到常數。

@q rc4-17 | al-recurrence#4 | 3 | 計算
Let $T(0)=T(1)=1$ and $T(n)=T(n-1)+T(n-2)+1$ for n at least two, with no reuse of previously computed calls. Define Fibonacci numbers by $F_0=0,F_1=1$. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] $T(n)=2F_{n+1}-1$.
[x] $T(5)=15$.
[x] The tight growth is $\Theta(\phi^n)$ for $\phi=(1+\sqrt5)/2$.
[ ] The recurrence has tight growth $\Theta(2^n)$ simply because there are two recursive calls.
[ ] Saving previously computed values would leave the number of distinct input indices exponential.
---
「二倍費氏減一」對：T(n)＋1 遵循費氏遞迴，兩個起點都是二，符合費氏數列的兩倍形式。「十五」對：第六個費氏數為八，2×8−1＝15。「φ 指數」對：費氏數列以約 1.618 的底數成長。「緊界二的 n 次方」錯：2ⁿ 是可用上界，但兩子問題分別少一與少二，不是完整等深二元樹。「不同索引仍指數多」錯：只有零到 n 共 n＋1 種索引，共用結果可以避免重複展開。

@q rc4-18 | al-recurrence#4 | 3 | 計算
For powers of two n, let $T(n)=T(n/2)+\lg n$ and $T(1)=1$. Write $L=\lg n$. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The nonbase costs sum to $L(L+1)/2$.
[x] $T(16)=11$.
[x] The solution is $\Theta((\lg n)^2)$.
[ ] There are $n/2$ nonbase calls for every power-of-two input.
[ ] The nonbase costs form the geometric series $L+L/2+L/4+\cdots$.
---
「等差和」對：對半規模的對數依次 L、L−1、…、一。「十一」對：4＋3＋2＋1＋基底一。「平方對數」對：等差和最高次 L²。「n/2 呼叫」錯：實際 L 次，例如十六只有四次，不是八次。「幾何」錯：那是反覆開根號時的對數成本；此處對半規模使對數每次減一。

@q rc4-20 | al-recurrence#4 | 2 | 計算

== 80

@q rc4-21 | al-recurrence#4 | 3 | 陷阱
Let $T(n)=T(\sqrt n)+\lg n$ and $T(2)=1$, evaluated at $n=2^{32}$. The recursion stops exactly at two. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The nonbase costs are 32,16,8,4,2.
[x] The total cost including the base is 63.
[ ] The total cost is 161 because every nonbase call costs 32.
[ ] The second recursive call's nonrecursive cost is again 32.
[ ] The decreasing local costs imply that the root's cost is irrelevant to the total.
---
「32 到 2 減半」對：每次對當前規模取對數，開根號令對數除二。「六十三」對：32＋16＋8＋4＋2＋1。「一百六十一」錯：把五層都當根的三十二再加基底。「下次仍三十二」錯：√(2³²)＝2¹⁶，下一個成本十六。「根不重要」錯：根三十二已約佔總和一半，正是幾何遞減總量由根階數控制的例子。

@q rc4-22 | al-recurrence#4 | 3 | 計算
Let $T(0)=0$ and $T(n)=T(n-1)+2^n$ for positive integer n. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] $T(n)=2^{n+1}-2$.
[x] $T(5)=62$.
[x] The tight growth is $\Theta(2^n)$.
[ ] The tight growth is $\Theta(n2^n)$ because all n terms equal the largest one.
[ ] $T(5)=32$.

「二的 n＋1 次方減二」對：2＋4＋…＋2ⁿ 的幾何和。「六十二」對：2＋4＋8＋16＋32。「二的 n 次方」對：完整和仍在最大項的固定倍數內。「多乘 n」錯：每項並不都等於最大項，最大項乘項數只是較鬆上界。「三十二」錯：只留最後一次的成本。

@q gd1-17 | al-greedy#1 | 3 | 計算
4 個工作（工時, 權重）：(2,1)、(3,6)、(1,2)、(4,4)。單機依序做，要讓「權重×完成時間」的總和最小。最小值？
== 68
---
照「工時／權重」由小到大排：(3,6) 和 (1,2) 都是 0.5，接著 (4,4) 的 1、(2,1) 的 2。例如 (1,2)、(3,6)、(4,4)、(2,1)：完成時間 1、4、8、10，總和 $2+24+32+10=68$；同比值的兩個誰先都一樣。

@q gd1-18 | al-greedy#1 | 2 | 計算
工作（工時, 截止）：(2,3)、(1,2)、(3,7)、(2,5)。單機依序做，要讓「最大延遲」（完成時間－截止，取最大）最小。最小值？
== 1
---
照截止時間排（最早截止優先）：(1,2)、(2,3)、(2,5)、(3,7)，完成 1、3、5、8，延遲 −1、0、0、1。

@q gd1-19 | al-greedy#1 | 3 | 陷阱
A factory has one machine and three jobs, each given as (processing time, deadline): $(3,3)$, $(2,4)$, and $(2,5)$. Jobs run one at a time without interruption, starting at time 0, and a job is late if it finishes after its deadline; its lateness is its finishing time minus its deadline, or 0 if it is on time. The textbook proves that scheduling the jobs in earliest-deadline-first order minimizes the maximum lateness. The factory manager, however, pays a penalty for each late job, so he cares about the number of late jobs rather than the worst lateness, and he asks whether the same order is still the best choice. A student compares the deadline order with the other orders of the three jobs, writing down the finishing time of every job in each order he tries, by hand. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] In deadline order, 2 jobs are late.
[x] Doing $(2,4)$ and $(2,5)$ first and $(3,3)$ last makes only 1 job late.
[x] So earliest deadline first does not always minimize the number of late jobs.
[ ] Earliest deadline first also minimizes the number of late jobs.
[ ] Earliest deadline first fails only when all processing times are equal.
---
照截止做：完成時間 3、5、7，後兩個遲交；先做兩個小的：完成時間 2、4、7，只有 $(3,3)$ 遲交，「In deadline order, 2…」、「Doing $(2,4)$ and $(2,5)$…」、「So earliest deadline first…」對。犧牲一個大工作能讓更多小工作準時；正確做法是照截止排，一旦會遲交就丟掉目前已排入中最長的工作（Moore–Hodgson）。不同的目標需要不同的貪婪規則，每一個都要各自證明。

@q gd1-20 | al-greedy#1 | 3 | 證明
用交換論證證明：照截止時間排序（最早截止優先）能讓最大延遲最小。
---
任何排程若有相鄰兩個工作 $i$ 在 $j$ 前面但 $d_i>d_j$（逆序），把它們交換。其他工作的完成時間不變；交換後 $j$ 提早完成，延遲不增加；$i$ 完成時間變成原本 $j$ 的完成時間 $f$，新延遲 $f-d_i<f-d_j$，不超過原本 $j$ 的延遲。所以最大延遲不會變大。一直交換到沒有逆序，就是照截止排序的排程，因此它不比任何最佳解差。

@q gd2-17 | al-greedy#2 | 2 | 計算
活動 (開始, 結束)：(1,3)、(2,4)、(3,6)、(5,7)、(6,8)、(7,10)、(9,11)。最多能選幾個互不衝突的活動（結束和開始相同不算衝突）？
== 4
---
最早結束：(1,3)、接著開始 ≥3 中最早結束的 (3,6)、再 (6,8)、再 (9,11)。

@q gd2-18 | al-greedy#2 | 2 | 計算
接上題，最少要刪掉幾個活動，剩下的才會互不衝突？
== 3
---
留下的最多 4 個，所以至少刪 $7-4$。「刪最少」和「留最多」是同一個問題。

@q gd2-19 | al-greedy#2 | 2 | 計算
活動 (1,4)、(2,6)、(3,5)、(5,8)、(6,9)、(7,10)，全部都要辦。最少幾間會議室？
== 3
---
同一時間最多 3 個活動重疊（例如時間 3.5 的前三個、時間 7.5 的後三個），所以至少 3 間；照開始時間依序放進有空的會議室就做得到。

@q gd2-20 | al-greedy#2 | 3 | 觀念
We place the fewest points on a line so that every given interval contains at least one point. Which of the following statements are correct?
[x] Pairwise non-overlapping intervals need distinct points, so the number of points is at least the maximum number of pairwise non-overlapping intervals.
[x] Greedy: repeatedly place a point at the right end of the earliest-ending interval that contains no point yet.
[x] Each greedy point comes from a different interval, and those intervals do not overlap, so the minimum number of points equals the maximum number of non-overlapping intervals.
[ ] The minimum number of points is always larger than the maximum number of non-overlapping intervals.
[ ] The two quantities are unrelated.
---
互不重疊的區間各需要不同的點，所以點數 ≥ 區間數，「Pairwise non-overlapping intervals need…」對。照結束時間貪婪，在「還沒被刺到的區間中最早結束」的結束點放一個點，放點時用到的那些區間彼此不重疊，所以點數剛好達到下界，兩者相等，貪婪放點規則與達到此下界的結論都成立。

@q gd6-17 | al-greedy#6 | 2 | 計算
面額 1、10、25，湊 30 元。貪婪用幾枚？最少幾枚？
== 6,3
?? 貪婪,最少
---
貪婪 25＋1×5＝6 枚；最佳 10×3。少了 5 元面額，美國硬幣那種貪婪就失效。

@q gd6-18 | al-greedy#6 | 2 | 計算
書桌只能放 2 本，依序要用 A B C A B C。替換「下次用到最晚」的書，和替換「最久沒用」的書，各要去書架拿幾次？
== 4,6
?? 下次最晚,最久沒用
---
下次最晚：A、B、C（丟 B）、A 命中、B（丟 A）、C 命中，共 4 次。最久沒用：每次都剛好丟掉下一本要用的，6 次全拿。

@q gd6-19 | al-greedy#6 | 3 | 觀念
We want to confirm that greedy change-making is optimal for coin denominations $c_1<c_2<\dots<c_k$ (with $c_1=1$). Which of the following statements are correct?
[x] Not every amount needs to be checked: if a counterexample exists, the smallest one is less than $c_{k-1}+c_k$.
[x] For denominations 1, 3, 4, checking up to 7 reveals the counterexample 6 (greedy 4+1+1, optimum 3+3).
[ ] Every positive integer must be checked.
[ ] Checking only the amount $c_k$ suffices.
[ ] Having a coin of value 1 guarantees that greedy is optimal.
---
已知的結果：若有反例，最小的反例小於 $c_{k-1}+c_k$，所以只要檢查到那裡，「Not every amount needs…」對、「every positive integer」錯。1、3、4 只要檢查到 7，就會在 6 發現反例，「For denominations 1, 3,…」對；只看 $c_k=4$ 本身看不到。有 1 元只保證湊得出來，不保證最少，「Having a coin of…」錯。

@q gd6-20 | al-greedy#6 | 3 | 計算
書桌放 3 本，依序要用 A B C D A B E A B C。替換「下次用到最晚」的書，總共要去書架拿幾次？
== 6
---
A、B、C 拿 3 次；D 時丟 C（下次最晚）；A、B 命中；E 時丟 D（之後不用）；A、B 命中；C 時再拿一次（丟 A、B、E 中下次最晚的）。共 6 次，這是任何替換方式的最少次數。

@q np4-17 | al-np#4 | 2 | 計算
路徑 a–b–c–d–e（邊 ab、bc、cd、de）。「挑一條還沒被蓋住的邊、兩端都加入」依序挑 ab、cd。得到的點覆蓋大小？最小點覆蓋大小？

?? 演算法,最佳

@q np4-18 | al-np#4 | 3 | 計算

@q np4-19 | al-np#4 | 3 | 觀念
A minimization scheme accepts an accuracy parameter epsilon. At epsilon=0.1 and optimum 100, its promised ratio is at most 1.1. Compare hypothetical runtime bounds n raised to the power 1/epsilon and n cubed divided by epsilon squared, distinguishing a fixed accuracy from an accuracy supplied as a varying parameter. The FPTAS statement about knapsack refers to its usual maximization version with the corresponding relative-value guarantee. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] For each fixed $\epsilon>0$, it runs in polynomial time with ratio at most $1+\epsilon$.
[x] Its running time may grow exponentially in $1/\epsilon$.
[x] An FPTAS also runs in time polynomial in $1/\epsilon$.
[x] Knapsack has an FPTAS.
[ ] Every PTAS finds an optimal solution in polynomial time once $\epsilon$ is small enough.
---
PTAS：「每個固定的 $\epsilon$ 都有多項式時間演算法」，保證 $(1+\epsilon)$ 倍以內，但「時間可以隨 $1/\epsilon$ 指數成長」。「FPTAS」更強，對 $n$ 和 $1/\epsilon$ 都是多項式，「背包就有」。PTAS 仍是近似，不保證最佳。

@q np4-20 | al-np#4 | 2 | 找錯
學生說：「近似比 2 的演算法，答案一定是最佳解的 2 倍。」錯在哪？
[x] 近似比是保證的上界：答案最多是最佳解的 2 倍，可能剛好等於最佳解
[ ] 沒錯
[ ] 應該是一半
[ ] 近似比跟最佳解無關
---
分析時常用「最佳解的下界」（例如匹配大小）來證明這個上界。

@q np4-21 | al-np#4 | 3 | 情境
0/1 背包要在多項式時間內得到「至少最佳解 99%」的答案。常見的做法是？
[x] 把每件物品的價值除以某個比例後取整數（縮小價值範圍），再用以價值為索引的表格法；縮小的比例由誤差決定
[ ] 用單位價值貪婪
[ ] 隨便選
[ ] 做不到
---
取整造成的誤差可以控制在 $\epsilon\cdot$OPT 以內，時間是 $n$ 和 $1/\epsilon$ 的多項式（FPTAS）。

@q sp6-17 | al-sp#6 | 2 | 計算
DAG：s→a、s→b、a→b、a→c、b→c、b→t、c→t。從 s 到 t 共有幾條不同的路徑？
== 5
---
照拓撲逆序算「到 t 的路徑數」：$c=1$、$b=c+1=2$、$a=b+c=3$、$s=a+b=5$。跟 DAG 最短路徑是同一種「照拓撲順序做 DP」的模式。

@q sp6-18 | al-sp#6 | 2 | 陷阱
A DAG has edges $s \to a$ (1), $s \to b$ (4), $b \to a$ ($-4$), and $a \to c$ (1). Single-source shortest paths from $s$ are computed. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] Since the graph is a DAG, it cannot contain a negative cycle, because it contains no cycle at all.
[x] Relaxing the outgoing edges of each vertex in topological order gives $\delta(s, c) = 1$.
[ ] Dijkstra's algorithm also reports $\delta(s, c) = 1$ on this graph, because the graph has no negative cycle.
[x] The topological-order method runs in $O(V + E)$ time even when some edge weights are negative.
[ ] Because of the negative edge, some shortest-path distances in this graph are undefined.
---
「DAG 沒有負環」對：連環都沒有。所以有負邊時最短路徑也一定有定義。

「拓撲順序鬆弛得 $\delta(s,c)=1$」對：拓撲順序 $s,b,a,c$：$b=4$、$a=\min(1,4-4)=0$、$c=0+1=1$。

「Dijkstra 也得 1」錯：Dijkstra 先取出 $a$（暫定 1）就定案，算出 $c=2$；之後取出 $b$ 才發現 $a$ 可以是 0，但已經來不及。沒有負環不代表 Dijkstra 能用，它要求的是**沒有負邊**。

「$O(V+E)$」對：拓撲排序加上每條邊鬆弛一次。

「有負邊所以距離沒有定義」錯：只有可達的負環才會讓距離沒有定義。

@q sp6-19 | al-sp#6 | 3 | 找錯
DFS is run on a DAG, recording each vertex's discovery time $d$ and finishing time $f$. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] Listing the vertices in decreasing order of $f$ gives a topological order.
[ ] Listing the vertices in increasing order of $f$ gives a topological order.
[x] For every edge $u\to v$, $u.f>v.f$.
[ ] Listing the vertices in increasing order of $d$ gives a topological order.
[x] For DAG shortest paths, relaxing the edges out of each vertex in increasing order of $f$ may leave some distances wrong.
---
「$f$ 由大到小是拓樸順序」對：每條邊 $u\to v$ 都有 $u.f>v.f$，所以 $f$ 大的排前面時，每條邊都從前面指向後面。

「$f$ 由小到大」錯：剛好反過來，會先處理到後面的點。

「每條邊 $u.f>v.f$」對：DFS 走到邊 $u\to v$ 時，$v$ 不是還沒發現（之後先完成）就是已完成；DAG 沒有後向邊，$v$ 不會還在進行中。

「$d$ 由小到大」錯：例：只有邊 $u\to v$，DFS 先從 $v$ 開始，$v.d<u.d$，但 $u$ 要排在前面。

「照 $f$ 由小到大鬆弛會錯」對：等於照反拓樸順序，處理 $v$ 的出邊時 $d[v]$ 可能還沒被前驅更新到。

@q sp6-20 | al-sp#6 | 2 | 計算
DAG：s→a 3、s→b 1、b→a 1、a→c 2、b→c 5、c→d −2、b→d 4。從 s 到 d 的最短距離？
== 2
---
拓撲順序 s、b、a、c、d。$d(b)=1$、$d(a)=\min(3,1+1)=2$、$d(c)=\min(2+2,1+5)=4$、$d(d)=\min(4-2,1+4)=2$。

@q np2-18 | al-np#2 | 3 | 計算
3-CNF-SAT 歸約到點覆蓋的標準構造：每個變數一條邊（$x$、$\lnot x$ 兩個點），每個子句一個三角形，三角形的點連到對應文字。3 個變數、2 個子句時，圖有幾個點、幾條邊？要找的覆蓋大小 $k$？
== 12,15,7
?? 點,邊,k
---
點 $2n+3m=6+6$；邊：變數 $n=3$、三角形 $3m=6$、連接 $3m=6$，共 15；$k=n+2m=7$。

@q np2-19 | al-np#2 | 2 | 情境
To reduce HAM-CYCLE (does a graph have a cycle through every vertex exactly once?) to the decision version of the traveling-salesman problem, give each pair of vertices distance 0 if they are adjacent in the original graph and distance 1 otherwise, and ask whether there is a tour of total length at most 0. Which of the following statements are correct?
?? 複選
[x] A tour of total length 0 exists exactly when the original graph has such a cycle.
[ ] The construction takes exponential time, since every possible tour must be listed.
[ ] The reduction shows that the traveling-salesman problem is easier than HAM-CYCLE.
[ ] The reduction works only if the distances satisfy the triangle inequality.
[ ] The reduction is wrong, because a tour of total length 0 can never exist.
---
總長 0 的環遊只能走距離 0 的邊，也就是原圖的邊，所以「存在 ⇔ 原圖有這種環」。構造只要填 $V\times V$ 的距離表，$O(V^2)$；歸約到旅行推銷員代表它至少一樣難；0、1 的距離不一定滿足三角不等式，歸約也不需要；原圖有這種環時就有總長 0 的環遊。

@q np2-20 | al-np#2 | 3 | 陷阱
有人把問題 A 轉換成問題 B，轉換結果的大小是原輸入的 $2^n$ 倍。這能證明 B 是 NP-hard 嗎？
[x] 不能：轉換本身要在多項式時間內完成，輸出大小也必須是多項式
[ ] 能，只要答案對應就好
[ ] 能，只要 A 是 NP-complete
[ ] 只要 B 在 NP 就能
---
指數大小的轉換什麼都證明不了：連 P 中的問題都能這樣「轉」成很多東西。

== 10
---

@q pb2-19 | al-prob#2 | 2 | 計算
Las Vegas 演算法每次嘗試成功機率 $\frac14$（獨立）。期望要試幾次？試超過 4 次（前 4 次都失敗）的機率？
== 4,0.316
== 4,81/256
?? 期望,超過4次
---

@q mf3-18 | al-mst-flow#3 | 2 | 計算
工人 1～4、工作 A～C。能做的：1–A、2–A、3–A、3–B、4–B、4–C。最大匹配大小？最小點覆蓋大小？
== 3,3
?? 最大匹配,最小點覆蓋
---
1、2、3 都會 A，三人中最多一人做 A：例如 1–A、3–B、4–C。點覆蓋 {A, B, C}（或 {A, 3, 4}）大小 3。二分圖中兩者相等（König）。

@q mf3-19 | al-mst-flow#3 | 2 | 情境
Workers are assigned to jobs with a maximum-flow network: $s\to$ each worker, worker $\to$ each job the worker can do, and each job $\to t$, all with limit 1. Now each worker may take up to 2 jobs, and each job still needs one worker. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] Set the limit of every edge from $s$ to a worker to 2, keeping all other limits at 1.
[ ] After this change, the maximum flow value can exceed the number of jobs.
[ ] Set the limit of every edge from a job to $t$ to 2.
[x] If some job $j$ instead needed two workers, setting the limit of $j\to t$ to 2 (and keeping worker-to-job edges at 1) would model it.
[x] The maximum flow value equals the largest number of (worker, job) assignments.
---
「$s$ 到工人改 2」對：上限放在哪條邊上，就是在限制哪一方的數量。

「最大流可以超過工作數」錯：每件工作到 $t$ 的上限仍是 1，流到 $t$ 的總量最多是工作數。

「工作到 $t$ 改 2」錯：那是讓一件工作由兩個人做。

「某件工作要兩人就改它到 $t$ 的邊」對：工人到工作仍是 1，同一人不會被算兩次。

「最大流＝最多的指派數」對：整數最大流有每條邊 0 或 1 的解，帶 1 的中間邊就是指派。

@q mf3-20 | al-mst-flow#3 | 3 | 陷阱
Which of the following statements about maximum matchings are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] In a general (non-bipartite) graph, the bipartite method of turning a matching problem into a maximum flow problem cannot be applied directly, because the vertices cannot be split into a side attached to $s$ and a side attached to $t$.
[x] A maximum matching in a general graph can still be found in polynomial time.
[x] In a cycle of 3 vertices, a maximum matching has only 1 edge.
[ ] No polynomial-time algorithm is known for maximum matching in general graphs.
[ ] Every graph with $n$ vertices has a matching with exactly $\lfloor n/2\rfloor$ edges.
---
「一般圖不能直接轉」對：二分圖的轉法靠「左邊接 $s$、右邊接 $t$、邊由左指向右」；一般圖的邊兩端沒有固定角色。

「一般圖仍是多項式時間」對：Edmonds 的花（blossom）演算法，處理奇數長度的環。

「三角形最大匹配 1 條」對：任兩條邊都共用一個點。奇數環正是一般圖比二分圖難的地方（二分圖沒有奇數環）。

「一般圖沒有已知的多項式解法」錯：見上。

「一定有 $\lfloor n/2\rfloor$ 條」錯：星形圖（一個中心連 $n-1$ 個點）最大匹配只有 1 條。

@q dc4-21 | al-dc#4 | 2 | 情境

== 5

@q dp1-21 | al-dp#1 | 3 | 觀念
A robot stands at one of the positions 0, 1, 2, 3, 4 on a line and may step one position left or right. A student defines the number of steps needed to reach position 0 as
```
f(0) = 0
f(i) = 1 + min(f(i - 1), f(i + 1))    for i = 1, 2, 3
f(4) = 1 + f(3)
```
and tries to compute it with dynamic programming. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
?? 複選
[x] No order of $i$ computes every $f(i)$ after all the entries that it reads.
[ ] Computing $f(1), f(2), f(3), f(4)$ in increasing order respects all dependencies, because each entry reads only smaller indices.
[ ] Filling $i = 1, 2, 3, 4$ once, starting from an all-zero array, yields $f(2) = 2$.
[ ] A memoized recursion for $f(1)$ terminates, because each call stores its answer before it calls anything else.
[ ] Adding a second parameter that the recurrence never uses removes the cyclic dependency.
---
「沒有任何順序可行」對：$f(1)$ 讀 $f(2)$，$f(2)$ 又讀 $f(1)$，互相依賴，不管先算誰都會讀到還沒算的格子。DP 的狀態依賴必須沒有環。

「遞增順序可行」錯：$f(i)$ 也讀 $f(i+1)$，不是只讀較小的。

「掃一次得 f(2)＝2」錯：程式跑出來是 0、1、1、1、2，$f(2)=1$，因為它讀到了還是 0 的 $f(3)$；正確值是 2。

「記憶化會結束」錯：答案要等呼叫完成才存，$f(1)\to f(2)\to f(1)\to\cdots$ 無限遞迴。

「加一個不用的參數」錯：依賴關係完全沒變。要真正換一種狀態定義（例如依步數一層一層往外算）才行。

@q dp4-19 | al-dp#4 | 3 | 觀念
A subset-sum table uses S[i][t] to indicate whether a prefix of i positive integers can sum to t. Begin with S[0][0] true and all positive targets false. For numbers 2, 3 and 7, trace targets from 0 through 5, using only the preceding row when adding an item. Also analyze the promised family T at most n squared. Decide which recurrence and complexity claims respect both 0/1 selection and encoded input size. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The promise $T\le n^2$ gives an $O(n^3)$ table bound.
[x] Without a numeric bound, binary T can make the table pseudo-polynomial.
[ ] Every numeric promise leaves the table nonpolynomial.
[x] Use $S[i-1][t]$ or $S[i-1][t-a_i]$ when the second index is nonnegative.
[x] The sample target 5 is reachable by taking 2 and 3.
---
「平方限制得立方」對。「一般二進位仍虛擬多項式」對。「任意限制也無用」錯：多項式數值界正好有用。「只用前列」對：分成不拿與拿一次，避免同件重複。「五可達」對：2＋3＝5。

@q mf2-21 | al-mst-flow#2 | 2 | 觀念
Edmonds–Karp is Ford–Fulkerson that always augments along a path with the fewest edges, found by BFS in the residual network. A student compares it with plain Ford–Fulkerson, which may choose any augmenting path, on networks with $V = 1000$ vertices, $E = 5000$ edges, and integer limits up to $10^6$. On one test network with 4 vertices, plain Ford–Fulkerson needs 2000000 augmentations when it keeps choosing paths through a middle edge of limit 1, while Edmonds–Karp needs only 2. The student wants to explain both counts. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The number of augmentations is $O(VE)$.
[x] The total running time is $O(VE^2)$, no matter how large the numbers on the edges are.
[ ] Each augmenting path found by BFS has the largest possible bottleneck.
[x] With integer edge values, Ford–Fulkerson with arbitrary augmenting paths may need as many augmentations as the value of the maximum flow.
[ ] Edmonds–Karp always needs only one augmentation.
---
「擴增 $O(VE)$ 次」對：最短擴增路徑的長度不會變短，每種長度下每條邊最多當 $O(V)$ 次瓶頸。

「$O(VE^2)$，跟數字大小無關」對：每次 BFS $O(E)$。

「每次瓶頸最大」錯：BFS 只管邊數最少，不管瓶頸大小。

「任意找可能要擴增最大流值那麼多次」對：例如每次只推 1 單位，容量很大時非常慢。

「只要一次」錯。

@q np1-19 | al-np#1 | 3 | 陷阱
「如果有人證明 P＝NP，那所有 NP-hard 的問題都能在多項式時間解。」對嗎？
[ ] 對
[x] 不對
---
NP-hard 只要求「至少跟 NP 中最難的一樣難」，可以比 NP 難得多，例如停機問題是 NP-hard 但根本不可判定。P＝NP 只會讓 NP-complete（在 NP 裡的那些）變成多項式。

@q np1-20 | al-np#1 | 2 | 計算
暴力檢查 50 個布林變數的所有真假指派，每秒檢查 $10^9$ 種。大約要幾天？
== 13
---
$2^{50}\approx1.13\times10^{15}$ 組，$\div10^9\approx1.13\times10^6$ 秒 $\approx13$ 天。每多 10 個變數就要乘上約 1000 倍。

@q np1-21 | al-np#1 | 2 | 計算
A delivery company stores a map of $n$ towns as an $n\times n$ table whose entry in row $i$ and column $j$ is 1 if a road joins towns $i$ and $j$, and 0 otherwise. A driver proposes an ordering of the towns, such as 3, 1, 4, 2 for $n = 4$, as a round trip that visits every town exactly once and returns to the start. Looking up one table entry takes constant time, and the company wants to know how expensive it is to check such a proposal, compared with finding a round trip from scratch. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
?? 複選
[x] The proposal can be checked in $O(n)$ time.
[ ] Checking the proposal takes $O(n!)$ time, since all orderings must be compared.
[ ] The proposal cannot be checked without first solving the problem.
[ ] Checking needs $O(n^2)$ time just to confirm that no town appears twice.
[ ] Easy checking would show that finding such a round trip is also easy.
---
用一個陣列記每個城鎮出現幾次、再查表看相鄰兩個（含頭尾）有沒有路，各 $O(n)$，所以「$O(n)$ 就能檢查」。不必和其他排列比，也不必先解問題；檢查重複用陣列記號只要 $O(n)$。容易驗證正是在 NP 的意思，和容易找到是兩回事。
