@q rc1-19 | al-recurrence#1 | 3 | 陷阱
$n^{1/\lg n}$ 隨 $n$ 怎麼成長？
[x] 是常數 2：$n^{1/\lg n}=2^{\lg n/\lg n}=2$
[ ] 像 $\sqrt n$
[ ] 像 $\lg n$
[ ] 趨近 1
---
看到變數同時出現在底數和指數，先改寫成 $2^{(\cdot)}$ 再比較。

@q rc1-20 | al-recurrence#1 | 2 | 陷阱
「$4^n=O(2^n)$。」對嗎？
[ ] 對
[x] 不對
---
$4^n/2^n=2^n\to\infty$，找不到常數 $c$。指數的底數不同就是不同的成長等級（對數的底數不同才只差常數）。

@q rc1-21 | al-recurrence#1 | 2 | 計算
要證明 $3n^3+2n^2+7=O(n^3)$，取 $c=4$。最小的 $n_0$（整數）是多少？
== 3
---
需要 $2n^2+7\le n^3$：$n=2$ 時 $15>8$ 不行；$n=3$ 時 $25\le27$ 可以，之後一直成立。

@q sp1-19 | al-sp#1 | 2 | 計算
s→a 1、s→b 1、a→c 1、b→c 1、c→t 1、a→t 3。s 到 t 有幾條不同的最短路徑？
== 2
---
最短距離 3：s→a→c→t、s→b→c→t。s→a→t 長 4。最短路徑不一定唯一。

@q sp1-20 | al-sp#1 | 3 | 陷阱
圖中有權重總和為 0 的環（沒有負環）。最短路徑還有定義嗎？
[x] 有：繞那個環不會變短，所以一定有一條簡單路徑也是最短的，最多 $V-1$ 條邊
[ ] 沒有定義
[ ] 最短距離是 $-\infty$
[ ] 只能用暴力
---
若最短路徑繞了零權重環，把環拿掉長度不變，重複拿到沒有環為止，就得到一條同樣短的簡單路徑。只有負環才讓距離無下界。

@q sp2-21 | al-sp#2 | 2 | 追蹤
Dijkstra 從 s：s→a 7、s→b 2、b→a 3、b→c 8、a→c 1、c→d 2、a→d 6。取出點的順序和 d 的距離？
== sbacd,8
?? 順序,d
---
s(0)、b(2)、a 由 b 更新成 5、c 由 a 更新成 6、d 由 c 更新成 8（a→d 是 11）。

@q sp2-22 | al-sp#2 | 3 | 比較
Dijkstra's algorithm is run on a graph with $V = 10^4$ vertices and $E = 10^6$ edges. Take $\lg V \approx 13.3$. Which of the following estimates and statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] With a binary heap, the running time is $O(E \lg V)$, about $1.3 \times 10^7$ basic steps.
[x] With a priority queue whose key updates cost $O(1)$ each and whose minimum removals cost $O(\lg V)$ each, when averaged over the whole run, the running time is $O(E + V \lg V)$, about $1.1 \times 10^6$ basic steps.
[x] With a plain array, the running time is $O(V^2)$, about $10^8$ basic steps.
[ ] With that priority queue with $O(1)$ average key updates, every single key update takes $O(1)$ time in the worst case.
[ ] In practice a binary heap is always slower than that priority queue with $O(1)$ average key updates on such graphs.
---
「二元堆積約 $1.3\times10^7$」對：每條邊可能一次更新鍵值，每次 $O(\lg V)$：$10^6\times13.3$。

「攤銷 $O(1)$ 更新的優先佇列約 $1.1\times10^6$」對：這種佇列之後會學到，就是 Fibonacci heap。更新鍵值攤銷 $O(1)$：$10^6+10^4\times13.3$。

「陣列約 $10^8$」對：$V^2=10^8$，這張圖不夠稠密，陣列最慢。

「每次更新最壞 $O(1)$」錯：是**攤銷** $O(1)$，偶爾一次會引發連鎖剪下。

「二元堆積實務上一定比較慢」錯：Fibonacci heap 常數大、指標多、對 cache 不友善，實務上二元堆積常常還比較快。漸近分析只說明 $E\gg V$ 時的趨勢。

@q sp3-20 | al-sp#3 | 3 | 陷阱
Bellman-Ford 如果每一輪剛好照「最短路徑上的邊的順序」鬆弛，要幾輪就收斂？
[x] 1 輪：路徑上的邊依序鬆弛一次，距離就一路傳到底（例如在 DAG 上照拓撲順序）
[ ] 一定要 $V-1$ 輪
[ ] 2 輪
[ ] 無法收斂
---
$V-1$ 是最壞情況；順序好的時候很快就不再變化，所以常加上「一輪沒有更新就提早結束」。

@q sp3-21 | al-sp#3 | 2 | 計算
Consider the difference constraints $x_1 - x_2 \le 3$, $x_2 - x_3 \le -2$, and $x_3 - x_1 \le -2$, where a constraint $x_i - x_j \le c$ becomes an edge from $j$ to $i$ with weight $c$. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The constraint graph contains the cycle $1 \to 3 \to 2 \to 1$ with total weight $-1$.
[x] The system has no solution.
[ ] The system has a solution, because each single constraint can be satisfied.
[x] Adding the three constraints gives $0 \le -1$, which shows directly that they cannot all hold.
[ ] If the third constraint were changed to $x_3 - x_1 \le -1$, the system would still have no solution.
---
「環 $1\to3\to2\to1$ 權重 $-1$」對：$x_3-x_1\le-2$ 是 $1\to3$($-2$)，$x_2-x_3\le-2$ 是 $3\to2$($-2$)，$x_1-x_2\le3$ 是 $2\to1$(3)，加起來 $-1$。

「無解」對、「三式相加得 $0\le-1$」對：負環上的約束相加，左邊全部抵消，右邊是負數。Bellman-Ford 偵測到負環就表示無解。

「每一條都能單獨滿足所以有解」錯：要同時滿足。

「改成 $\le-1$ 還是無解」錯：環的權重變成 0，沒有負環就有解，例如 $x_1=0$、$x_3=-1$、$x_2=-3$：$0-(-3)=3\le3$、$-3-(-1)=-2\le-2$、$-1-0=-1\le-1$。

@q dp1-22 | al-dp#1 | 3 | 計算
7 個人排成一列，每人戴或不戴帽子，但相鄰兩人不能都戴。有幾種戴法？
== 34
---
令 $f(n)$ 為 $n$ 人的戴法：最後一人不戴有 $f(n-1)$ 種，戴的話前一人不能戴，有 $f(n-2)$ 種。$f(1)=2$、$f(2)=3$，接著 5、8、13、21、34。

@q gd3-22 | al-greedy#3 | 3 | 陷阱
A binary prefix code is wanted for four characters. Which of the following statements are correct?
[x] No prefix code has codeword lengths 1, 2, 2, 3.
[x] $2^{-1}+2^{-2}+2^{-2}+2^{-3}=1.125>1$, which violates the Kraft inequality.
[x] A prefix code with codeword lengths 1, 2, 3, 3 exists.
[ ] Huffman's algorithm can produce codeword lengths 1, 2, 2, 3.
[ ] Whether lengths 1, 2, 2, 3 are possible depends on the character frequencies.
---
碼長 $\ell_i$ 的前綴碼存在 ⇔ $\sum2^{-\ell_i}\le1$。長度 1 的碼用掉一半的「空間」，兩個長度 2 用掉另一半，已經沒有位置給長度 3，「No prefix code has…」、「$2^{-1}+2^{-2}+2^{-2}+2^{-3}=1.125>1$, which violates the…」對，Huffman 也做不出來，跟頻率無關。1、2、3、3：$0.5+0.25+0.125+0.125=1$，例如 0、10、110、111，「A prefix code with…」對。

@q np1-22 | al-np#1 | 3 | 情境
A university schedules 300 classes into 40 rooms and 25 time slots, subject to rules such as "no teacher teaches two classes at once". You find a polynomial-time transformation that turns every class-scheduling instance into a Boolean formula that is satisfiable exactly when the schedule exists; for this university, the formula has about 300000 variables. A satisfiability program then decides the formula, and its answer is reported as the answer to the scheduling question. Your advisor asks what this transformation does and does not prove about the difficulty of scheduling. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] This shows that scheduling is no harder than formula satisfiability, so scheduling is in NP.
[x] This does not show that scheduling is hard.
[ ] This proves that scheduling is NP-hard, since formula satisfiability is NP-hard.
[ ] This proves that scheduling is in P, since the transformation runs in polynomial time.
[ ] This shows that formula satisfiability is no harder than scheduling.
---
排課 $\le_p$ 可滿足性，意思是「可滿足性會解 ⇒ 排課會解」：排課「不比它難」，而可滿足性在 NP，所以排課也在 NP；但這「不能說明排課難」，要反過來把已知難的問題轉成排課。轉換快不代表排課在 P，因為轉過去的問題本身難。方向反過來的說法也不成立。

@q np1-23 | al-np#1 | 2 | 比較
$n=10$ 時，$n^{100}$ 和 $2^n$ 哪個大？這說明什麼？
[x] $n^{100}=10^{100}$ 遠大於 $2^{10}=1024$；多項式時間在理論上「容易」，不代表對實際大小的輸入一定快
[ ] $2^n$ 比較大
[ ] 一樣大
[ ] 無法比較
---
要到 $n$ 大約 1000 以上，$2^n$ 才超過 $n^{100}$。但實際遇到的多項式演算法次方通常很小，所以 P 仍是「可行」的好近似。

@q dc1-21 | al-dc#1 | 2 | 計算
`[-2, 3, -1, 4, -6, 5, 2]` 的最大子陣列和？
== 7
---
候選：3、−1、4 和為 6；5、2 和為 7；從 3 一路到 2 也是 $6-6+7=7$。最大 7。

@q dc1-22 | al-dc#1 | 2 | 追蹤
分治求最大子陣列：`[2, -3, 4, -1 | 2, 1, -5, 3]` 從中間切開。跨過中線的最佳子陣列和？
== 6
---
左半「以中線結尾」的最大和：$-1$、$3$（4、−1）、$0$、$2$，取 3。右半「從中線開始」：2、3、−2、1，取 3。合起來 6（4、−1、2、1）。

@q dc1-23 | al-dc#1 | 2 | 比較
The maximum subarray problem can be solved by divide and conquer, which splits the array at its midpoint and combines the two halves with a scan across the middle, or by Kadane's scan, which goes from left to right remembering the best sum of a block ending at the current position and the best sum seen so far:
```
best = cur = a[0]
for i = 1 to n - 1:
    cur  = max(a[i], cur + a[i])
    best = max(best, cur)
```
A student runs both methods on arrays of $10^6$ numbers, including arrays whose entries are all negative, such as $[-3, -1, -2]$, for which the answer is $-1$, and compares their running times and their use of extra memory, and checks how the scan must be started to handle all-negative input. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The divide-and-conquer solution runs in $O(n\lg n)$ time.
[x] Kadane's scan runs in $O(n)$ time with $O(1)$ extra space.
[x] Both stated time bounds imply a looser $O(n^2)$ upper bound.
[ ] Divide and conquer is faster than Kadane's scan.
[x] Initializing the nonempty-subarray scan from the first input value lets it handle arrays whose entries are all negative.
---
「標準分治 n lg n」對：兩個半大子問題加線性跨中線掃描。「Kadane 線性常數額外空間」對：保留目前結尾最佳和全域最佳。「兩法平方上界」對：線性與 n lg n 都屬 O(n²)，並不表示緊界相同。「分治較快」錯：比較此兩個指定版本，線性掃描的漸近界較小。「全負也可」對：非空版本從首元素初始化，例如負五、負二、負七會回傳負二，不能錯設零代表空子陣列。

@q dc2-21 | al-dc#2 | 1 | 計算
$16\times16$ 的二維陣列相乘，Strassen 一路遞迴到 $1\times1$，總共做幾次純量乘法？一般方法呢？
== 2401,4096
?? Strassen,一般
---
每層 7 個子問題、遞迴 4 層：$7^4$；一般 $16^3$。

@q dc2-22 | al-dc#2 | 3 | 比較
Someone multiplies matrices by splitting them into $3\times3$ blocks using only 23 block multiplications, so $T(n)=23T(n/3)+\Theta(n^2)$. Which of the following statements are correct?
[x] Its running time is $\Theta(n^{\log_323})$, where $\log_323\approx2.854$.
[x] Since $\log_323$ is larger than Strassen's $\lg7\approx2.807$, the method is asymptotically slower.
[x] To beat Strassen, a $3\times3$ block scheme would need at most 21 block multiplications.
[ ] It is faster than Strassen because 23 is larger than 7.
[ ] Its running time is $\Theta(n^3)$.
---
比較這類演算法要看 $\log_b a$：$\log_323\approx2.854>2.807$，「Its running time is $\Theta(n^{\log_323})$,…」、「Since $\log_323$ is larger…」對、「faster」錯；$23<27$，也比 $\Theta(n^3)$ 快。$\log_321\approx2.771<2.807$、$\log_322\approx2.814>2.807$，要 21 次以下才贏，「To beat Strassen, a…」對。

@q dc2-23 | al-dc#2 | 3 | 計算
Karatsuba 乘兩個 1024 位數，切到 32 位時改用直式乘法（$32^2$ 次單位數相乘）。總共約幾次單位數相乘？
== 248832
---
$1024\to32$ 要遞迴 5 層，產生 $3^5=243$ 個 32 位的子問題，每個 1024 次：$243\times1024$。直式要 $1024^2\approx10^6$。

@q dc3-22 | al-dc#3 | 1 | 追蹤

@q dp2-24 | al-dp#2 | 2 | 計算

@q dp3-21 | al-dp#3 | 2 | 追蹤
ABAC 和 CABA 的 LCS 長度？從表格右下角倒推得到的一個 LCS 是？
== 3,ABA
?? 長度,LCS
---
倒推時遇到字元相同就往左上並記下字元，不同就往較大的那格走。這裡得到 ABA。最右下角的值就是長度，倒推只需要 $O(m+n)$ 步。

@q dp3-22 | al-dp#3 | 2 | 計算

== 40,800

$10^{10}\times4$ bytes＝40 GB；兩列 $2\times10^5\times4=8\times10^5$ bytes＝800 KB。只要長度不要字串時，兩列就夠。

@q dp3-23 | al-dp#3 | 3 | 觀念
想要 LCS 的**字串本身**、又只能用線性空間。Hirschberg 的做法是？
[x] 用兩列的方法分別從前往後、從後往前算，找出最佳路徑經過中間那一列的哪一格，再對左右兩半遞迴
[ ] 把整張表壓縮後存起來
[ ] 只存對角線
[ ] 做不到
---
時間仍是 $O(mn)$（每層總工作量減半，加總是常數倍），空間 $O(m+n)$。這是分治和 DP 的結合。

@q dp5-22 | al-dp#5 | 3 | 追蹤

@q rc2-21 | al-recurrence#2 | 2 | 計算
$T(n)=5T(n/2)+n^2$ 的解是？
[x] $\Theta(n^{\lg5})\approx\Theta(n^{2.32})$
[ ] $\Theta(n^2)$
[ ] $\Theta(n^2\lg n)$
[ ] $\Theta(n^{2.5})$
---
$n^{\log_25}$ 比 $n^2$ 大一個多項式倍數，Master 第一種情況，由葉子主導。

@q rc2-22 | al-recurrence#2 | 3 | 計算
For powers of three, let $T(n)=27T(n/3)+n^3$ and $T(1)=1$. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The comparison quantity $n^{\log_3 27}$ equals $n^3$.
[x] The tight solution is $\Theta(n^3\lg n)$.
[x] $T(9)=2187$.
[ ] The tight solution is $\Theta(n^{27})$ because there are 27 children.
[ ] Each successive internal depth has 27 times the previous depth's total nonrecursive work.
---
「比較量 n³」對：log₃27＝3。「n³ lg n」對：與本地成本同階，每層 n³，共對數層。「2187」對：T(3)＝27＋27＝54，T(9)＝27×54＋729＝2187。「n²⁷」錯：分支數要和縮小倍率一起取對數，不是直接作成長次方。「每層二十七倍」錯：每個子規模立方成本除 27，剛好抵銷分支倍數，整層成本不變。

@q rc2-23 | al-recurrence#2 | 3 | 計算
$T(n)=4T(n/2)+n^{2.5}$。屬於 Master 哪一種情況？解是？
[x] 第三種：$n^{2.5}$ 比 $n^{\lg4}=n^2$ 大一個多項式倍數，而且 $4(n/2)^{2.5}=\frac1{\sqrt2}n^{2.5}$ 滿足規則性條件，解 $\Theta(n^{2.5})$
[ ] 第一種，$\Theta(n^2)$
[ ] 第二種，$\Theta(n^2\lg n)$
[ ] 不能用 Master
---
第三種情況除了比大小，還要檢查 $af(n/b)\le cf(n)$（$c<1$）；多項式的 $f$ 通常自動滿足。

@q dp4-20 | al-dp#4 | 3 | 計算
0/1 背包 $n=100$。容量 $W$ 從 $10^6$ 變成 $10^{12}$，輸入大約多幾個位元？$O(nW)$ 的工作量變幾倍？
== 20,1000000
== 20,10^6
?? 多幾位元,變幾倍
---
寫下 $W$ 需要 $\lg W$ 位元：$\lg10^{12}-\lg10^6\approx40-20=20$。輸入只多 20 位元，工作量卻乘上 $10^6$——這就是「偽多項式」。

@q gd1-21 | al-greedy#1 | 3 | 陷阱
兩個工作（工時, 權重）：(10, 3)、(1, 2)，要讓「權重×完成時間」總和最小。「權重大的先做」和「工時／權重小的先做」各得多少？
== 52,35
?? 權重大先,比值小先
---
權重大先：(10,3) 先，$3\times10+2\times11=52$。比值小先：(1,2) 的比值 0.5 先，$2\times1+3\times11=35$。只看權重忽略了工時。

@q gd2-21 | al-greedy#2 | 3 | 陷阱
All activities must be held, and we want the minimum number of rooms. Activities are processed in some order, and each is put into any room that is free at its start time (a new room is opened only if none is free). Which of the following statements are correct?
[x] Processing in order of earliest finish time may use more rooms than necessary.
[x] Processing in order of earliest start time uses the minimum number of rooms.
[ ] Processing in order of earliest finish time is also optimal for this problem.
[ ] One room always suffices.
[ ] Processing in order of earliest finish time may leave some activity unscheduled.
---
反例：$[0,1)$、$[0,2)$、$[2,4)$、$[1,5)$ 照結束時間處理：前兩個開兩間，$[2,4)$ 放進第一間（1 點就空了），輪到 $[1,5)$ 時第一間到 4 點、第二間到 2 點都還在用，只好開第三間；但最多同時只有兩場，2 間就夠（程式隨機測試找到的例子）。照開始時間排，開新會議室時一定是因為所有會議室都正在用，所以間數等於最大同時場數，最佳。每場都會被安排，只是間數多；一間當然不一定夠。

@q gd2-22 | al-greedy#2 | 2 | 計算
區間 [1,3]、[2,5]、[4,6]、[7,8]、[6,9]（端點相同算重疊）。在數線上最少放幾個點，讓每個區間都至少包含一個點？
== 3
---
照結束時間：在 3 放點（蓋住 [1,3]、[2,5]）；下一個沒被蓋的 [4,6]，在 6 放點（也蓋住 [6,9]）；剩 [7,8]，在 8 放點。

@q gd6-22 | al-greedy#6 | 2 | 計算
書桌只放 2 本，依序要用 A B A C B A C。替換「下次用到最晚」的書，共要去書架拿幾次？
== 4
---
A、B 拿；A 命中；C 時 B 下次在第 5 個、A 在第 6 個，丟 A；B 命中；A 時丟 B（之後不再用）；C 命中。共 A、B、C、A 四次。

@q mf3-21 | al-mst-flow#3 | 2 | 計算

== 2

@q pb2-21 | al-prob#2 | 3 | 計算
Las Vegas 演算法期望時間 $T$。跑到 $2T$ 還沒結束就停下來隨便回答。用「非負隨機變數超過期望值 $c$ 倍的機率最多 $1/c$」估計，答錯的機率最多是？
== 0.5
== 1/2
---

@q sp1-21 | al-sp#1 | 3 | 陷阱
A delivery company models each road as an edge whose weight is its driving time in minutes. From the warehouse $s$ to a customer $t$, there is a direct road of weight 10 and another route $s\to a\to b\to t$ made of three roads of weight 3 each. The company then adds a fixed 2-minute handling charge to every road, so every edge weight grows by 2, and it later considers a second rule that doubles every road's time instead, for night driving. A manager assumes that neither change can affect which route is fastest, since every road is treated the same way. A student is asked to check the claim for both rules and to explain which kinds of routes lose the most from the added constant, using these two routes as an example. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] After adding 2 to every edge weight, the direct edge has weight 12 and the other path 15, so the shortest path changes to the direct edge.
[x] Before the change, the three-edge path, with total 9, is the shortest.
[ ] Adding the same constant to every edge never changes which path is shortest.
[x] Paths with more edges increase more when a constant is added to every edge.
[ ] Multiplying every edge weight by 2 could also change which path is shortest.
---
「加 2 後換成直接那條」對：每條邊加常數，邊數多的路徑加得多。

「原本三段的最短」對：$9<10$。

「加常數不會改變」錯：見上。這也是「把負邊都加到非負」不能直接用的原因。

「邊多的加得多」對。

「乘 2 也會改變」錯：每條路徑都乘 2，大小順序不變。

@q sp2-23 | al-sp#2 | 3 | 陷阱
有向圖 s→a 2、s→b 3、b→a −2。直接跑 Dijkstra，算出的 d(a) 是多少？真正的最短距離呢？
== 2,1
?? Dijkstra,真正
---
Dijkstra 先取出 a（2）就認定它確定了；之後取出 b（3），鬆弛 b→a 得 1，但 a 已經「完成」不會再處理，也不會影響它後面的點。負邊讓「先取出的距離最小」這個保證失效。

@q sp6-21 | al-sp#6 | 2 | 計算
DAG：s→a 3、s→b 2、a→c 4、b→c 6、c→t 1。s 到 t 的**最長**路徑長度？
== 9
---
照拓撲順序取最大：$d(a)=3$、$d(b)=2$、$d(c)=\max(7,8)=8$、$d(t)=9$。DAG 上最長路徑容易，一般圖上卻是 NP-hard。

@q sp6-22 | al-sp#6 | 2 | 觀念
A DAG has edges $s \to a$ (3), $s \to b$ (2), $a \to c$ (4), $b \to c$ (6), and $c \to t$ (1). We want the longest path from $s$ to $t$. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] Negating every edge weight and then computing shortest paths in the DAG gives the longest paths.
[x] The longest path from $s$ to $t$ has weight 9 and goes $s \to b \to c \to t$.
[x] Processing the vertices in topological order and relaxing with $\max$ instead of $\min$ also gives the longest paths.
[ ] In a general directed graph with cycles, negating the weights and running Bellman-Ford also gives longest simple paths.
[ ] The shortest path from $s$ to $t$ also has weight 9.
---
「取負號再求最短」對：DAG 沒有環，取負號不會產生負環，最短路徑仍有定義。

「最長是 9，走 $s\to b\to c\to t$」對：到 $c$ 取 $\max(3+4,\ 2+6)=8$，再加 1。

「拓撲順序用 $\max$ 鬆弛」對：跟取負號是同一件事。專案排程的關鍵路徑就是這樣算的。

「一般有環的圖也行」錯：原本的正權重環取負號後變成負環，最短路徑沒有定義；一般圖上的最長簡單路徑是 NP-hard。

「最短也是 9」錯：最短走 $s\to a\to c\to t$，$3+4+1=8$。

@q pb3-23 | al-prob#3 | 3 | 計算
Rabin–Karp（十進位、模 13）：視窗「314」的值是 $314\bmod13=2$。往右滑一格變成「141」，用滾動公式 $(10\times(2-3\times(100\bmod13))+1)\bmod13$ 算出的新值？
== 11

@q sp4-22 | al-sp#4 | 2 | 計算

@q np3-22 | al-np#3 | 3 | 比較
In a connected undirected graph, compare an ordinary minimum-weight spanning tree with a spanning tree whose maximum degree is 2. A four-vertex star with edges (1,2), (1,3), (1,4), all weight 1, supplies a small test: its only spanning tree retains every edge. Select all that apply.
[x] Finding a minimum-weight spanning tree is in P.
[x] A spanning tree in which every vertex has degree at most 2 is exactly a path through every vertex.
[x] Deciding whether a graph has a spanning tree with maximum degree 2 is NP-complete.
[x] Adding a seemingly harmless constraint can turn an easy problem into a hard one.
[ ] Deciding whether a spanning tree with maximum degree 2 exists is in P, since spanning trees are easy to find.
---

@q rc3-21 | al-recurrence#3 | 3 | 陷阱

@q mf3-23 | al-mst-flow#3 | 3 | 觀念
After a maximum matching of a bipartite graph with sides $L$ and $R$ is found by maximum flow, let $Z$ be the set of vertices reachable from $s$ in the final residual network. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The smallest number of vertices that touch every edge equals the size of a maximum matching.
[x] The set $(L \setminus Z) \cup (R \cap Z)$ touches every edge and has as many vertices as the maximum matching.
[ ] The set of all matched vertices in $L$ always touches every edge.
[x] In the graph with edges $u_1$–$v_1$ and $u_2$–$v_1$, a maximum matching has 1 edge, and the single vertex $v_1$ touches every edge.
[ ] In bipartite graphs, finding the smallest set of vertices that touches every edge has no known polynomial-time algorithm.
---
「最少點覆蓋＝最大匹配」對：König 定理。

「$(L\setminus Z)\cup(R\cap Z)$」對：這是從最小割讀出點覆蓋的標準做法；割的每條 $s\to u$、$v\to t$ 邊各對應一個點。

「左邊配到的點一定覆蓋所有邊」錯：例如 $u_1$–$v_1$、$u_2$–$v_1$ 中配 $u_1$–$v_1$，$u_1$ 碰不到 $u_2$–$v_1$。

「$v_1$ 一個點就夠」對：兩條邊都碰到 $v_1$，最大匹配也是 1。

「沒有多項式演算法」錯：二分圖上用最大流就能多項式時間求出；一般圖才是 NP-hard。

== 6

@q rc1-22 | al-recurrence#1 | 1 | 計算
$1^3+2^3+\cdots+n^3=\Theta(n^k)$，$k$ 是多少？
== 4
---
和是 $\left(\frac{n(n+1)}2\right)^2$，最高次 $n^4$。粗估：$n$ 項、最大一項 $n^3$，上界 $n^4$；後一半每項至少 $(n/2)^3$，下界也是 $n^4$ 等級。

@q rc1-23 | al-recurrence#1 | 2 | 找錯
學生說：「$n=O(n)$，所以 $n=o(n)$。」錯在哪？
[x] $o$ 要求比值趨近 0（嚴格比較慢），$n/n=1$ 不趨近 0
[ ] 沒錯
[ ] $n$ 不是 $O(n)$
[ ] $o$ 和 $O$ 意思一樣
---
$o$ 要求 $f/g\to0$（嚴格比較慢）。$n/n=1$ 不趨近 0，所以 $n\ne o(n)$。$O$ 像 $\le$、$o$ 像 $<$。

@q sp3-23 | al-sp#3 | 3 | 計算
差分約束：$x_1-x_2\le2$、$x_2-x_3\le-1$、$x_1-x_3\le0$。加一個虛擬起點連到每個變數（權重 0），用 Bellman-Ford 求出的一組解 $(x_1,x_2,x_3)$？
== 0,-1,0
---
約束 $x_j-x_i\le w$ 對應邊 $x_i\to x_j$ 權重 $w$：$x_2\to x_1$ 2、$x_3\to x_2$ $-1$、$x_3\to x_1$ 0。最短距離 $d(x_3)=0$、$d(x_2)=-1$、$d(x_1)=\min(0,1,0)=0$。代回三條都成立。

@q sp4-24 | al-sp#4 | 2 | 情境
Shortest paths between every pair of vertices are needed on a directed graph with $V$ vertices and $E$ edges. Johnson's algorithm first runs Bellman-Ford from a new vertex $q$ to get $h(v)$, reweights each edge as $w(u, v) + h(u) - h(v)$, and then runs Dijkstra's algorithm from every vertex. The target graphs have $V = 10^4$ vertices and $E = 5\times10^4$ edges, and some edges have negative weights such as $-3$. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] If all edge weights are already nonnegative, the Bellman-Ford step can be skipped, which is the same as taking $h(v) = 0$ for every $v$.
[x] With a binary heap, Johnson's algorithm takes $O(VE \lg V)$ time, which is much less than $V^3$ on sparse graphs.
[ ] Reweighting changes which path between two vertices is the shortest.
[x] If the graph has no negative cycle, every reweighted edge weight is nonnegative.
[ ] Johnson's algorithm still gives correct shortest paths when the graph contains a negative cycle.
---
「已經非負就不用 Bellman-Ford」對：重新加權只是為了讓 Dijkstra 能處理負邊；$h$ 全取 0 時權重不變。

「$O(VE\lg V)$、稀疏圖遠小於 $V^3$」對：Bellman-Ford 一次 $O(VE)$，加上 $V$ 次 Dijkstra 各 $O(E\lg V)$。$E$ 接近 $V$ 時約 $V^2\lg V$，遠小於 $V^3$（之後會學到的 Floyd–Warshall 就是 $O(V^3)$）。

「重新加權會改變最短路徑」錯：任一條 $u$ 到 $v$ 的路徑都加上同一個常數 $h(u)-h(v)$，誰最短不變。

「沒有負環時新權重都非負」對：$h$ 是最短距離，滿足 $h(v)\le h(u)+w(u,v)$，移項就是新權重 $\ge0$。

「有負環也正確」錯：有負環時最短距離沒有定義；Bellman-Ford 那一步會偵測到並回報。

@q dp1-23 | al-dp#1 | 2 | 計算
格子 $\left[\begin{smallmatrix}2&1&3\\4&1&2\\1&5&1\end{smallmatrix}\right]$，從左上走到右下，每步只能向右或向下，經過的數字總和最小是多少？
== 7
---
$d[i][j]=g[i][j]+\min(d[i-1][j],d[i][j-1])$。最佳路線 2→1→1→2→1。

@q dp1-24 | al-dp#1 | 2 | 計算
數字串用 A＝1、B＝2、…、Z＝26 解碼。"1226" 有幾種解法？
== 5
---
$f(i)$＝從第 $i$ 位開始的解法數：取一位就加 $f(i+1)$，前兩位在 10～26 之間再加 $f(i+2)$。依序得 1-2-2-6、12-2-6、1-22-6、1-2-26、12-26。

@q dp2-25 | al-dp#2 | 2 | 計算
CHARACTER 的最長回文子序列（不必連續）長度？
== 5
---
例如 C A R A C。$L[i][j]$：兩端字元相同就是 $L[i+1][j-1]+2$，否則取 $\max(L[i+1][j],L[i][j-1])$。也等於字串和自己反轉的 LCS。

@q dp2-26 | al-dp#2 | 2 | 計算
用 2、3、5 元硬幣（每種無限）湊 12 元，不計順序有幾種湊法？
== 5
---
外層跑硬幣種類、內層跑金額：$w[x]$＋＝$w[x-c]$。5 種：2×6、3×4、2×3＋3×2、2×2＋3＋5、2＋5×2。外層跑金額、內層跑硬幣會把順序不同的也算進去。

@q dp3-24 | al-dp#3 | 2 | 追蹤

@q mf1-23 | al-mst-flow#1 | 3 | 陷阱

== 16

@q sp1-22 | al-sp#1 | 1 | 計算
10 個點的圖沒有負環。任一條最短路徑（取簡單的那條）最多有幾條邊？
== 9

簡單路徑不重複經過點，最多經過 10 個點、9 條邊。這也是 Bellman-Ford 跑 $V-1$ 輪就夠的原因。


@q sp2-24 | al-sp#2 | 2 | 計算
無向圖：A–B 4、A–C 1、C–B 2、B–D 5、C–D 8、D–E 3、C–E 10。從 A 到 E 的最短距離？有幾條不同的最短路徑？
== 11,2
?? 距離,條數
---
Dijkstra 依序確定 A(0)、C(1)、B(3)、D(8)、E(11)。A–C–B–D–E $=1+2+5+3$、A–C–E $=1+10$，兩條都是 11。


@q sp2-25 | al-sp#2 | 3 | 比較
$A^*$ search repeatedly removes the vertex with the smallest $d(v) + h(v)$, where $d(v)$ is the current distance from the start and $h(v)$ estimates the distance from $v$ to the goal. All edge weights are nonnegative. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] With $h(v) = 0$ for every $v$, $A^*$ behaves exactly like Dijkstra's algorithm.
[x] If $h$ never overestimates and also satisfies $h(u) \le w(u, v) + h(v)$ for every edge, $A^*$ returns a shortest path to the goal.
[x] On a road map, the straight-line distance to the goal is a valid choice of $h$, because no road route is shorter than the straight line.
[ ] $h$ may be arbitrarily large without affecting whether the returned path is shortest.
[ ] $A^*$ always explores more vertices than Dijkstra's algorithm.
---
「$h=0$ 就是 Dijkstra」對：取出的順序完全一樣。

「不高估且滿足三角不等式就正確」對：這時重新定義的邊權 $w(u,v)+h(v)-h(u)\ge0$，$A^*$ 等於在新權重上跑 Dijkstra。

「直線距離可以當 $h$」對：任何道路路徑都不會比直線短，所以不會高估；地圖導航常這樣用。

「$h$ 可以任意大」錯：高估時可能太早認定某條較長的路，回傳的不是最短路徑。

「一定探索更多點」錯：$h$ 越接近真實距離，偏離目標方向的點越少被探索，通常比 Dijkstra 少。


@q sp5-94 | al-sp#5 | 2 | 追蹤
Floyd–Warshall：1→2 4、2→3 1、1→3 7、3→1 2。處理完中間點 $k=1$ 後的 $D[3][2]$，處理完 $k=2$ 後的 $D[1][3]$？
== 6,5
?? D[3][2],D[1][3]
---
$k=1$：$D[3][2]=D[3][1]+D[1][2]=2+4=6$。$k=2$：$D[1][3]=\min(7,D[1][2]+D[2][3])=\min(7,5)=5$。


@q sp6-23 | al-sp#6 | 2 | 計算
DAG：s→a −1、s→b 2、a→b 4、a→c 3、b→c −5。s 到 c 的最短距離？
== -3
---
拓撲順序 s、a、b、c：$d(a)=-1$、$d(b)=\min(2,3)=2$、$d(c)=\min(-1+3,2-5)=-3$。有負邊沒關係，DAG 不會有負環。


@q mf2-23 | al-mst-flow#2 | 3 | 陷阱
流量網路 s→a 5、s→b 4、a→b 2、a→t 3、b→t 6。割 $S=\{s,b\}$、$T=\{a,t\}$ 的容量？
== 11
---
只算從 $S$ 指到 $T$ 的邊：s→a 5、b→t 6。a→b 是從 $T$ 指向 $S$，不算。這個割比最小割（9）大。


@q np3-23 | al-np#3 | 3 | 比較
Compare two DAGs on vertices 1,2,3,4. The first has edges (1,2), (2,3), (3,4); the second has edges (1,2), (1,3), (2,4), (3,4). A Hamiltonian path must follow directed edges while visiting every vertex once. Examine topological orders and whether consecutive vertices are joined. Contrast these restricted inputs with the general directed Hamiltonian-path decision problem, and assume P differs from NP when distinguishing complexity classes. Which statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] On a DAG, the problem is in P.
[x] If such a path exists, it must follow a topological order of the DAG.
[ ] On a DAG, the problem is still NP-complete, since a DAG is a general directed graph.
[ ] If a DAG has several topological orders, one of them must be such a path.
[ ] No DAG has such a path, since a DAG contains no cycles.
---
路徑若存在，沿著它走每條邊都往前，「一定是拓撲順序」，而且此時拓撲順序唯一；算出任一個拓撲順序檢查相鄰兩點都有邊即可，$O(V+E)$，「在 P」。拓撲順序不唯一時一定有相鄰兩點沒有邊，路徑就不存在；像一條鏈的 DAG 就有這種路徑，沒有環不影響。

== 4.5

@q gd1-22 | al-greedy#1 | 3 | 計算

== 4,18,468

@q rc3-22 | al-recurrence#3 | 3 | 計算
$T(n)=T(n/2)+T(n/4)+T(n/8)+n$。用代入法證 $T(n)\le cn$，$c$ 最小要取多少？
== 8
---
$c\frac n2+c\frac n4+c\frac n8+n=\frac78cn+n\le cn$ ⇔ $c\ge8$。比例和 $\frac78<1$，所以是 $\Theta(n)$，常數是 $\frac1{1-7/8}$。

@q sp1-23 | al-sp#1 | 1 | 追蹤
跑完最短路徑後，記錄「從哪個點走過來」的陣列 $\pi$：$\pi[b]=s$、$\pi[c]=b$、$\pi[d]=c$、$\pi[a]=s$。s 到 d 的最短路徑？
== s b c d
== s,b,c,d
---
從 d 沿 $\pi$ 往回走：d←c←b←s，再反過來。所有 $\pi$ 指標合起來是一棵以 s 為根的最短路徑樹。

@q dc4-24 | al-dc#4 | 3 | 觀念
Let $p$ be an arrangement of $1,\dots,n$ (so $p(i)$ is the value at position $i$), and let $p^{-1}$ be its inverse arrangement ($p^{-1}(p(i))=i$). Which of the following statements are correct?
[x] $p$ and $p^{-1}$ have the same number of inversions.
[x] For $p=[3,1,2]$, $p^{-1}=[2,3,1]$, and each has 2 inversions.
[x] The inversion counts of $p$ and of $p$ written backwards add up to $n(n-1)/2$.
[ ] The inversion counts of $p$ and $p^{-1}$ always add up to $n(n-1)/2$.
[ ] $p^{-1}$ always has fewer inversions than $p$.
---
$(i,j)$ 是 $p$ 的逆序 ⇔ $(p(j),p(i))$ 是 $p^{-1}$ 的逆序，可以一個換一個地配對，所以一樣多，「$p$ and $p^{-1}$ have…」對、「The inversion counts of $p$ and $p^{-1}$…」、「$p^{-1}$ always has fewer…」錯。[3,1,2] 的反排列是 [2,3,1]，逆序對分別是 (3,1)、(3,2) 和 (2,1)、(3,1)，各 2 個，「For $p=[3,1,2]$, $p^{-1}=[2,3,1]$, and…」對。倒過來寫時每一對的順序都翻轉，逆序和順序互換，兩者加起來是 $\binom n2$，「The inversion counts of $p$ and of…」對。

== 12

例如 2, 2, 3, 3。允許相等時，tail 陣列的二分搜尋要找「第一個大於」而不是「第一個大於等於」。


@q gd1-24 | al-greedy#1 | 3 | 觀念
For the problem of choosing the most non-overlapping activities, the earliest-finish rule can be proved correct with a "greedy stays ahead" argument instead of an exchange argument. Let the greedy choices be $g_1, g_2, \ldots$ and those of an optimal solution be $o_1, o_2, \ldots$, both sorted by finish time; for example, with activities $[0,3)$, $[2,4)$, $[4,6)$, and $[3,8)$, greedy picks $[0,3)$ and then $[4,6)$. A student writes down the induction step and wonders what exactly the argument compares. Which of the following statements describe the "greedy stays ahead" argument for choosing the most non-overlapping activities by the earliest-finish rule? Select all that apply.
[x] For every $k$, the $k$-th activity chosen by greedy finishes no later than the $k$-th activity of any optimal solution.
[x] It follows that greedy chooses at least as many activities as an optimal solution.
[ ] Greedy chooses exactly the same activities as every optimal solution.
[ ] Greedy's first activity is the longest one.
[ ] The argument relies on the optimal solution being unique.
---
用歸納法證明貪婪的第 $k$ 個結束時間一直不晚於最佳解的第 $k$ 個；如果最佳解多一個活動，那個活動也能接在貪婪後面，矛盾，所以貪婪至少一樣多，「For every $k$, the…」、「It follows that greedy…」對。貪婪不必和最佳解選一樣的活動，最佳解也常常不唯一；第一個活動是最早結束的，不一定最長。這是交換論證之外，另一種常用的貪婪正確性證明。


@q gd2-24 | al-greedy#2 | 1 | 計算
區間 [0,3)、[1,4)、[2,5)、[3,6)、[4,7) 全部都要安排，最少要幾間會議室？
== 3
---
時間 2～3 之間 [0,3)、[1,4)、[2,5) 同時進行，最大重疊 3。


@q gd3-24 | al-greedy#3 | 1 | 追蹤
前綴碼 a=0、b=10、c=110、d=111。解碼 1100100111。
== cabad
---
從頭讀到一個完整的碼就輸出：110→c、0→a、10→b、0→a、111→d。前綴碼不會有歧義。


@q gd4-24 | al-greedy#4 | 2 | 陷阱
In 0/1 knapsack, suppose all items have the same weight. Which of the following statements are correct?
[x] Taking items in decreasing order of value is optimal.
[x] The weight limit only determines how many items fit.
[x] The difficulty of 0/1 knapsack comes from items that differ in both weight and value and cannot be divided.
[ ] Taking items in increasing order of value is optimal.
[ ] Dynamic programming is still required.
---
重量都相同時，容量只決定能拿幾件，拿價值最大的那幾件就對了，「Taking items in decreasing…」、「The weight limit only…」對。0/1 背包困難的根源是「重量和價值都不同，不能切」，「The difficulty of 0/1…」對；特殊情況下貪婪就夠了。


@q gd5-24 | al-greedy#5 | 1 | 計算
集合 {1,2,3,4} 上「大小 ≤2 的子集合都獨立」的均勻擬陣，有幾個極大獨立集？
== 6
---
基底就是大小剛好 2 的子集合：$\binom42$。所有基底一樣大，這是擬陣的性質。


@q gd6-25 | al-greedy#6 | 3 | 觀念
書桌只能放 $k=4$ 本書。線上的「丟最久沒用的書」和離線最佳（知道未來、丟下次用到最晚的）相比，最壞情況下去書架的次數最多差幾倍？
== 4
---
已知「丟最久沒用的」是 $k$-competitive：次數最多約是最佳的 $k$ 倍（另有一個常數項），$k=4$ 就是 4 倍。而且任何不知道未來的確定性策略都逃不掉這個倍數。


@q mf1-24 | al-mst-flow#1 | 3 | 情境
A graph has the edges $a$–$b$ (1), $b$–$c$ (2), $c$–$d$ (3), $d$–$a$ (4), and $a$–$c$ (5), and its minimum spanning tree $T = \{a\text{–}b,\ b\text{–}c,\ c\text{–}d\}$ has been computed. Then the weight of the edge $a$–$c$, which is not in $T$, is decreased. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] If the weight of $a$–$c$ becomes 1.5, the new minimum spanning tree is $\{a\text{–}b,\ a\text{–}c,\ c\text{–}d\}$ with total weight 5.5.
[x] If the weight of $a$–$c$ becomes 2.5, the minimum spanning tree does not change.
[x] In general, it suffices to find the heaviest edge on the tree path between the endpoints of the changed edge and to swap it out if it is heavier than the new weight, which takes $O(V)$ time.
[ ] In general, the minimum spanning tree has to be recomputed from scratch.
[ ] In general, it suffices to add the changed edge and remove any edge of the cycle that it creates.
---
把 $a$–$c$ 加進樹會形成唯一的環 $a$–$b$–$c$–$a$；依環性質，環上最重的邊不需要。

「變 1.5 時換掉 $b$–$c$」對：樹上 $a$ 到 $c$ 的路徑是 $a$–$b$(1)、$b$–$c$(2)，最重是 2 > 1.5，換掉。新樹 $1+1.5+3=5.5$。

「變 2.5 時不變」對：路徑上最重的是 2 < 2.5，$a$–$c$ 自己是環上最重的，不需要。

「找路徑上最重的邊、$O(V)$」對：在樹上找一條路徑最多走 $V$ 個點。

「一定要重算」錯：只改一條非樹邊，只會影響那一個環。

「隨便拿掉環上一條」錯：要拿掉最重的那條，否則總權重不是最小。例如變 1.5 時拿掉 $a$–$b$(1)，總重 6.5，比 5.5 大。


@q mf2-24 | al-mst-flow#2 | 2 | 觀念
A logistics team computes how many trucks per hour can travel from a depot $s$ to a port $t$ through a road network with a limit on each road. Partway through an algorithm, they have found a valid flow of value 7 on a map with 12 junctions, and separately, by inspecting the map, they have found an $s$-$t$ cut whose edges leaving the source side have total limit 9. Their manager wants to know what these two numbers already say about the maximum flow, and what would prove that a flow is maximum. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The maximum flow value is between 7 and 9, inclusive.
[ ] The maximum flow value must be 7.
[ ] The maximum flow value must be 8.
[x] If a flow and a cut with equal values are found, the flow is maximum and the cut is minimum.
[x] The value of every flow is at most the total limit of every cut.
---
「介於 7 和 9」對：最大流 $\ge$ 任何已知的流，又 $\le$ 任何割。

「一定是 7」、「一定是 8」錯：只知道範圍。

「值相等時兩者同時最佳」對：這就是演算法結束時的證明。

「任何流 $\le$ 任何割」對：每單位的流都要跨過割一次。


@q mf3-24 | al-mst-flow#3 | 2 | 計算
二分圖最大匹配用 Hopcroft–Karp，時間 $O(E\sqrt V)$。$V=10^4$、$E=10^5$，大約多少基本操作？
== 10000000
== 10^7
---
$10^5\times\sqrt{10^4}=10^5\times100$。比每次找一條擴增路徑的 $O(VE)=10^9$ 快很多。


@q np1-24 | al-np#1 | 1 | 觀念
要說明「能不能用 3 種顏色塗點、讓相鄰的點不同色」在 NP，需要提供什麼樣的證據（certificate），怎麼驗證？
[x] 證據是每個點塗的顏色；逐條邊檢查兩端顏色不同，$O(V+E)$
[ ] 證據是所有可能的塗法
[ ] 不能驗證
[ ] 要暴力試 $3^n$ 種
---
在 NP 的意思就是「答案是是時，有一個短證據能被快速驗證」。

6 個點、8 條邊的簡單圖，補圖（原本沒連的都連、原本連的都不連）有幾條邊？用來把獨立集轉成團。

@q np4-24 | al-np#4 | 2 | 計算

@q pb1-24 | al-prob#1 | 2 | 計算

== 4.47
== 161/36
---
$P(\max\le k)=(k/6)^2$，$P(\max=k)=\frac{2k-1}{36}$。$\sum k\frac{2k-1}{36}=\frac{161}{36}\approx4.47$。


@q pb2-24 | al-prob#2 | 3 | 計算

@q rc1-24 | al-recurrence#1 | 3 | 比較
Two algorithms for a puzzle run in time $f(n)=2^{(\lg n)^2}$ and $g(n)=n^{\lg\lg n}$ on inputs of size $n > 2$, with all logarithms base 2. Both expressions raise something to a power that contains a logarithm, and a student wonders whether they grow at the same rate. To compare them, the student rewrites each as a power of 2, using $n = 2^{\lg n}$, and also evaluates both at $n = 16$, where $\lg n = 4$ and $\lg\lg n = 2$, to see how far apart they are. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] $f(n)=n^{\lg n}$.
[x] $g(n)/f(n)$ tends to zero.
[ ] $f(n)=\Theta(g(n))$ because both expressions contain logarithms in a power.
[ ] At n equal to 16, f and g both equal 256.
[ ] $2^{(\lg n)^2}=n^2$ for every input.
---
「n 的 lg n 次方」對：2^((lg n)(lg n))＝(2^(lg n))^(lg n)。「g/f 趨零」對：寫 m＝lg n，兩者二進位指數分別 m² 與 m lg m，差距 m²−m lg m 趨無限。「含對數就同階」錯：要比較實際指數。「十六都二五六」錯：f＝16⁴＝65536，g＝16²＝256。「總是 n²」錯：lg n 不是固定二，不能把平方錯移到 n 外面。

[x] $\Theta(n^3\lg n)$
[ ] $\Theta(n^3)$
[ ] $\Theta(n^8)$
[ ] $\Theta(n^4)$
---
$n^{\log_28}=n^3$ 和 $f(n)$ 同階，Master 第二種情況。


@q rc3-23 | al-recurrence#3 | 1 | 計算
$T(n)=3T(n/3)+n$，$n=81$，遞迴到 $T(1)$。遞迴樹最底層有幾個葉子？
== 81
---
深度 $\log_381=4$，葉子 $3^4=81$。每層總成本都是 81，共 5 層（含葉子層），總和 $\Theta(n\lg n)$。

== 64

$T(2)=2$、$T(4)=4\times2=8$、$T(8)=8\times8=64$。一般 $T(2^k)=2^{k(k+1)/2}$，比任何多項式都快。


@q sp1-24 | al-sp#1 | 2 | 觀念
A directed graph has no negative cycle, and a shortest path from $s$ to $v$ passes through the vertex $u$. A student studies the optimal-substructure property that the shortest-path algorithms rely on, using the small graph with edges $s \to u$ (2), $u \to v$ (3), and $s \to v$ (6), and then asks whether the same property holds for longest simple paths, and whether a shortest path can ever contain a cycle of positive weight. The answers decide whether a dynamic-programming approach can be used for each problem. Which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The part of this path from $s$ to $u$ is a shortest path from $s$ to $u$.
[x] If the part from $s$ to $u$ were not shortest, replacing it by a shorter $s$-to-$u$ path would give a shorter $s$-to-$v$ path.
[ ] Every part of a longest simple path is also a longest simple path between its endpoints.
[x] In the graph with edges $s \to u$ (2), $u \to v$ (3), and $s \to v$ (6), the shortest path from $s$ to $v$ is $s \to u \to v$ with weight 5.
[ ] A shortest path may contain a cycle of positive total weight.
---
「$s$ 到 $u$ 那一段也是最短的」對、「否則換掉會更短」對：這就是最短路徑的最佳子結構，用「剪下貼上」的論證證明，也是 Dijkstra、Bellman-Ford 能逐步算的基礎。

「最長簡單路徑也有這個性質」錯：最長簡單路徑的子路徑換成更長的那段後，可能跟其他部分重複經過同一個點，不再是簡單路徑，論證失效。

「$s\to u\to v$ 權重 5」對：比直接走的 6 短。

「最短路徑可以含正權重的環」錯：把環拿掉會更短。


@q sp2-26 | al-sp#2 | 1 | 計算
Dijkstra 在 50 條邊的圖上，「把堆積中某點的距離調小」的操作最多做幾次？
== 50
---
每條邊最多被鬆弛一次（取出它的起點時），每次鬆弛最多一次 decrease-key。


@q sp3-24 | al-sp#3 | 2 | 觀念
Bellman-Ford 常加一個「這一輪都沒有任何距離改變就停」的條件。為什麼可以停？
[x] 沒有改變表示所有邊都滿足三角不等式，之後再跑也不會變；最短距離已經求出
[ ] 為了避免負環
[ ] 不能停，一定要跑滿 $V-1$ 輪
[ ] 因為圖是 DAG
---
這讓很多實際的圖只要幾輪就結束；最壞情況仍是 $V-1$ 輪。


@q sp5-95 | al-sp#5 | 2 | 觀念
After Floyd–Warshall finishes on a directed graph with $V$ vertices, where the table $D$ starts with $D[i][i] = 0$, which of the following statements are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] If $D[i][i] < 0$ for some $i$, the graph contains a negative cycle through $i$.
[x] If the graph has no negative cycle, then $D[i][i] = 0$ for every $i$ at the end.
[ ] If $D[i][j] = \infty$ for some $i$ and $j$, the graph contains a negative cycle.
[x] Floyd–Warshall runs in $O(V^3)$ time.
[ ] Floyd–Warshall cannot be used when some edge weights are negative.
---
「某個 $D[i][i]<0$ 就有負環」對：從 $i$ 出發繞回自己的總權重是負的。

「沒有負環時對角線都是 0」對：繞回自己的路徑權重都 $\ge0$，不會比初始的 0 更小。

「有 $\infty$ 就有負環」錯：$\infty$ 只代表 $i$ 走不到 $j$。

「$O(V^3)$」對：三層迴圈。

「不能有負邊」錯：負邊可以，只要沒有負環；那是 Dijkstra 的限制。


@q sp6-24 | al-sp#6 | 1 | 計算
專案排程中，某項工作最早可以在第 3 天開始，最晚在第 5 天開始也不會延誤整個專案。它的寬裕時間（slack）是幾天？
== 2
---
寬裕＝最晚開始－最早開始。寬裕為 0 的工作就在關鍵路徑上。

@q dp1-26 | al-dp#1 | 3 | 計算
有 2 顆一樣的蛋、36 層樓，要找出蛋從幾樓以上丟會破（最壞情況下）。最少要丟幾次？
== 8
---
令 $f(t)$＝丟 $t$ 次、2 顆蛋最多能判斷幾層：第一顆從第 $t$ 層丟，破了用第二顆從下往上試 $t-1$ 層，沒破還剩 $f(t-1)$。$f(t)=t+f(t-1)=\frac{t(t+1)}2$，$f(8)=36$。


@q dp4-24 | al-dp#4 | 2 | 情境
A knapsack instance has just 3 items: weight-value pairs (10,60), (20,100) and (30,120). Its weight-limit is 1000000000, and a literal full table is limited to 1000000 entries. Compare exhaustive subset enumeration with scanning every item-weight-limit pair. Then separately test the decreasing-value-per-weight rule on the same items with weight-limit 50, where choosing an item makes it unavailable thereafter. Which statements about exact algorithms and the proposed selection method are correct? Select all that apply.
[x] The simplest correct method is to try all $2^3 = 8$ subsets of the items.
[x] The $O(nW)$ table would have about $3 \times 10^9$ entries, which is impractical here.
[ ] The $O(nW)$ dynamic program is the only correct method for this instance.
[x] For $n$ items, trying all subsets takes $O(n \cdot 2^n)$ time, which is fine when $n$ is small.
[ ] Taking items in decreasing order of value per unit weight always gives the optimal answer.
---
「試全部 8 個子集合」對：每個子集合加總重量和價值，挑合法裡最大的。

「表格約 $3\times10^9$ 格，不實際」對：時間和記憶體都太大。

「只能用 $O(nW)$」錯：方法很多，要看哪個參數小；這裡 $n$ 很小。

「$O(n2^n)$，$n$ 小時沒問題」對：$n=3$ 只要 24 步。

「照單位價值貪心」錯：0/1 背包貪心不保證最佳；例如上限 50，物品 (10,60)、(20,100)、(30,120)，貪心選前兩個得 160，最佳是後兩個 220。
